\chapter{Session 2}


\section{Surah 2: The Chapter of the Heifer (Medina).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)

\noindent[\textbf{2:255}]
God, there is no god but He, the living, the self-subsistent. Slumber takes Him not, nor
sleep. His is what is in the heavens and what is in the earth. Who is it that intercedes with Him
save by His permission? He knows what is before them and what behind them, and they
comprehend not aught of His knowledge but of what He pleases. His throne extends over the
heavens and the earth, and it tires Him not to guard them both, for He is high and grand.

\noindent[\textbf{2:256}]
There is no compulsion in religion; the right way has been distinguished from the wrong,
and whoso disbelieves in Taghut and believes in God, he has got hold of the firm handle in
which is no breaking off; but God both hears and knows.

\noindent[\textbf{2:257}]
God is the patron of those who believe, He brings them forth from darkness into light.
But those who misbelieve, their patrons are Taghut, these bring them forth from light to
darkness,--- fellows of the Fire, they dwell therein for aye.

\section{Surah 2: The Chapter of the Heifer (Medina).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{2:285}]
The Apostle believes in what is sent down to him from his Lord, and the believers all
believe on God, and His angels, and His Books, and His apostles,--- we make no difference
between any of His apostles,--- they say, 'We hear and obey, Thy pardon, O Lord! for to Thee
our journey tends.

\section{Surah 4: The Chapter of Women (Medinah).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{4:163}]
Verily, we have inspired thee as we inspired Noah and the prophets after him, and as we
inspired Abraham, and Ishmael, and Jacob, and the tribes, and Jesus, and Job, and Jonas, and
Aaron, and Solomon; and to David did we give Psalms.

\noindent[\textbf{4:164}]
Of apostles we have already told thee of some before; and of apostles some we have not
told thee of;--- But Moses did God speak to, speaking;---

\noindent[\textbf{4:165}]
apostles giving glad tidings and warning, that men should have no argument against God,
after the apostles, for God is mighty, wise!

\noindent[\textbf{4:166}]
But God bears witness to what He has revealed to thee: He revealed it in His knowledge,
and the angels bear witness too; though God is witness enough.

\section{Surah 14: The Chapter of Abraham (Peace Be On Him!) (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{14:31}]
Say to my servants who believe, that they be steadfast in prayer and expend in alms of
what we have bestowed upon them in secret and in public, before there comes the day when
there shall be no buying and no friendship.

\noindent[\textbf{14:32}]
God it is who created the heavens and the earth; and sent down from the sky water, and
brought forth therewith fruits as a provision for you; and subjected to you the ships, to float
therein upon the sea at His bidding; and subjected for you the rivers;

\noindent[\textbf{14:33}]
and subjected for you the sun and the moon, constant both; and subjected for you the
night and the day;

\noindent[\textbf{14:34}]
and brought you of everything ye asked Him: but if ye try to number God's favours, ye
cannot count them;--- verily, man is very unjust and ungrateful.

\noindent[\textbf{14:35}]
And when Abraham said, 'My Lord, make this land safe, and turn me and my sons away
from serving idols!

\noindent[\textbf{14:36}]
'My Lord, verily, they have led many men astray; but he who follows me, verily, he is of
me; but he who rebels against me,--- verily, thou art pardoning, merciful!

\noindent[\textbf{14:37}]
'O our Lord! verily, I have made some of my seed dwell in a valley without corn, by thy
Sacred House. O our Lord! let them be steadfast in prayer and make the hearts of men yearn
towards them, and provide them with fruits, haply they may give thanks.

\noindent[\textbf{14:38}]
'O our Lord! verily, Thou knowest what we hide and what we publish; for naught is hid
from God in the earth or in the sky.

\noindent[\textbf{14:39}]
Praise to God who hath bestowed on me, notwithstanding my old age, Ishmael and
Isaac!--- verily, my Lord surely hears prayer.

\noindent[\textbf{14:40}]
'O my Lord! make me steadfast in prayer, and of my seed likewise! O our Lord! and
accept my prayer!

\noindent[\textbf{14:41}]
O our Lord! pardon me and my parents and the believers on the reckoning day!'

\section{Surah 26: The Chapter of the Poets (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{26:69}]
And recite to them the story of Abraham;

\noindent[\textbf{26:70}]
when he said to his father and his people, 'What do ye serve?'

\noindent[\textbf{26:71}]
They said, 'We serve idols, and we are still devoted to them.

\noindent[\textbf{26:72}]
He said, 'Can they hear you when ye call,

\noindent[\textbf{26:73}]
or profit you, or harm?'

\noindent[\textbf{26:74}]
They said, 'No; but we found our fathers doing thus.'

\noindent[\textbf{26:75}]
He said, 'Have ye considered what ye have been serving,

\noindent[\textbf{26:76}]
ye and your fathers before you?

\noindent[\textbf{26:77}]
Verily, they are foes to me, save only the Lord of the worlds,

\noindent[\textbf{26:78}]
who created me and guides me,

\noindent[\textbf{26:79}]
and who gives me food and drink.

\noindent[\textbf{26:80}]
And when I am sick He heals me;

\noindent[\textbf{26:81}]
He who will kill me, and then bring me to life;

\noindent[\textbf{26:82}]
and who I hope will forgive me my sins on the day of judgment!

\noindent[\textbf{26:83}]
Lord, grant me judgment, and let me reach the righteous;

\noindent[\textbf{26:84}]
and give me a tongue of good report amongst posterity;

\noindent[\textbf{26:85}]
and make me of the heirs of the paradise of pleasure;

\noindent[\textbf{26:86}]
and pardon my father, verily, he is of those who err;

\noindent[\textbf{26:87}]
and disgrace me not on the day when they are raised up again;

\noindent[\textbf{26:88}]
the day when wealth shall profit not, nor sons,

\noindent[\textbf{26:89}]
but only he who comes to God with a sound heart.

\noindent[\textbf{26:90}]
And paradise shall be brought near to the pious;

\noindent[\textbf{26:91}]
and hell shall be brought forth to those who go astray,

\noindent[\textbf{26:92}]
and it shall be said to them, "Where is what ye used to worship

\noindent[\textbf{26:93}]
beside God? can they help you, or get help themselves? "

\noindent[\textbf{26:94}]
And they shall fall headlong into it, they and those who have gone astray,

\noindent[\textbf{26:95}]
and the hosts of Iblis all together!

\noindent[\textbf{26:96}]
'They shall say, while they quarrel therein,

\noindent[\textbf{26:97}]
"By God! we were surely in an obvious error,

\noindent[\textbf{26:98}]
when we made you equal to the Lord of the worlds!

\noindent[\textbf{26:99}]
but it was only sinners who led us astray.

\noindent[\textbf{26:100}]
But we have no intercessors

\noindent[\textbf{26:101}]
and no warm friend;

\noindent[\textbf{26:102}]
but had we a turn we would be of the believers."' ---

\noindent[\textbf{26:103}]
Verily, in that is a sign, but most of them will never be believers;

\noindent[\textbf{26:104}]
and, verily, thy Lord He is mighty and merciful.

\section{Surah 6: The Chapter of Cattle (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{6:74}]
When Abraham said to his father Azar, 'Dost thou take idols for gods? verily, I see thee
and thy people in obvious error.'

\noindent[\textbf{6:75}]
Thus did we show Abraham the kingdom of heaven and of the earth, that he should be of
those who are sure.

\noindent[\textbf{6:76}]
And when the night overshadowed him he saw a star and said, 'This is my Lord;' but
when it set he said, 'I love not those that set.'

\noindent[\textbf{6:77}]
And when he saw the moon beginning to rise he said, 'This is my Lord;' but when it set
he said, 'If God my Lord guides me not I shall surely be of the people who err.'

\noindent[\textbf{6:78}]
And when he saw the sun beginning to rise he said, 'This is my Lord, this is greatest of
all;' but when it set he said, 'O my people! verily, I am clear of what ye associate with God;

\noindent[\textbf{6:79}]
verily, I have turned my face to him who originated the heaven and the earth, as a 'Hanif,
and I am not of the idolaters.'

\noindent[\textbf{6:80}]
And his people disputed with him;--- he said, 'Do ye dispute with me concerning God,
when He has guided me? but I fear not what ye associate with Him unless my Lord should wish
for anything. My Lord doth comprehend all things in His knowledge, will ye not then remember?

\noindent[\textbf{6:81}]
How should I fear what ye associate with Him, when ye yourselves fear not to associate
with God what He has sent down to you no power to do? Which then of the two sects is worthier
of belief, if indeed ye know?'

\noindent[\textbf{6:82}]
Those who believe and do not obscure their faith with wrong, they are those who shall
have security, and they are guided.

\section{Surah 21: Chapter of the Prophets (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{21:51}]
And we gave Abraham a right direction before; for about him we knew.

\noindent[\textbf{21:52}]
When he said to his father and to his people, 'What are these images to which ye pay
devotion?'

\noindent[\textbf{21:53}]
Said they, 'We found our fathers serving them.'

\noindent[\textbf{21:54}]
Said he, 'Both you and your fathers have been in obvious error.'

\noindent[\textbf{21:55}]
They said, 'Dost thou come to us with the truth, or art thou but of those who play?'

\noindent[\textbf{21:56}]
He said, 'Nay, but your Lord is Lord of the heavens and the earth, which He originated;
and I am of those who testify to this;

\noindent[\textbf{21:57}]
and, by God! I will plot against your idols after ye have turned and shown me your
backs!'

\noindent[\textbf{21:58}]
So he brake them all in pieces, except a large one they had; that haply they might refer it
to that.

\noindent[\textbf{21:59}]
Said they, 'Who has done this with our gods? verily, he is of the wrong-doers!'

\noindent[\textbf{21:60}]
They said, 'We heard a youth mention them who is called Abraham.'

\noindent[\textbf{21:61}]
Said they, 'Then bring him before the eyes of men; haply they will bear witness.'

\noindent[\textbf{21:62}]
Said they, 'Was it thou who did this to our gods, O Abraham?'

\noindent[\textbf{21:63}]
Said he, 'Nay, it was this largest of them; but ask them, if they can speak.'

\noindent[\textbf{21:64}]
Then they came to themselves and said, 'Verily, ye are the wrong-doers.'

\noindent[\textbf{21:65}]
Then they turned upside down again: 'Thou knewest that these cannot speak.'

\noindent[\textbf{21:66}]
Said he, 'Will ye then serve, beside God, what cannot profit you at all, nor harm you?

\noindent[\textbf{21:67}]
fie upon you, and what ye serve beside God! have ye then no sense?'

\noindent[\textbf{21:68}]
Said they, 'Burn him, and help your gods, if ye are going to do so!'

\noindent[\textbf{21:69}]
We said, 'O fire! be thou cool and a safety for Abraham!'

\noindent[\textbf{21:70}]
They desired to plot against him, but we made them the losers.

\noindent[\textbf{21:71}]
And we brought him and Lot safely to the land which we have blessed for the world,

\noindent[\textbf{21:72}]
and we bestowed upon him Isaac and Jacob as a fresh gift, and each of them we made
righteous persons;

\noindent[\textbf{21:73}]
and we made them high priests to guide (men) by our bidding, and we inspired them to
do good works, and to be steadfast in prayer, and to give alms; and they did serve us.

\section{Surah 37: The Chapter of the 'Ranged' (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{37:83}]
And, verily, of his sect was Abraham;

\noindent[\textbf{37:84}]
when he came to his Lord with a sound heart;

\noindent[\textbf{37:85}]
when he said to his father and his people, 'What is it that ye serve?

\noindent[\textbf{37:86}]
with a lie do ye desire gods beside God?

\noindent[\textbf{37:87}]
What then is your thought respecting the Lord of the worlds?'

\noindent[\textbf{37:88}]
And he looked a look at the stars

\noindent[\textbf{37:89}]
and said, 'Verily, I am sick!'

\noindent[\textbf{37:90}]
and they turned their backs upon him fleeing.

\noindent[\textbf{37:91}]
And he went aside unto their gods and said, 'Will ye not eat?

\noindent[\textbf{37:92}]
What ails you that ye will not speak?'

\noindent[\textbf{37:93}]
And he went aside to them smiting with the right hand.

\noindent[\textbf{37:94}]
And they rushed towards him.

\noindent[\textbf{37:95}]
Said he, 'Do ye serve what ye hew out,

\noindent[\textbf{37:96}]
when God has created you, and what ye make?'

\noindent[\textbf{37:97}]
Said they, 'Build for him a pyre, and throw him into the flaming hell!'

\noindent[\textbf{37:98}]
They desired to plot against him, but we made them inferior.

\noindent[\textbf{37:99}]
Said he, 'Verily, I am going to my Lord, He will guide me.

\noindent[\textbf{37:100}]
My Lord! grant me (a son), one of the righteous;'

\noindent[\textbf{37:101}]
and we gave him glad tidings of a clement boy.

\noindent[\textbf{37:102}]
And when he reached the age to work with him, he said, 'O my boy! verily, I have seen
in a dream that I should sacrifice thee, look then what thou seest right.' Said he, 'O my sire! do
what thou art bidden; thou wilt find me, if it please God, one of the patient!'

\noindent[\textbf{37:103}]
And when they were resigned, and Abraham had thrown him down upon his forehead,

\noindent[\textbf{37:104}]
we called to him, 'O Abraham!

\noindent[\textbf{37:105}]
thou hast verified the vision; verily, thus do we reward those who do well.

\noindent[\textbf{37:106}]
This is surely an obvious trial.'

\noindent[\textbf{37:107}]
And we ransomed him with a mighty victim;

\noindent[\textbf{37:108}]
and we left for him amongst posterity,

\noindent[\textbf{37:109}]
'Peace upon Abraham;

\noindent[\textbf{37:110}]
thus do we reward those who do well;

\noindent[\textbf{37:111}]
verily, he was of our servants who believe!'

\noindent[\textbf{37:112}]
And we gave him glad tidings of Isaac, a prophet among the righteous;

\noindent[\textbf{37:113}]
and we blessed him and Isaac;--- of their seed is one who does well, and one who
obviously wrongs himself.

\section{Surah 43: The Chapter of Gilding (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{43:26}]
When Abraham said to his father and his people, 'Verily, I am clear of all that ye serve,

\noindent[\textbf{43:27}]
except Him who created me; for, verily, He will guide me:'

\noindent[\textbf{43:28}]
and he made it a word remaining among his posterity, that haply they might return.

\noindent[\textbf{43:29}]
Nay; but I let these (Meccans) and their fathers have enjoyment until the truth came to
them, and an apostle.

\noindent[\textbf{43:30}]
And when the truth came to them they said, 'This is magic, and we therein do
disbelieve!'

\noindent[\textbf{43:31}]
And they say, 'Unless this Qur'an were sent down to a man great in the two cities. . . . .'

\section{Surah 3: The Chapter of Imran's Family (Medina).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{3:65}]
O people of the Book, why do ye dispute about Abraham, when the law and the gospel
were not revealed until after him? What! do ye not understand?

\noindent[\textbf{3:66}]
Here ye are, disputing about what ye have some knowledge of; why then do ye dispute
about what ye have no knowledge of? God knows and ye know not.

\noindent[\textbf{3:67}]
Abraham was not a Jew, nor yet a Christian, but he was a 'Hanif resigned, and not of the
idolaters.

\noindent[\textbf{3:68}]
Verily, the people most worthy of Abraham are those who follow him and his prophets,
and those who believe;--- God is the patron of the believers.

\section{Surah 2: The Chapter of the Heifer (Medina).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{2:124}]
And when his Lord tried Abraham with words, and he fulfilled them, He said, 'Verily, I
will set thee as a high priest for men.' Said he, ' And of my seed?' God said, 'My covenant
touches not the evildoers.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:125}]
And when we made the House a place of resort unto men, and a sanctuary, and (said)
take the station of Abraham for a place of prayer; and covenanted with Abraham and Ishmael,
saying, 'Do ye two cleanse my house for those who make the circuit, for those who pay
devotions there, for those who bow down, and for those too who adore.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:126}]
When Abraham said, 'Lord, make this a town of safety, and provide the dwellers there
with fruits, such as believe in God and the last day!' (God) said, ' And he who misbelieves, I will
give him but little to enjoy, then will drive him to the torment of the fire, an evil journey will it
be.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:127}]
And when Abraham raised up the foundations of the House with Ishmael, 'Lord! receive
it from us, verily, thou art hearing and dost know.

\noindent[\textbf{2:128}]
Lord! and make us too resigned unto Thee, and of our seed also a nation resigned unto
Thee, and show us our rites, and turn towards us, verily, Thou art easy to be turned and merciful.

\noindent[\textbf{2:129}]
Lord! and send them an apostle from amongst themselves, to read to them Thy signs and
teach them the Book and wisdom, and to purify them; verily, Thou art the mighty and the wise.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:130}]
Who is averse from the faith of Abraham save one who is foolish of soul? for we have
chosen him in this world, and in the future he is surely of the righteous.

\noindent[\textbf{2:131}]
When his Lord said to him, 'Be resigned,' he said, 'I am resigned unto the Lord of the
worlds.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:132}]
And Abraham instructed his sons therein, and Jacob (saying), 'O my sons! verily, God
has chosen for you a religion, do not therefore die unless ye be resigned.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:133}]
Were ye then witnesses when Jacob was facing death, when he said to his sons, 'What
will ye serve when I am gone?' They said, 'We will serve thy God, the God of thy fathers
Abraham, and Ishmael, and Isaac, one God; and we are unto Him resigned.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:134}]
That is a nation that has passed away, theirs is what they gained; and yours shall be what
ye have gained; ye shall not be questioned as to that which they have done.

\noindent[\textbf{2:135}]
They say, 'Be ye Jews or Christians so shall ye of Abraham be guided.' Say, 'Not so! but
the faith of Abraham the 'Hanif, be was not of the idolaters.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:136}]
Say ye, 'We believe in God, and what has been revealed to us, and what has been
revealed to Abraham, and Ishmael, and Isaac, and Jacob, and the Tribes, and what was brought
to Moses and Jesus, and what was brought unto the Prophets from their Lord; we will not
distinguish between any one of them, and unto Him are we resigned.

\noindent[\textbf{2:137}]
If they believe in that in which ye believe, then are they guided; but if they turn back,
then are they only in a schism, and God will suffice thee against them, for He both hears and
knows.

\noindent[\textbf{2:138}]
The dye of God! and who is better than God at dyeing? and we are worshippers of Him.

\noindent[\textbf{2:139}]
Say, 'Do ye dispute with us concerning God, and He is our Lord and your Lord? Ye have
your works and we have ours, and unto Him are we sincere.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:140}]
Do ye say that Abraham, and Ishmael, and Isaac, and Jacob, and the Tribes were Jews or
Christians? Say, 'Are ye more knowing than God? Who is more unjust than one who conceals a
testimony that he has from God?' But God is not careless of what ye do.

\noindent[\textbf{2:141}]
That is a nation that has passed away; theirs is what they gained, and yours shall be what
ye have gained; ye shall not be questioned as to that which they have done.

\section{Surah 51: The Chapter of the Scatterers (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{51:24}]
Has the tale of Abraham's honoured guests reached thee?

\noindent[\textbf{51:25}]
When they entered in unto him and said, 'Peace!' he said, 'Peace!---a people
unrecognised.'

\noindent[\textbf{51:26}]
And he went aside unto his people and fetched a fat calf,

\noindent[\textbf{51:27}]
and brought it nigh unto them; said he, 'Will ye then not eat?'

\noindent[\textbf{51:28}]
And he felt a secret fear of them: said they, Fear not.' And they gave him glad tidings of
a knowing boy.

\noindent[\textbf{51:29}]
And his wife approached with a noise, and smote her face, and said, 'An old woman,
barren!'

\noindent[\textbf{51:30}]
Said they, 'Thus says thy Lord, He is knowing, wise.'

\noindent[\textbf{51:31}]
Said he, 'And about what is your errand, O ye messengers?'

\noindent[\textbf{51:32}]
They said, 'Verily, we are sent unto a sinful people,

\noindent[\textbf{51:33}]
to send upon them stones of clay,

\noindent[\textbf{51:34}]
marked from thy Lord for the extravagant.'

\noindent[\textbf{51:35}]
And we sent out therefrom such as were in it of the believers;

\noindent[\textbf{51:36}]
but we only found therein one house of Muslims.

\noindent[\textbf{51:37}]
And we left therein a sign to those who fear the grievous woe.

\section{Surah 29: The Chapter of the Spider (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{29:16}]
And Abraham when he said to his people, 'Serve God and fear Him, that is better for you
if ye did but know.

\noindent[\textbf{29:17}]
Ye only serve beside God idols and do create a lie; verily, those whom ye serve beside
God cannot control for themselves provision; then crave provision with God, and serve Him, and
give thanks to Him; unto Him shall ye return!

\noindent[\textbf{29:18}]
And if ye say it is a lie, nations before you called (the apostles) liars too; but an apostle
has only his plain message to preach!'

\noindent[\textbf{29:19}]
Have they not seen how God produces the creation, and then turns it back? verily, that to
God is easy.

\noindent[\textbf{29:20}]
Say, 'Journey ye on in the land, and behold how the creation appeared; then God
produces another production: verily, God is mighty over all!'

\noindent[\textbf{29:21}]
He torments whom He will, and has mercy on whom He will; and unto Him shall ye be
returned.

\noindent[\textbf{29:22}]
Nor can ye make Him helpless in the earth, nor in the heavens; nor have ye beside God a
patron or a helper.

\noindent[\textbf{29:23}]
And those who disbelieve in God's signs and in meeting with Him, these shall despair of
my mercy; and these, for them is grievous woe.

\noindent[\textbf{29:24}]
But the answer of his people was only to say, 'Kill him or burn him!' But God saved him
from the fire; verily, in that are signs unto a people who believe.

\noindent[\textbf{29:25}]
He said, 'Verily, ye take beside God idols, through mutual friendship in the life of this
world; then on the day of judgment ye shall deny each other, and shall curse each other, and your
resort shall be the fire, and ye shall have none to help.'

\noindent[\textbf{29:26}]
And Lot believed him. And (Abraham) said, 'Verily, I flee unto my Lord! Verily, He is
mighty, wise!

\noindent[\textbf{29:27}]
and we granted him Isaac and Jacob; and we placed in his seed prophecy and the Book;
and we gave him his hire in this world; and, verily, he in the next shall be among the righteous.'

\noindent[\textbf{29:28}]
And Lot when be said to his people, 'Verily, ye approach an abomination which no one
in all the world ever anticipated you in!

\noindent[\textbf{29:29}]
What! do ye approach men? and stop folks on the highway? and approach in your
assembly sin?' but the answer of his people was only to say, 'Bring us God's torment, if thou art
of those who speak the truth!'

\noindent[\textbf{29:30}]
Said he, 'My Lord! help me against a people who do evil!'

\noindent[\textbf{29:31}]
And when our messengers came to Abraham with the glad tidings, they said, 'We are
about to destroy the people of this city. Verily, the people thereof are wrong-doers.'

\noindent[\textbf{29:32}]
Said he, 'Verily, in it is Lot;' they said, 'We know best who is therein; we shall of a
surety save him and his people, except his wife, who is of those who linger.'

\noindent[\textbf{29:33}]
And when our messengers came to Lot, he was vexed for them, and his arm was
straitened for them; and they said, 'Fear not, neither grieve; we are about to save thee and thy
people, except thy wife, who is of those who linger.

\noindent[\textbf{29:34}]
Verily, we are about to send down upon the people of this city a horror from heaven, for
that they have sinned;

\noindent[\textbf{29:35}]
and we have left therefrom a manifest sign unto a people who have sense.'

\section{Surah 2: The Chapter of the Heifer (Medina).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{2:258}]
Do you not look at him who disputed with Abraham about his Lord, that God had given
him the kingdom? When Abraham said, 'My Lord is He who giveth life and death,' he said, 'I
give life and death.' Abraham said, 'But verily, God brings the sun from the east, do thou then
bring it from the west?' And he who misbelieved was dumbfounded, for God does not guide
unjust folk.

\noindent[\textbf{2:259}]
Or like him who passed by a village, when it was desolate and turned over on its roofs,
and said, 'How will God revive this after its death?' And God made him die for a hundred years,
then He raised him, and said, 'How long hast thou tarried?' Said he, 'I have tarried a day, or
some part of a day.' He said, 'Nay, thou hast tarried a hundred years; look at thy food and drink,
they are not spoiled, and look at thine ass; for we will make thee a sign to men. And look at the
bones how we scatter them and then clothe them with flesh.' And when it was made manifest to
him, he said, 'I know that God is mighty over all.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:260}]
And when Abraham said, 'Lord, show me how thou wilt revive the dead,' He said,
'What, dost thou not yet believe?' Said he, 'Yea, but that my heart may be quieted.' He said,
'Then take four birds, and take them close to thyself; then put a part of them on every mountain;
then call them, and they will come to thee in haste; and know that God is mighty, wise.'

\section{Surah 37: The Chapter of the 'Ranged' (Mecca).}

 (selections)

\noindent[\textbf{37:133}]
And, verily, Lot was surely among the apostles;

\noindent[\textbf{37:134}]
when we saved him and his people altogether,

\noindent[\textbf{37:135}]
except an old woman amongst those who lingered;

\noindent[\textbf{37:136}]
then we destroyed the others;

\noindent[\textbf{37:137}]
verily, ye pass by them in the morning

\noindent[\textbf{37:138}]
and at night; have ye then no sense?

\section{Surah 27: The Chapter of the Ant (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{27:54}]
And Lot when he said to his people, 'Do ye approach an abominable sin while ye can
see?

\noindent[\textbf{27:55}]
do ye indeed approach men lustfully rather than women? nay! ye are a people who are
ignorant.'

\noindent[\textbf{27:56}]
But the answer of his people was only to say, 'Drive out Lot's family from your city!
verily, they are a folk who would keep pure.'

\noindent[\textbf{27:57}]
But we saved him and his family except his wife, her we destined to be of those who
lingered;

\noindent[\textbf{27:58}]
and we rained down upon them rain, and evil was the rain of those who were warned.

\section{Surah 12: The Chapter of Joseph (Peace Be On Him!) (Mecca).}

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{12:1}]
ALIF LAM RA. Those are the signs of the perspicuous Book.

\noindent[\textbf{12:2}]
Verily, we have revealed it, an Arabic Qur'an; haply ye may understand.

\noindent[\textbf{12:3}]
We tell thee the best of stories, in inspiring thee with this Qur'an, though thou wert
before it among the heedless.

\noindent[\textbf{12:4}]
When Joseph said to his father, 'O my sire! Verily, I saw eleven stars, and the sun, and
the moon,--- I saw them adoring me!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:5}]
He said, 'O my boy! tell not thy vision to thy brethren, for they will plot a plot against
thee verily, the devil is to man an open foe.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:6}]
Thus does thy Lord choose thee, and teach thee the interpretation of sayings, and fulfill
His favour upon thee, and upon Jacob's people, as He fulfilled it upon thy two forefathers before
thee, Abraham and Isaac,--- verily, thy Lord is knowing, wise!

\noindent[\textbf{12:7}]
In Joseph and his brethren were signs to those who enquire!

\noindent[\textbf{12:8}]
When they said, 'Surely, Joseph and his brother are dearer to our father than we, a band
although we be; verily, our father is in obvious error.

\noindent[\textbf{12:9}]
'Slay Joseph, or cast him in some land; that your father's face may be free for you, and ye
may be, after he is gone, a people who do right.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:10}]
A speaker from amongst them spake, 'Slay not Joseph, but throw him into the bottom of
the pit; some of the travellers may pick him up, if so ye do.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:11}]
Said they, 'O our father! what ails thee that thou wilt not trust us with Joseph while we
are unto him sincere?

\noindent[\textbf{12:12}]
Send him with us tomorrow to revel and to play, and, verily, we over him will keep good
guard.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:13}]
Said he, 'Verily, it grieves me that ye should go off with him, for I fear lest the wolf
devour him while ye of him do take no heed.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:14}]
Said they, 'Why, if the wolf should devour him while we are (such) a band, verily, we
then should deserve to lose!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:15}]
And when they had gone off with him and agreed to put him in the depths of the pit, and
we inspired him, 'Thou shalt surely inform them of this affair of theirs and they shall not
perceive.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:16}]
And they came to their father at eve and weeping

\noindent[\textbf{12:17}]
said, 'O our father! verily, we went forth to race and left Joseph by our goods, and the
wolf devoured him,--- but thou wilt not believe us, truth tellers though we be.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:18}]
And they brought his shirt with lying blood upon it. Said he, 'Nay, but your souls have
induced you to do this; but patience is fair! and God is He whom I ask for aid against that which
ye describe.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:19}]
And travellers came and sent their water-drawer; and he let down his bucket. Said he, 'O
glad tidings! this is a youth.' And they kept him secret, as a chattel; but God knew what they
were doing.

\noindent[\textbf{12:20}]
And they sold him for a mean price,--- drachmae counted out,--- and they parted with
him cheaply.

\noindent[\textbf{12:21}]
And the man from Egypt who had bought him said to his wife, 'Honour his abiding here;
it may be he will be of use to us, or we may adopt him as a son.' Thus did we establish Joseph in
the land; and we did surely teach him the interpretation of sayings; for God can overcome His
affairs, though most men do not know.

\noindent[\textbf{12:22}]
And when he had reached his strength we brought him judgment and knowledge, for thus
do we reward those who do good.

\noindent[\textbf{12:23}]
And she in whose house he was desired him for his person; and she locked the doors and
said, 'Come along with thee!' Said he, 'Refuge in God! verily, my Lord has made good my
abiding here; verily, the wrong-doers shall not prosper.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:24}]
And she was anxious for him, and he would have been anxious for her, had it not been
that he saw the demonstration of his Lord; thus did we turn evil and fornication from him; verily,
he was of our sincere servants.

\noindent[\textbf{12:25}]
And they raced to the door and she rent his shirt from behind; and they met her master at
the door. Said she, 'What is the recompense of him who wishes evil for thy family, but that
imprisonment or a grievous torment?'

\noindent[\textbf{12:26}]
Said he, 'She desired me for my person.' And a witness from among her family bore
witness: 'If his shirt be rent from in front, then she speaks the truth and he is of the liars;

\noindent[\textbf{12:27}]
but if his shirt be rent from behind, then she lies and he is of the truth tellers.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:28}]
And when he saw his shirt rent from behind he said, 'This is one of your tricks; verily,
your tricks are mighty!

\noindent[\textbf{12:29}]
Joseph! turn aside from this. And do thou, woman, ask pardon for thy fault; verily, thou
wert of the sinners.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:30}]
And women in the city said, 'The wife of the prince desires her young man for his
person; he has infatuated her with love: verily, we see her in obvious error.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:31}]
And when she heard of their craftiness, she sent to them and prepared for them a banquet,
and gave each of them a knife; and she said, 'Come forth to them!' And when they saw him they
said, 'Great God!' and cut their hands and said, 'God forbid! This is no mortal, this is nothing
but an honourable angel.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:32}]
Said she, 'This is he concerning whom ye blamed me. I did desire him for his person, but
he was too continent. But if he do not what I bid him he shall surely be imprisoned and shall
surely be among the small!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:33}]
Said he, 'My Lord! Prison is dearer to me than what they call on me to do; and unless
Thou turn from me their craftiness I shall feel a passion for them and shall be among the
ignorant!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:34}]
And his Lord answered him and turned from him their craftiness; verily, He both hears
and knows!

\noindent[\textbf{12:35}]
Then it appeared good to them, even after they had seen the signs, to imprison him until a
time.

\noindent[\textbf{12:36}]
And there entered the prison with him two young men. Said one of them, 'Verily, I see
myself pressing wine.' And the other said, 'Verily, I see myself bearing on my head loaves from
which the birds do eat; inform us of the interpretation thereof; verily, we see that thou art of
those who do good.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:37}]
He said, 'There shall not come to you any food with which ye are provided, but I will
inform you both of its interpretation before it comes to you. That is (some) of what my Lord has
taught me; verily, I have left the faith of a people who do not believe in God, while in the future
too they disbelieve.

\noindent[\textbf{12:38}]
And I have followed the faith of my fathers, Abraham and Isaac and Jacob; we could not
associate aught with God; that is from God's grace upon us and upon men: but most men give
not thanks.

\noindent[\textbf{12:39}]
O ye twain fellow-prisoners! Are manifold lords better, or God, the one, the dominant?

\noindent[\textbf{12:40}]
What ye worship beside Him are naught but names which ye have named, ye and your
fathers, for which God has sent down no authority. Judgment is only God's; He bids you worship
only Him. That is the standard of religion,--- but most men do not know.

\noindent[\textbf{12:41}]
O ye twain fellow-prisoners! as for one of you, he shall pour out wine for his lord: and as
for the other, he shall be crucified, and the birds shall eat of his head. The matter is decreed
whereon ye asked me for a decision!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:42}]
And he said to him whom he thought would escape of those two, 'Remember me with thy
lord!' But Satan made him forget the remembrance of his lord, so he tarried in prison a few
years.

\noindent[\textbf{12:43}]
Then said the king, 'Verily, I see seven fat kine which seven lean kine devoured; and
seven green ears of corn and others dry. O ye chiefs! Explain to me my vision, if a vision ye can
expound!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:44}]
Said they, 'Confused dreams, and naught of the exposition of such dreams know we!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:45}]
Then he who had escaped of those twain said,--- remembering after a while,--- 'Verily, I
will inform you of the interpretation thereof, so send me.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:46}]
'Joseph! O thou truth teller! explain to us the seven fat kine which seven lean devoured;
and the seven green ears of corn and others dry. Haply I may go back to the men, haply they then
may know!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:47}]
He said, 'Ye shall sow for seven years, as is your wont; but what ye reap, let it remain in
the ear, except a little whereof ye shall eat.

\noindent[\textbf{12:48}]
Then there shall come after that seven severe (years) which shall devour what ye have put
by before for them, save a little of what ye shall preserve.

\noindent[\textbf{12:49}]
Then there will come after that a year in which men shall have rain and in which they
shall press.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:50}]
Then said the king, 'Bring him to me.' And when the messenger came to him, he said,
'Go back to thy lord, and ask him, "What meant the women who cut their hands? Verily, my lord
knows their craftiness!"'

\noindent[\textbf{12:51}]
He said, 'What was your design when ye desired Joseph for his person?' They said, 'God
forbid! we know no bad of him.' Said the wife of the prince, ' Now does the truth appear! I
desired him for his person and, verily, he is of those who tell the truth.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:52}]
'That' (said Joseph) 'was that he might know that I did not betray him in his absence, and
that God guides not the craft of those who do betray!

\noindent[\textbf{12:53}]
Yet I do not clear myself, for the soul is very urgent to evil, save what my Lord has had
mercy on; verily, my Lord is forgiving and merciful!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:54}]
And the king said, 'Bring him to me. I will take him specially for myself.' And when he
had spoken with him he said, 'Verily, today thou art with us in a permanent place of trust.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:55}]
He said, 'Place me over the treasures of the land; verily, I will be a knowing keeper.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:56}]
Thus did we establish Joseph in the land that he might settle in what part thereof he
pleased --- we overtake with our mercy whom we will, nor do we waste the hire of those who do
good;

\noindent[\textbf{12:57}]
and surely the hire of the future life is better for those who believe and who have feared.

\noindent[\textbf{12:58}]
And his brethren came to Joseph, and they entered in unto him and he knew them, but
they recognised not him.

\noindent[\textbf{12:59}]
And when he had equipped them with their equipment he said, 'Bring me a brother that
ye have from your father; do ye not see that I give good measure, and that I am the best of
entertainers?

\noindent[\textbf{12:60}]
But if ye bring him not to me, no measure shall ye have with me, nor shall ye come nigh
me.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:61}]
They said, 'We will desire him of our father, and we will surely do it.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:62}]
Then he said to his young men, 'Put their chattels in their packs, haply they may know it
when they are come back to their family; haply they may return.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:63}]
And when they returned to their father, they said, 'O our father! Measure is withheld
from us; so send with us our brother that we may get measure, and, verily, him we will keep!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:64}]
He said, 'Shall I entrust you with him, save as I entrusted you with his brother before?
but God is the best of keepers, and He is the most merciful of the merciful.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:65}]
And when they opened their goods they found their chattels restored to them. Said they,
'O our father! What more can we crave? Here are our chattels restored to us, and we shall guard
our brother, and shall have an additional measure beside that --- a small measure.

\noindent[\textbf{12:66}]
He said, 'I will by no means send him with you until you give me a compact from God
that ye will surely bring him to me, unless ye be encompassed.' So when they had given him
their compact he said, 'God over what ye say has charge.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:67}]
And he said, 'O my sons! enter not by one gate, but enter by several gates; but I cannot
avail you aught against God. Judgment is only God's; upon Him do I rely, and on Him do the
reliant rely.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:68}]
And when they had entered as their father bade them, it availed them nothing against
God, save for a want in Jacob's soul which it fulfilled; for, verily, he was possessed of
knowledge, for that we had taught him;--- but most men do not know.

\noindent[\textbf{12:69}]
And when they entered in unto Joseph, he took his brother to stay with him, and said,
'Verily, I am thy brother --- then take not ill that which they have been doing.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:70}]
And when he had equipped them with their equipment he placed the drinking cup in his
brother's pack; then a crier cried out, 'O ye caravan! verily, ye are thieves!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:71}]
They said, approaching them, 'What is it that ye miss?'

\noindent[\textbf{12:72}]
Said they, 'We miss the goblet of the king, and whoso brings it shall have a camel-load,
and I am guarantee thereof.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:73}]
They said, 'By God! Ye knew we came not to do evil in the land, and that we were not
thieves.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:74}]
They said, 'And what shall be the recompense thereof if ye be liars?'

\noindent[\textbf{12:75}]
They said, 'The recompense thereof is he in whose pack it is found --- he shall be the
recompense thereof; thus do we recompense the unjust.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:76}]
And he began with their sacks before the sacks of his brother; then he drew it forth from
his brother's sack. Thus did we devise a stratagem for Joseph. He could not take his brother by
the king's religion except God pleased;--- we raise the degrees of whomsoever we please, and
over every possessor of knowledge is one who knows.

\noindent[\textbf{12:77}]
They said, 'If he has stolen, a brother of his has stolen before him.' But Joseph kept it
secret in his soul and disclosed it not to them. Said he, 'Ye are in a bad case, and God knows best
about what ye describe.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:78}]
They said, 'O prince! Verily, he has a father, a very old man; take then one of us instead
of him; verily, we can see that thou art of those who do good.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:79}]
Said he, '(I seek) refuge in God from taking any save him with whom we found our
property; verily, we should then be certainly unjust.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:80}]
And when they despaired of him they retired to consult privately. Said the eldest of them,
'Do ye not know that your father has taken a compact from God against you? Aforetime ye
exceeded in the matter of Joseph --- I will surely not quit the land until my father give me leave,
or God judge for me, for He is the best of judges.

\noindent[\textbf{12:81}]
'Return ye to your father and say, "O our father! verily, thy son has committed theft, and
we bore testimony to naught but what we knew; for of the unforeseen we were not keepers!"

\noindent[\textbf{12:82}]
'Ask then in the city where we were, and of the caravan in which we approached it, for,
verily, we tell the truth.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:83}]
Said he, 'Nay, your souls have induced you to do this thing. But patience is fair. It may
be that God will give me them all together;--- verily, He is knowing, wise.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:84}]
And he turned away from them and said, 'O my lament for Joseph!' and his eyes grew
white with grief, for he repressed (his woe).

\noindent[\textbf{12:85}]
They said, 'By God! thou wilt not cease to remember Joseph till thou art at the point of
death, or art of those who perish!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:86}]
Said he, 'I only complain of my emotion and my grief to God, for I know that from God
which ye know nothing of.

\noindent[\textbf{12:87}]
'O my sons! go and enquire concerning Joseph and his brother, and despair not of God's
comfort; for, verily, none need despair of God's comfort save a misbelieving people!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:88}]
And when they entered in unto him they said, 'O prince! distress has touched both us and
our families, and we have brought trifling chattels. So give us full measure and bestow upon us
in charity; verily, God rewards the charitable.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:89}]
He said, 'Do ye know what ye did with Joseph and his brother, while ye were ignorant?'

\noindent[\textbf{12:90}]
They said, 'Art thou then indeed Joseph? He said, 'I am Joseph, and this is my brother;
God has been gracious towards us. Verily, whoso fears God and is patient,--- verily, God wastes
not the hire of those who do good!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:91}]
They said, 'By God! God has chosen thee over us; and we indeed were sinners.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:92}]
He said, 'No reproach against you today! God will pardon you, for He is the most
merciful of the merciful.

\noindent[\textbf{12:93}]
Take this my shirt, and throw it over the face of my father, he will become able to see;
and bring, me your families all together.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:94}]
And when the caravan departed, their father said, 'Verily, I find the smell of Joseph,
unless ye think I dote!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:95}]
They said, 'By God! thou art in thy old error.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:96}]
And when the herald of glad tidings came he threw it on his face, and he was restored to
sight. Said he, 'Did I not tell you that I know from God that of which ye know not?'

\noindent[\textbf{12:97}]
They said, 'O our father! ask pardon for us of our sins;--- verily, we were sinners.

\noindent[\textbf{12:98}]
He said, 'I will ask pardon for you from my Lord; verily, He is the pardoning and
merciful.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:99}]
And when they entered in unto Joseph, he took his father to stay with him, and said,
'Enter ye into Egypt, if it please God, safe.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:100}]
And he raised his father upon the throne, and they fell down before him adoring. And he
said, 'O my sire! This is the interpretation of my vision aforetime; my Lord has made it come
true, and He has been good to me, in bringing me forth out of prison, and bringing you from the
desert, after Satan had made a breach between me and my brethren;--- verily, my Lord is kind to
whomsoever He will;--- verily, He is the knowing, the wise!

\noindent[\textbf{12:101}]
O my Lord! thou least given me dominion, and hast taught me the interpretation of
sayings; O originator of the heavens and the earth! Thou art my patron in this world and the next;
take me to Thyself resigned, And let me reach the righteous!'

\noindent[\textbf{12:102}]
That is one of the stories of the unseen which we inspire thee with, though thou wert not
with them when they agreed in their affair, when they were so crafty.---

\noindent[\textbf{12:103}]
And yet most men, though thou shouldst be urgent, will not believe.

\noindent[\textbf{12:104}]
Thou dost not ask them for it a hire; it is naught but a reminder to the world.

\noindent[\textbf{12:105}]
How many a sign in the heavens and the earth do they pass by and turn away therefrom!

\noindent[\textbf{12:106}]
Nor do most of them believe in God without associating (other gods) with Him.

\noindent[\textbf{12:107}]
Are they safe, then, from overwhelming vengeance coming on them from the torment of
God? or from the Hour coming upon them suddenly while they do not perceive?

\noindent[\textbf{12:108}]
Say, 'This is my way; I call now unto God on clear proof, I and those who follow me;
and celebrated be God's praises, for I am not of the idolaters.'

\noindent[\textbf{12:109}]
Nor did we ever send before thee any save men whom we inspired, of the people of the
cities. Have they not journeyed on in the earth, and beheld how was the end of those before
them? But the abode of the future is surely better for those who believe;--- what! have they then
no sense?

\noindent[\textbf{12:110}]
Until when the apostles despaired and they thought that they were proved liars, our help
came to them, and whosoever we pleased was saved; but our violence is not averted from the
sinful people.

\noindent[\textbf{12:111}]
Their stories were a lesson to those endowed with minds. It was not a tale forged, but a
verification of what was before it, and a detailing of everything, and a guide and a mercy to a
people who believe.

\clearpage
\chapter{Session 3}


\section{Surah 3: The Chapter of Imran's Family (Medina).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{3:69}]
A sect of the people of the Book would fain they could lead you astray, but they only lead
themselves astray, and they do not perceive.

\noindent[\textbf{3:70}]
O people of the Book! why do ye disbelieve in the signs of God, the while ye witness
them?

\noindent[\textbf{3:71}]
O people of the Book! why do ye clothe the truth with falsehood and hide the truth the
while ye know?

\noindent[\textbf{3:72}]
A sect of the people of the Book say, 'Believe in what was revealed to those who
believed at the first appearance of the day, and disbelieve it at the end thereof,' --- that (others)
may perchance go back (from their faith)---

\noindent[\textbf{3:73}]
'do not believe save one who followeth your religion.' Say, 'Verily, the (true) guidance is
the guidance of God, that one should be given like what ye are given.' Or would they dispute
with you before your Lord, say, 'Grace is in the hand of God, He gives it to whom he pleases, for
God both comprehends and knows.

\noindent[\textbf{3:74}]
He specially favours with his mercy whom he pleases, for God is Lord of mighty grace.

\noindent[\textbf{3:75}]
And of the people of the Book, there are some of them who, if thou entrust them with a
talent give it back to you; and some of them, if thou entrust them with a dinar, he will not give it
back to thee except so long as thou dost stand over him. That is because they say, We owe no
duty to the Gentiles;' but they tell a lie against God, the while they know.

\noindent[\textbf{3:76}]
Yea, whoso fulfils his covenant and fears,--- verily, God loves those who fear.

\noindent[\textbf{3:77}]
Those who sell God's covenant and their oaths for a little price, these have no portion in
the future life. God will not speak to them, and will not look upon them on the resurrection day,
and will not purify them; but for them is grievous woe.

\noindent[\textbf{3:78}]
And, verily, amongst them is a sect who twist their tongues concerning the Book, that ye
may reckon it to be from the Book, but it is not from the Book. They say, 'It is from God,' but it
is not from God, and they tell a lie against God, the while they know.

\noindent[\textbf{3:79}]
It is not right for a man that God should give him a Book, and judgment, and prophecy,
and that then he should say to men, 'Be ye servants of mine rather than of God;' but be ye rather
masters of teaching the Book and of what ye learn.

\noindent[\textbf{3:80}]
He does not bid you take the angels and the prophets for your lords; shall He bid you
misbelieve again when you are once resigned?

\noindent[\textbf{3:81}]
And when God took the compact from the prophets '(this is) surely what we have given
you of the Book and wisdom. Then shall come to you the Apostle confirming what is with you.
Ye must believe in him and help him.' He said, moreover, 'Are ye resolved and have ye taken
my compact on that (condition).' They say, 'We are resolved.' He said, 'Then bear witness, for I
am witness with you;

\noindent[\textbf{3:82}]
but he who turns back after that, these are sinners.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:83}]
What is it other than God's religion that they crave? when to Him is resigned whosoever
is in the heavens and the earth, will he or nill he, and to him shall they return!

\section{Surah 72: The Chapter of the Ginn (Mecca).}

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{72:1}]
Say, 'I have been inspired that there listened a company of the ginn, and they said, "We
have heard a marvellous Qur'an

\noindent[\textbf{72:2}]
that guides to the right direction; and we believe therein, and we join no one with our
Lord,

\noindent[\textbf{72:3}]
for, verily, He --- may the majesty of our Lord be exalted!--- has taken to Himself neither
consort nor son.

\noindent[\textbf{72:4}]
'"And, verily, a fool among us spake against God wide of the mark!

\noindent[\textbf{72:5}]
"'And we thought that men and ginn would never speak a lie against God.

\noindent[\textbf{72:6}]
"'And there are persons amongst men who seek for refuge with persons amongst the
ginn; but they increase them in their perverseness.

\noindent[\textbf{72:7}]
And they thought, as ye thought, that God would not raise up any one from the dead.

\noindent[\textbf{72:8}]
"'But we touched the heavens and found them filled with a mighty guard and shooting-
stars;

\noindent[\textbf{72:9}]
and we did sit in certain seats thereof to listen; but whoso of us listens now finds a
shooting-star for him on guard.

\noindent[\textbf{72:10}]
'"And, verily, we know not whether evil be meant for those who are in the earth, or if
their Lord means right by them.

\noindent[\textbf{72:11}]
'"And of us are some who are pious, and of us are some who are otherwise: we are in
separate bands.

\noindent[\textbf{72:12}]
'"And we thought that we could not frustrate God in the earth, and could not frustrate
Him by flight.

\noindent[\textbf{72:13}]
"But, verily, when we heard the guidance we believed therein, and he who believes in his
Lord shall fear neither diminution nor loss.

\noindent[\textbf{72:14}]
'"And, verily, of us are some who are Muslims, and of us some are trespassers; but those
of us who are Muslims they strive after right direction;

\noindent[\textbf{72:15}]
and as for the trespassers they are fuel for hell."'

\noindent[\textbf{72:16}]
And if they will go right upon the way, we will irrigate them with copious water

\noindent[\textbf{72:17}]
to try them thereby; and whoso turns from the remembrance of his Lord He will drive
him to severe torment.

\noindent[\textbf{72:18}]
And (say) that the mosques are God's, and that ye should not call on any one with God,

\noindent[\textbf{72:19}]
and that when God's servant stood up to pray they called out to him and well-nigh
crowded upon him.

\noindent[\textbf{72:20}]
Say, 'I only call upon my Lord, and I join no one with Him.'

\noindent[\textbf{72:21}]
Say, 'Verily, I cannot control for you either harm, or right direction.'

\noindent[\textbf{72:22}]
Say, 'Verily, as for me none can protect me against God, nor do I find any refuge beside
Him,---

\noindent[\textbf{72:23}]
except delivering the message from God and His errands: and whoso rebels against God
and His Apostle, verily, for him is the fire of hell for them to dwell therein for ever and for aye!'

\noindent[\textbf{72:24}]
Until when they see what they are threatened with, then shall they surely know who is
most weak at helping and fewest in numbers!

\noindent[\textbf{72:25}]
Say, 'I know not if what ye are threatened with be nigh, or if my Lord will set for it a
term.

\noindent[\textbf{72:26}]
He knows the unseen, and He lets no one know His unseen,

\noindent[\textbf{72:27}]
save such apostle as He is well pleased with: for, verily, He sends marching before him
and behind him a guard!'

\noindent[\textbf{72:28}]
That He may know that they have delivered the errands of their Lord, for He compasses
what they have, and reckons everything by number.

\clearpage
\chapter{Session 4}


\section{Surah 53: The Chapter of the Star (Mecca).}

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{53:1}]
By the star when it falls,

\noindent[\textbf{53:2}]
your comrade errs not, nor is he deluded!

\noindent[\textbf{53:3}]
nor speaks he out of lust!

\noindent[\textbf{53:4}]
It is but an inspiration inspired!

\noindent[\textbf{53:5}]
One mighty in power taught him,

\noindent[\textbf{53:6}]
endowed with sound understanding, and appeared,

\noindent[\textbf{53:7}]
he being in the loftiest tract.

\noindent[\textbf{53:8}]
Then drew he near and hovered o'er!

\noindent[\textbf{53:9}]
until he was two bows length off or nigher still!

\noindent[\textbf{53:10}]
Then he inspired his servant what he inspired him;

\noindent[\textbf{53:11}]
the heart belies not what he saw!

\noindent[\textbf{53:12}]
What, will ye dispute with him on what he saw?

\noindent[\textbf{53:13}]
And he saw him another time,

\noindent[\textbf{53:14}]
by the lote tree none may pass;

\noindent[\textbf{53:15}]
near which is the garden of the Abode!

\noindent[\textbf{53:16}]
When there covered the lote tree what did cover it!

\noindent[\textbf{53:17}]
The sight swerved not nor wandered.

\noindent[\textbf{53:18}]
He saw then the greatest of the signs of his Lord.

\noindent[\textbf{53:19}]
Have ye considered Allat and Al 'Huzza,

\noindent[\textbf{53:20}]
and Manat the other third?

\noindent[\textbf{53:21}]
Shall there be male offspring for Him and female for you?

\noindent[\textbf{53:22}]
That were an unfair division!

\noindent[\textbf{53:23}]
They are but names which ye have named, ye and your fathers! God has sent down no
authority for them! They do but follow suspicion and what their souls lust after!--- And yet there
has come to them guidance from their Lord.

\noindent[\textbf{53:24}]
Shall man have what he desires?

\noindent[\textbf{53:25}]
But God's is the hereafter and the present!

\noindent[\textbf{53:26}]
How many an angel in the heaven!--- their intercession avails not at all, save after God
has given permission to whomsoever He will and is pleased with!

\noindent[\textbf{53:27}]
Verily, those who believe not in the hereafter do surely name the angels with female
names!---

\noindent[\textbf{53:28}]
but they have no knowledge thereof; they do but follow suspicion, and, verily, suspicion
shall not avail against the truth at all!

\noindent[\textbf{53:29}]
But turn aside from him who turns his back upon our remembrance and desires naught
but this world's life!

\noindent[\textbf{53:30}]
This is their sum of knowledge; verily, thy Lord knows best who has erred from His way,
and He knows best who is guided!

\noindent[\textbf{53:31}]
God's is what is in the heavens and what is in the earth, that He may reward those who do
evil for what they have done; and may reward those who do good with good!

\noindent[\textbf{53:32}]
those who shun great sins and iniquities,--- all but venial faults,--- verily, thy Lord is of
ample forgiveness; He knows best about you, when He produced you from the earth, and when
ye were embryos in the wombs of your mothers. Make not yourselves out, then, to be pure; He
knows best who it is that fears.

\noindent[\textbf{53:33}]
Hast thou considered him who turns his back?

\noindent[\textbf{53:34}]
who gives but little and then stops?

\noindent[\textbf{53:35}]
Has he then the knowledge of the unseen, so that he can see?

\noindent[\textbf{53:36}]
Has he not been informed of what is in the pages of Moses

\noindent[\textbf{53:37}]
and Abraham who fulfilled his word?---

\noindent[\textbf{53:38}]
that no burdened soul shall bear the burden of another?

\noindent[\textbf{53:39}]
and that man shall have only that for which he strives;

\noindent[\textbf{53:40}]
and that his striving shall at length be seen?

\noindent[\textbf{53:41}]
Then shall he be rewarded for it with the most full reward;

\noindent[\textbf{53:42}]
and that unto thy Lord is the limit;

\noindent[\textbf{53:43}]
and that it is He who makes men laugh and weep;

\noindent[\textbf{53:44}]
and that it is He who kills and makes alive;

\noindent[\textbf{53:45}]
and that He created pairs, male and female,

\noindent[\textbf{53:46}]
from a clot when it is emitted;

\noindent[\textbf{53:47}]
and that for Him is the next production;

\noindent[\textbf{53:48}]
and that he enriches and gives possession;

\noindent[\textbf{53:49}]
and that He is the Lord of the Dog-star,

\noindent[\textbf{53:50}]
and that He it was who destroyed 'Ad of yore,

\noindent[\textbf{53:51}]
and Thamud, and left none of them;

\noindent[\textbf{53:52}]
and the people of Noah before them,--- verily, they were most unjust and outrageous!

\noindent[\textbf{53:53}]
And the overthrown (cities) He threw down;

\noindent[\textbf{53:54}]
and there covered them what did cover them!

\noindent[\textbf{53:55}]
Which then of your Lord's benefits do ye dispute?

\noindent[\textbf{53:56}]
This is a warner, one of the warners of yore!

\noindent[\textbf{53:57}]
The approaching day approaches;

\noindent[\textbf{53:58}]
there, is none to discover it but God.

\noindent[\textbf{53:59}]
At this new discourse then do ye wonder?

\noindent[\textbf{53:60}]
and do ye laugh and not weep?

\noindent[\textbf{53:61}]
and ye divert yourselves the while!

\noindent[\textbf{53:62}]
But adore God and serve (Him).

\section{Surah 14: The Chapter of Abraham (Peace Be On Him!) (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{14:1}]
ALIF LAM RA. A book which we have sent down to thee, to bring men forth from
darkness into light, by permission of their Lord, unto the way of the mighty and praiseworthy
one.

\noindent[\textbf{14:2}]
God is He whose is whatsoever is in the heavens and whatsoever is in the earth. Alas for
the misbelievers, for their torment is keen!

\noindent[\textbf{14:3}]
Who love this world's life better than the next, and turn folks from the path of God, and
crave to make it crooked; these are in remote error.

\noindent[\textbf{14:4}]
We have not sent any apostle save with the language of his people, that he might explain
to them. But God leads whom He will astray, and guides whom He will; and He is the mighty,
the wise.

\section{Surah 10: The Chapter of Jonah (Peace Be on Him!) (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{10:37}]
This Qur'an could not have been devised by any beside God; but it verifies that which
was before it, and details the Book --- there is no doubt therein --- from the Lord of the worlds.

\noindent[\textbf{10:38}]
Do they say, 'He hath devised it?' say then, 'Bring a surah like it,--- and call, if ye can,
on other than God, if ye do tell the truth!'

\noindent[\textbf{10:39}]
Yet they call that a lie, the knowledge of which they cannot compass, while its
interpretation has not yet come to them; so did those before them charge with lying, and see what
was the end of the unjust!

\noindent[\textbf{10:40}]
Of them are some who believe therein; and of them are some who do not believe therein;
but thy Lord knows best who are corrupters.

\noindent[\textbf{10:41}]
But if they call thee liar, say, 'I have my work, and ye have your work; ye are clear of
what I work, and I am clear of what ye work.'

\noindent[\textbf{10:42}]
There are some of them who listen to thee --- canst thou make the deaf to hear, although
they have no sense?

\noindent[\textbf{10:43}]
And of them are some who look at thee --- canst thou guide the blind, although they
cannot see?

\noindent[\textbf{10:44}]
Verily, God wrongs not man at all, but men do wrong themselves.

\noindent[\textbf{10:45}]
And on the day when we will gather them together it will be as though they had not
tarried save an hour of the day, they shall know each other. Lost are those who called the
meeting with God a lie, and were not guided!

\noindent[\textbf{10:46}]
Either we will show thee something of that with which we threatened them, or we will
take thee to ourself, for unto us is their return; then is God a witness to what they do.

\noindent[\textbf{10:47}]
Every nation has its apostle; and when their apostle comes to them, it is decided between
them with justice, and they are not wronged.

\noindent[\textbf{10:48}]
But they say, 'When is this threat (to come), if ye tell the truth?'

\noindent[\textbf{10:49}]
Say, 'I have no power over myself for harm or for profit, save what God will. Every
nation has its appointed time; when their appointed time comes to them they cannot delay it for
an hour or bring it on.'

\noindent[\textbf{10:50}]
Say, 'Let us see now when the torment comes to you, by night or day, what will the
sinners fain bring on thereof?

\noindent[\textbf{10:51}]
And when it has fallen --- will ye believe in it now!--- And yet ye wish to bring it on!

\noindent[\textbf{10:52}]
Then shall it be said to those who have done wrong, Taste ye the torment of eternity!
shall ye be recompensed except for that which ye have earned?'

\noindent[\textbf{10:53}]
They will ask thee to inform them whether it be true. Say, 'Aye, by my Lord! verily, it is
the truth, nor can ye weaken him.'

\noindent[\textbf{10:54}]
And if every soul that hath done wrong had whatever is in the earth, it would give it as a
ransom. They will utter their repentance when they see the torment; and it shall be decided
between them with justice, nor shall they be wronged.

\noindent[\textbf{10:55}]
Is not indeed what is in the heavens and what is in the earth God's? is not indeed the
promise of God true? Though most of them know not.

\noindent[\textbf{10:56}]
He quickens and He kills, and unto Him are ye returned!

\noindent[\textbf{10:57}]
O ye folk! there has come to you a warning from your Lord, and a balm for what is in
your breasts, and a guidance and a mercy to believers.

\noindent[\textbf{10:58}]
Say, 'By the grace of God and by His mercy,--- and in that let them rejoice! It is better
than that which they collect!'

\noindent[\textbf{10:59}]
Let us see now what God has sent down to you of provision! and yet ye have made of it
unlawful and lawful. Say, 'Does God permit you, or against God do ye forge lies?'

\noindent[\textbf{10:60}]
What will those who forge lies against God think on the resurrection day? Verily, God is
Lord of grace towards men, but most of them do not give thanks!

\noindent[\textbf{10:61}]
Nor shalt thou be in any affair, nor shalt thou recite concerning it a Qur'an --- nor shall ye
do a work, without our being witness against you, when ye are engaged therein: nor does the
weight of an atom escape thy Lord in earth or in heaven; nor is there less than that or greater, but
it is in the perspicuous Book.

\noindent[\textbf{10:62}]
Are not, verily, the friends of God those on whom there is no fear, neither shall they be
grieved?---

\noindent[\textbf{10:63}]
They who believed and who did fear ---

\noindent[\textbf{10:64}]
for them are good tidings in the life of this world, and in the future too; there is no
changing the words of God! That is the mighty happiness!

\noindent[\textbf{10:65}]
Let not their speech grieve thee; verily, power is wholly God's! He both hears and
knows.

\noindent[\textbf{10:66}]
Is not, verily, whoever is in the heavens and whoever is in the earth God's? What then do
they follow who call on associates other than God? Verily, they follow nothing but suspicion,
and verily, they are telling naught but lies.

\noindent[\textbf{10:67}]
He it is who made for you the night, that ye might rest therein, and the day to see therein;
verily, in that are signs unto a people who can hear.

\noindent[\textbf{10:68}]
They say, 'God has taken to Himself a son.' Celebrated be His praises! He is the rich
one! His is whatever is in the heavens, and whatever is in the earth. Ye have no authority for
this! will ye say against God, that which ye do not know?

\noindent[\textbf{10:69}]
Say, 'Verily, those who forge against God a lie shall not prosper!'

\noindent[\textbf{10:70}]
A provision in this world --- then unto us is their return! then we will make them taste
keen torment for that they misbelieved.

\section{Surah 74: The Chapter of the 'Covered' (Mecca).}

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{74:1}]
O thou who art covered!

\noindent[\textbf{74:2}]
rise up and warn!

\noindent[\textbf{74:3}]
And thy Lord magnify!

\noindent[\textbf{74:4}]
And thy garments purify!

\noindent[\textbf{74:5}]
And abomination shun!

\noindent[\textbf{74:6}]
And grant not favours to gain increase!

\noindent[\textbf{74:7}]
And for thy Lord await!

\noindent[\textbf{74:8}]
And when the trump is blown,---

\noindent[\textbf{74:9}]
for that day is a difficult day!

\noindent[\textbf{74:10}]
for the misbelievers aught but easy!

\noindent[\textbf{74:11}]
Leave me alone with him I have created,

\noindent[\textbf{74:12}]
and for whom I have made extensive wealth,

\noindent[\textbf{74:13}]
and sons that he may look upon,

\noindent[\textbf{74:14}]
and for whom I have smoothed things down.

\noindent[\textbf{74:15}]
Then he desires that I should increase!

\noindent[\textbf{74:16}]
nay, verily, he is hostile to our signs!

\noindent[\textbf{74:17}]
I will drive him up a hill!

\noindent[\textbf{74:18}]
Then he reflected and planned!

\noindent[\textbf{74:19}]
May he be killed, --- how he planned!

\noindent[\textbf{74:20}]
Again, may he be killed, --- how he planned!

\noindent[\textbf{74:21}]
Then he looked;

\noindent[\textbf{74:22}]
then he frowned and scowled;

\noindent[\textbf{74:23}]
then he retreated and was big with pride

\noindent[\textbf{74:24}]
and said, 'This is only magic exhibited!

\noindent[\textbf{74:25}]
this is only mortal speech!' ---

\noindent[\textbf{74:26}]
I will broil him in hell-fire!

\noindent[\textbf{74:27}]
and what shall make thee know what hell-fire is?

\noindent[\textbf{74:28}]
It will not leave and will not let alone.

\noindent[\textbf{74:29}]
It scorches the flesh;

\noindent[\textbf{74:30}]
over it are nineteen (angels).

\noindent[\textbf{74:31}]
We have made only angels guardians of the fire, and we have only made their number a
trial to those who misbelieve; that those who have been given the Book may be certain, and that
those who believe may be increased in faith; and that those who have been given the Book and
the believers may not doubt; and that those in whose hearts is sickness, and the misbelievers may
say, 'What does God mean by this as a parable?' Thus God leads astray whom He pleases, and
guides him He pleases: and none knows the hosts of thy Lord save Himself; and it is only a
reminder to mortals!

\noindent[\textbf{74:32}]
Nay, by the moon!

\noindent[\textbf{74:33}]
And the night when it retires!

\noindent[\textbf{74:34}]
And the morning when it brightly dawns!

\noindent[\textbf{74:35}]
Verily, it is one of the greatest misfortunes;

\noindent[\textbf{74:36}]
a warning to mortals;

\noindent[\textbf{74:37}]
for him amongst you who wishes to press forward or to tarry!

\noindent[\textbf{74:38}]
Every soul is pledged for what it earns;

\noindent[\textbf{74:39}]
except the fellows of the right:

\noindent[\textbf{74:40}]
in gardens shall they ask each other

\noindent[\textbf{74:41}]
about the sinners!---

\noindent[\textbf{74:42}]
'What drove you into hell-fire?'

\noindent[\textbf{74:43}]
They shall say, 'We weren't of those who prayed;

\noindent[\textbf{74:44}]
we didn't feed the poor;

\noindent[\textbf{74:45}]
but we did plunge into discussion with those who plunged,

\noindent[\textbf{74:46}]
and we called the judgment day a lie

\noindent[\textbf{74:47}]
until the certainty did come to us!'

\noindent[\textbf{74:48}]
But there shall not profit them the intercession of the intercessors.

\noindent[\textbf{74:49}]
What ailed them that they turned away from the memorial

\noindent[\textbf{74:50}]
as though they were timid asses

\noindent[\textbf{74:51}]
fleeing from a lion?

\noindent[\textbf{74:52}]
Nay, every man of them wished that he might have given him books spread open!

\noindent[\textbf{74:53}]
Nay, but they did not fear the hereafter!

\noindent[\textbf{74:54}]
Nay, it is a memorial!

\noindent[\textbf{74:55}]
and let him who will remember it;

\noindent[\textbf{74:56}]
but none will remember it except God please. He is most worthy of fear; and he is most
worthy to forgive!

\section{Surah 41: The Chapter 'Detailed' (Mecca).}

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{41:1}]
HA MIM.

\noindent[\textbf{41:2}]
A revelation from the merciful, the compassionate;

\noindent[\textbf{41:3}]
a book whose signs are detailed; an Arabic Qur'an for a people who do know;

\noindent[\textbf{41:4}]
a herald of glad tidings and a warning. But most of them turn aside and do not hear,

\noindent[\textbf{41:5}]
and say, 'Our hearts are veiled from what thou dost call us to, and in our ears is dullness,
and between us and thee there is a veil. Act thou; verily, we are acting too!'

\noindent[\textbf{41:6}]
Say, 'I am but a mortal like yourselves, I am inspired that your God is one God; then go
straight to Him, and ask forgiveness of Him; and woe to the idolaters,

\noindent[\textbf{41:7}]
who give not alms, and in the hereafter disbelieve!'

\noindent[\textbf{41:8}]
Verily, those who believe and do right, for them is a hire that is not grudged.

\noindent[\textbf{41:9}]
Say, 'What! do ye really misbelieve in Him who created the earth in two days, and do ye
make peers for Him?--- that is the Lord of the worlds!'

\noindent[\textbf{41:10}]
And He placed thereon firm mountains above it and blessed it, and apportioned therein its
foods in four days alike for those who ask.

\noindent[\textbf{41:11}]
Then He made for the heaven and it was but smoke, and He said to it and to the earth,
'Come, ye two, whether ye will or no!' They said, 'We come willingly!'

\noindent[\textbf{41:12}]
And He decreed them seven heavens in two days, and inspired every heaven with its
bidding: and we adorned the lower heaven with lamps and guardian angels; that is the decree of
the mighty, the knowing One.

\noindent[\textbf{41:13}]
But if they turn aside, then say, 'I have warned you of a thunder-clap like the thunder-
clap of 'Ad and Thamud;

\noindent[\textbf{41:14}]
when their apostles came to them from before them and from behind them (saying),
"Serve ye none but God."' They said, 'If our Lord pleased He would send down angels; so we in
what ye are sent with disbelieve,'

\noindent[\textbf{41:15}]
And as for 'Ad, they were big with pride in the land, without right, and said, 'Who is
stronger than us in might?' Did they not see that God who created them He was stronger than
they in might? But they did gainsay our signs.

\noindent[\textbf{41:16}]
And we sent upon them a cold blast in unfortunate days, that we might make them taste
the torment of disgrace in the life of this world;--- but the torment of the hereafter is more
disgraceful, and they shall not be helped.

\noindent[\textbf{41:17}]
And as for Thamud we guided them; but they preferred blindness to guidance, and the
thunder-clap of the torment of abasement caught them for what they had earned;

\noindent[\textbf{41:18}]
but we saved those who believed and who did fear.

\noindent[\textbf{41:19}]
And the day when the enemies of God shall be gathered together into the fire, marshalled
along;

\noindent[\textbf{41:20}]
until when they come to it, their hearing and their eyesight and their skins shall bear
witness against them of that which they have done.

\noindent[\textbf{41:21}]
And they shall say to their skins, 'Why have ye borne witness against us?' they shall say,
'God gave us speech who has given speech to everything; He created you at first, and unto Him
shall ye be returned;

\noindent[\textbf{41:22}]
and ye could not conceal yourselves that your hearing and your eyesight should not be
witness against you, nor your skins; but ye thought that God did not know much of what ye do.

\noindent[\textbf{41:23}]
And that thought of yours which ye thought concerning your Lord has destroyed you, and
ye have now become of those who lose'!'

\noindent[\textbf{41:24}]
And if they are patient, still the fire is a resort for them; and if they ask for favour again,
they shall not be taken into favour.

\noindent[\textbf{41:25}]
We will allot to them mates, for they have made seemly to them what was before them
and what was behind them; and due against them was the sentence on the nations who passed
away before them; both of ginns and of mankind; verily, they were the losers!

\noindent[\textbf{41:26}]
Those who misbelieve say, 'Listen not to this Qur'an, but talk foolishly about it, haply ye
may gain the upper hand.'

\noindent[\textbf{41:27}]
But we will make those who misbelieve taste keen torment; and we will recompense
them with the worst of that which they have done.

\noindent[\textbf{41:28}]
That is, the recompense of the enemies of God,--- the fire! for them is an eternal abode
therein: a recompense for that they did gainsay our signs.

\noindent[\textbf{41:29}]
And those who misbelieved say, 'Our Lord, show us those who have led us astray
amongst the ginns and mankind; we will place them beneath our feet, and they shall both be
amongst those who are put down!'

\noindent[\textbf{41:30}]
Verily, those who say, 'Our Lord is God,' and then go straight, the angels descend upon
them --- 'fear not and be not grieved, but receive the glad tidings of Paradise which ye were
promised;

\noindent[\textbf{41:31}]
we are your patrons in the life of this world and in the next, and ye shall have therein
what your souls desire, and ye shall have therein what ye call for,---

\noindent[\textbf{41:32}]
an entertainment from the forgiving, the merciful!'

\noindent[\textbf{41:33}]
And who speaks better than he who calls to God and does right, and says, 'Verily, I am of
those resigned?'

\noindent[\textbf{41:34}]
Good and evil shall not be deemed alike; repel (evil) with what is best, and lo! He
between whom and thyself was enmity is as though he were a warm patron.

\noindent[\textbf{41:35}]
But none shall meet with it save those who are patient; and none shall meet with it save
those who are endowed with mighty good fortune.

\noindent[\textbf{41:36}]
And if an incitement from the devil incites you, then seek refuge in God; verily, He both
hears and knows.

\noindent[\textbf{41:37}]
And of His signs are the night and the day, and the sun and the moon. Adore ye not the
sun, neither the moon; but adore God who created you, if it be Him ye serve.

\noindent[\textbf{41:38}]
But if they be too big with pride --- yet those who are with thy Lord celebrate His praises
by night and day, and they are never weary.

\noindent[\textbf{41:39}]
And of His signs (is this), that thou mayest see the earth drooping, and when we send
down water upon it it stirs and swells; verily, He who quickens it will surely quicken the dead;
verily, He is mighty over all.

\noindent[\textbf{41:40}]
Verily, those who are inclined to oppose our signs are not hidden from us. Is he who is
cast into the fire better, or he who comes safe on the resurrection day? Do what ye will: verily,
He on what ye do doth look.

\noindent[\textbf{41:41}]
Verily, those who misbelieve in the reminder when it comes to them --- and, verily, it is a
glorious Book!

\noindent[\textbf{41:42}]
falsehood shall not come to it, from before it, nor from behind it --- a revelation from the
wise, the praiseworthy One.

\noindent[\textbf{41:43}]
Naught is said to thee but what was said to the apostles before thee, 'Verily, thy Lord is
Lord of forgiveness and Lord of grievous torment!'

\noindent[\textbf{41:44}]
And had we made it a foreign Qur'an, they would have said, 'Unless its signs be detailed.
...What! foreign and Arabic?' Say, 'It is, for those who believe, a guidance and a healing. But
those who believe not, in their ears is dullness, and it is blindness to them; these are called to
from a far-off place.

\noindent[\textbf{41:45}]
And we gave Moses the Book, and it was disputed about; but had it not been for thy
Lord's word already passed it would have been decided between them, for, verily, they were in
hesitating doubt thereon.

\noindent[\textbf{41:46}]
Whoso does right it is for his soul, and whoso does evil it is against it, for thy Lord is not
unjust towards His servants.

\noindent[\textbf{41:47}]
To Him is referred the knowledge of the Hour: and no fruits come forth from their husks,
and no female conceives, or is delivered, save with His knowledge. And the day when He shall
call to them, 'Where are the partners ye did join with me?'
