livelihood in the life of this world, and we exalt some of them above others in degrees, that some
may take others into subjection; but the mercy of thy Lord is better than that which they amass.

\noindent[\textbf{43:33}]
And but that men would then have been one nation, we would have made for those who
misbelieve in the Merciful One roofs of silver for their houses, and steps up thereto which they
might mount;

\noindent[\textbf{43:34}]
and to their houses doors, and bedsteads on which they might recline;

\noindent[\textbf{43:35}]
and gilding,--- for, verily, all that is a provision of the life of this world, but the hereafter
is better with thy Lord for those who fear!

\noindent[\textbf{43:36}]
And whosoever turns from the reminder of the Merciful One, we will chain to him a
devil, who shall be his mate;

\noindent[\textbf{43:37}]
and, verily these shall turn them from the path while they reckon that they are guided;

\noindent[\textbf{43:38}]
until when he comes to us he shall say, 'O, would that between me and thee there were
the distance of the two orients, for an evil mate (art thou)!'

\noindent[\textbf{43:39}]
But it shall not avail you on that day, since ye were unjust; verily, in the torment shall ye
share!

\noindent[\textbf{43:40}]
What! canst thou make the deaf to hear, or guide the blind, or him who is in obvious
error?

\noindent[\textbf{43:41}]
Whether then we take thee off we will surely take vengeance on them;

\noindent[\textbf{43:42}]
or whether we show thee that which we have promised them; for, verily, we have power
over them.

\noindent[\textbf{43:43}]
Say, 'Dost thou hold to what is inspired thee?' verily, thou art in the right way,

\noindent[\textbf{43:44}]
and, verily, it is a reminder to thee and to thy people, but in the end they shall be asked.

\noindent[\textbf{43:45}]
And ask those whom we have sent before thee amongst the prophets, 'Did we make gods
beside the Merciful One for them to serve?'

\noindent[\textbf{43:46}]
We did send Moses with our signs to Pharaoh and his chiefs, and he said, 'Verily, I am
the apostle of the Lord of the worlds;

\noindent[\textbf{43:47}]
but when he came to them with our signs, lo, they laughed at them!'

\noindent[\textbf{43:48}]
And we did not show them a sign, but it was greater than its fellow; and we seized them
with the torment, haply they might turn.

\noindent[\textbf{43:49}]
And they said, 'O thou magician! pray for us to thy Lord, as He has engaged with thee:
verily, we are guided.'

\noindent[\textbf{43:50}]
And when we removed from them the torment, behold they broke their word.

\noindent[\textbf{43:51}]
And Pharaoh proclaimed amongst his people; said he, 'O my people! is not the kingdom
of Egypt mine? and these rivers that flow beneath me? What! can ye then not see?

\noindent[\textbf{43:52}]
Am I better than this fellow, who is contemptible, who can hardly explain himself?

\noindent[\textbf{43:53}]
Unless then bracelets of gold be cast upon him, or there come with him angels as his
mates ... !'

\noindent[\textbf{43:54}]
And he taught his people levity; and they obeyed him: verily, they were an abominable
people.

\noindent[\textbf{43:55}]
And when they had annoyed us we took vengeance on them, and we drowned them all
together,

\noindent[\textbf{43:56}]
and we made them a precedent and an example to those after them.

\noindent[\textbf{43:57}]
And when the son of Mary was set forth as a parable, behold thy people turned away
from him

\noindent[\textbf{43:58}]
and said, 'Are our gods better, or is he?' They did not set it forth to thee save for
wrangling. Nay, but they are a contentious people.

\noindent[\textbf{43:59}]
He is but a servant whom we have been gracious to, and we have made him an example
for the children of Israel.

\noindent[\textbf{43:60}]
And if we please we can make of you angels in the earth to succeed you.

\noindent[\textbf{43:61}]
And, verily, he is a sign of the Hour. Doubt not then concerning it, but follow this right
way;

\noindent[\textbf{43:62}]
and let not the devil turn you away; verily, he is to you an open foe!

\noindent[\textbf{43:63}]
And when Jesus came with manifest signs he said, 'I am come to you with wisdom, and I
will explain to you something of that whereon ye did dispute, then fear God, obey me;

\noindent[\textbf{43:64}]
verily, God, He is my Lord and your Lord, serve Him then, this is the right way.'

\noindent[\textbf{43:65}]
But the confederates disputed amongst themselves; and woe to those who are unjust from
the torment of a grievous day!

\noindent[\textbf{43:66}]
Do they expect aught but that the Hour will come upon them suddenly while they do not
perceive?

\noindent[\textbf{43:67}]
Friends on that day shall be foes to each other, save those who fear.

\noindent[\textbf{43:68}]
O my servants! there is no fear for you on that day; nor shall ye be grieved

\noindent[\textbf{43:69}]
who believe in our signs and who are resigned.

\noindent[\textbf{43:70}]
Enter ye into Paradise, ye and your wives, happy!

\noindent[\textbf{43:71}]
Dishes of gold and pitchers shall be sent round to them; therein is what souls desire, and
eyes shall be delighted, and ye therein shall dwell for aye;

\noindent[\textbf{43:72}]
for that is Paradise which ye are given as an inheritance for that which ye have done.

\noindent[\textbf{43:73}]
Therein shall ye have much fruit whereof to eat.

\noindent[\textbf{43:74}]
Verily, the sinners are in the torment of hell to dwell for aye.

\noindent[\textbf{43:75}]
It shall not be intermitted for them, and they therein shall be confused.

\noindent[\textbf{43:76}]
We have not wronged them, but it was themselves they wronged.

\noindent[\textbf{43:77}]
And they shall cry out, 'O Malik! let thy lord make an end of us;' he shall say, 'Verily, ye
are to tarry here.'

\noindent[\textbf{43:78}]
We have brought you the truth, but most of you are averse from the truth.

\noindent[\textbf{43:79}]
Have they arranged the affair? then will we arrange it too!

\noindent[\textbf{43:80}]
Or do they reckon that we did not hear their secrets and their whispering? Nay, but our
messengers are with them writing down.

\noindent[\textbf{43:81}]
Say, 'If the Merciful One has a son then am I the first to worship him.

\noindent[\textbf{43:82}]
Celebrated be the praise of the Lord of the heavens and the earth! the Lord of the throne,
above all they attribute to Him!

\noindent[\textbf{43:83}]
But leave them to ponder and to play until they meet that day of theirs which they are
promised.

\noindent[\textbf{43:84}]
He it is who is in the heaven a God and in the earth a God! and He is the wise, the
knowing.

\noindent[\textbf{43:85}]
And blessed be he whose is the kingdom of the heavens and the earth, and what is
between both, and His is the knowledge of the Hour, and unto Him shall ye be brought back!

\noindent[\textbf{43:86}]
And those they call on beside Him shall not possess intercession except those only who
bear witness for the truth and who do know.

\noindent[\textbf{43:87}]
And if thou shouldst ask them who created them they shall surely say, 'God!' How then
can they lie?

\noindent[\textbf{43:88}]
And what he says, 'O Lord, verily, these are a people who do not believe;

\noindent[\textbf{43:89}]
shun them then and say, "Peace!" for they at length shall know!'

\section{Surah 22: The Chapter of the Pilgrimage (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{22:52}]
We have not sent before thee any apostle or prophet, but that when he wished, Satan
threw not something into his wish; but God annuls what Satan throws; then does God confirm his
signs, and God is knowing, wise ---

\noindent[\textbf{22:53}]
to make what Satan throws a trial unto those in whose hearts is sickness, and those whose
hearts are hard; and, verily, the wrong --- doers are in a wide schism ---

\noindent[\textbf{22:54}]
and that those who have been given 'the knowledge' may know that it is the truth from
thy Lord, and may believe therein, and that their hearts may be lowly; for, verily, God surely will
guide those who believe into a right way.

\noindent[\textbf{22:55}]
But those who misbelieve will not cease to be in doubt thereof until the Hour comes on
them suddenly, or there comes on them the torment of the barren day.

\noindent[\textbf{22:56}]
The kingdom on that day shall be God's, He shall judge between them; and those who
believe and do aright shall be in gardens of pleasure,

\noindent[\textbf{22:57}]
but those who misbelieve and say our signs are lies, these --- for them is shameful woe.

\section{Surah 44: The Chapter of Smoke (Mecca).}

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{44:1}]
HA MIM.

\noindent[\textbf{44:2}]
By the perspicuous Book!

\noindent[\textbf{44:3}]
verily, we have sent it down on a blessed night;--- verily, we had given warning ---

\noindent[\textbf{44:4}]
wherein is decided every wise affair,

\noindent[\textbf{44:5}]
as an order from us. Verily, we were sending (apostles)---

\noindent[\textbf{44:6}]
a mercy from thy Lord; verily, He both hears and knows:

\noindent[\textbf{44:7}]
from the Lord of the heavens and the earth and what is between the two, if ye were but
sure.

\noindent[\textbf{44:8}]
There is no god but He, He quickens and He kills --- your Lord and the Lord of your
fathers of yore!

\noindent[\textbf{44:9}]
Nay, they in doubt do play!

\noindent[\textbf{44:10}]
But expect thou the day when the heaven shall bring obvious smoke

\noindent[\textbf{44:11}]
to cover men --- this is grievous torment!

\noindent[\textbf{44:12}]
Our Lord! remove from us the torment; verily, we are believers.

\noindent[\textbf{44:13}]
How can they have the reminder (now), when they have had a plain apostle,

\noindent[\textbf{44:14}]
and when they turned their backs away from him and said, 'Taught! mad!'

\noindent[\textbf{44:15}]
Verily, we will remove the torment a little, (but) ye will surely return!

\noindent[\textbf{44:16}]
On the day when we will assault with the great assault, verily, we will take vengeance.

\noindent[\textbf{44:17}]
And we already tried the people of Pharaoh when there came to them a noble apostle:

\noindent[\textbf{44:18}]
'Send back to me God's servants; verily, I am to you a faithful apostle;'

\noindent[\textbf{44:19}]
and, 'Exalt not yourselves above God; verily, I come to you with obvious authority.

\noindent[\textbf{44:20}]
And, verily, I seek refuge in my Lord and your Lord, that ye stone me not.

\noindent[\textbf{44:21}]
And if ye believe not in me then let me alone!'

\noindent[\textbf{44:22}]
Then he called upon his Lord, 'Verily, these are a sinful people.'

\noindent[\textbf{44:23}]
So journey with my servants by night --- verily, ye will be pursued.

\noindent[\textbf{44:24}]
But leave the sea in quiet --- verily, they are a host to be drowned!

\noindent[\textbf{44:25}]
How many gardens and springs have they left,

\noindent[\textbf{44:26}]
and corn lands and a noble place,

\noindent[\textbf{44:27}]
and comfort wherein they did enjoy themselves!

\noindent[\textbf{44:28}]
Thus --- and we gave them for an inheritance to another people.

\noindent[\textbf{44:29}]
And the heaven wept not for them, nor the earth, nor were they respited.

\noindent[\textbf{44:30}]
But we saved the children of Israel from shameful woe!---

\noindent[\textbf{44:31}]
--- from Pharaoh; verily, he was haughty, one of the extravagant!

\noindent[\textbf{44:32}]
And we did choose them, wittingly, above the worlds;

\noindent[\textbf{44:33}]
and we gave them signs wherein was an obvious trial!

\noindent[\textbf{44:34}]
Verily, these say,

\noindent[\textbf{44:35}]
'It is but our first death,

\noindent[\textbf{44:36}]
so bring our fathers, if ye do speak the truth!'

\noindent[\textbf{44:37}]
Are they better than the people of Tubbah, and those before them? We destroyed them ---
verily, they were sinners!

\noindent[\textbf{44:38}]
Nor did we create the heavens and the earth, and what is between the two in sport:

\noindent[\textbf{44:39}]
we did but create them in truth, though most of them know it not!

\noindent[\textbf{44:40}]
Verily, the day of separation is their appointed term;

\noindent[\textbf{44:41}]
the day when master shall not avail client at all, nor shall they be helped;

\noindent[\textbf{44:42}]
save whomsoever God shall have mercy on; verily, He is the mighty, the merciful!

\noindent[\textbf{44:43}]
Verily, the Zaqqum tree

\noindent[\textbf{44:44}]
(shall be) the food of the sinful:

\noindent[\textbf{44:45}]
as it were melting, shall it boil in their bellies

\noindent[\textbf{44:46}]
like the boiling of hot water!---

\noindent[\textbf{44:47}]
'Take him and hale him into the midst of hell!

\noindent[\textbf{44:48}]
then pour over his head the torment of hot water!---

\noindent[\textbf{44:49}]
Taste! verily, thou art the mighty, the honourable!

\noindent[\textbf{44:50}]
Verily, this is that whereon ye did dispute!'

\noindent[\textbf{44:51}]
Verily, the pious shall be in a safe place!

\noindent[\textbf{44:52}]
in gardens and springs,

\noindent[\textbf{44:53}]
they shall be clad in satin and stout silk face to face.

\noindent[\textbf{44:54}]
Thus!--- and we will wed them to bright and large-eyed maids!

\noindent[\textbf{44:55}]
They shall call therein for every fruit in safety.

\noindent[\textbf{44:56}]
They shall not taste therein of death save their first death, and we will keep them from the
torment of hell!

\noindent[\textbf{44:57}]
Grace from thy Lord, that is the grand bliss!

\noindent[\textbf{44:58}]
And we have only made it easy for thy tongue, that haply they may be mindful.

\noindent[\textbf{44:59}]
Then watch thou; verily, they are watching too!

\clearpage
\chapter{Session 6}


\section{Surah 28: The Chapter of the Story (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{28:44}]
Thou wast not upon the western side when we decided for Moses, but afar off; nor wast
thou of the witnesses.

\noindent[\textbf{28:45}]
But we raised up (other) generations, and life was prolonged for them; and thou wast not
staying amidst the people of Midian, reciting to them our signs; but we were sending our
apostles.

\noindent[\textbf{28:46}]
Nor wast thou by the side of the mountain when we called; but it is a mercy from thy
Lord, that thou mayest warn a people to whom no warner has come before thee; haply they may
be mindful!

\noindent[\textbf{28:47}]
And lest there should befall them a mishap for what their hands have sent before, and
they should say, 'Our Lord! why didst thou not send to us an apostle? for we would have
followed thy signs and been of the believers.'

\noindent[\textbf{28:48}]
And when the truth comes to them from us they say, 'We are given the like of what
Moses was given.' Did they not disbelieve in what Moses was given before?--- they say, 'Two
works of sorcery back up each other;' and they say, 'Verily, we do disbelieve in all.'

\noindent[\textbf{28:49}]
Say, 'Bring, then, a book from God which shall be a better guide than both, and I will
follow it, if ye do tell the truth!'

\noindent[\textbf{28:50}]
And if they cannot answer thee, then know that they follow their own lusts; and who is
more in error than he who follows his own lust without guidance from God? verily, God guides
not an unjust people!

\noindent[\textbf{28:51}]
And we caused the word to reach them, haply they may be mindful!

\noindent[\textbf{28:52}]
Those to whom we gave the Book before it, they believe therein;

\noindent[\textbf{28:53}]
and when it is recited to them they say, 'We believe in it as truth from our Lord; verily,
we were resigned before it came!'

\noindent[\textbf{28:54}]
These shall be given their hire twice over, for that they were patient, and repelled evil
with good, and of what we have bestowed upon them give alms.

\noindent[\textbf{28:55}]
And when they hear vain talk, they turn away from it and say, 'We have our works, and
ye have your works. Peace be upon you! we do not seek the ignorant!'

\noindent[\textbf{28:56}]
Verily, thou canst not guide whom thou dost like, but God guides whom He pleases; for
He knows best who are to be guided.

\section{Surah 5: The Chapter of the Table (Medinah).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{5:11}]
O ye who believe! remember God's favour towards you, when a people intended to
stretch their hands against you, but He withheld their hands from you; and upon God let
believers rely.

\noindent[\textbf{5:12}]
God did take a compact from the children of Israel, and raised up of them twelve
wardens; and God said, 'Verily, I am with you, if ye be steadfast in prayer, and give alms, and
believe in my apostles, and assist them, and lend to God a goodly loan; then will I cover your
offences and make you enter gardens beneath which rivers flow: and whoso disbelieves after
that, he hath erred from the level way.'

\noindent[\textbf{5:13}]
And for that they broke their compact, we cursed them, and placed in their hearts
hardness, so that they perverted the words from their places, and forgot a portion of what they
were reminded of. But thou wilt not cease to light upon treachery amongst them, save a few of
them; but pardon them and shun them; verily, God loves the kind.

\noindent[\textbf{5:14}]
And of those who say, 'Verily, we are Christians,' we have taken a compact; but they
have forgotten a portion of what they were reminded of; wherefore have we excited amongst
them enmity and hatred till the resurrection day; but God will tell them of what they have done.

\noindent[\textbf{5:15}]
O ye people of the Book! our Apostle has come to you to explain to you much of what ye
had hidden of the Book, and to pardon much. There has come to you from God a light, and a
perspicuous Book;

\noindent[\textbf{5:16}]
God guides thereby those who follow His pleasure to the way of peace, and brings them
into a right way.

\noindent[\textbf{5:17}]
They misbelieve who say, 'Verily, God is the Messiah the son of Mary;' say, 'Who has
any hold on God, if he wished to destroy the Messiah the son of Mary, and his mother, and those
who are on earth altogether?' God's is the kingdom of the heavens and the earth and what is
between the two; He createth what He will, for God is mighty over all!

\noindent[\textbf{5:18}]
But the Jews and the Christians say, 'We are the sons of God and His beloved.' Say,
'Why then does He punish you for your sins? nay, ye are mortals of those whom He has created!
He pardons whom He pleases, and punishes whom He pleases; for God's is the kingdom of the
heavens and the earth, and what is between the two, and unto Him the journey is.'

\noindent[\textbf{5:19}]
O people of the Book! our Apostle has come to you, explaining to you the interval of
apostles; lest ye say, 'There came not to us a herald of glad tidings nor a warner.' But there has
come to you now a herald of glad tidings and a warner, and God is mighty over all!

\noindent[\textbf{5:20}]
When Moses said to his people, 'O my people! remember the favour of God towards you
when He made amongst you prophets, and made for you kings, and brought you what never was
brought to anybody in the worlds.

\noindent[\textbf{5:21}]
O my people! enter the Holy Land which God has prescribed for you; and be ye not
thrust back upon your hinder parts and retreat losers.

\noindent[\textbf{5:22}]
They said, ' O Moses! verily, therein is a people, giants; and we will surely not enter
therein until they go out from thence; but if they go out then we will enter in.'

\noindent[\textbf{5:23}]
Then said two men of those who fear, --- God had been gracious to them both, --- 'Enter
ye upon them by the door, and when ye have entered it, verily, ye shall be victorious; and upon
God do ye rely if ye be believers.'

\noindent[\textbf{5:24}]
They said, 'O Moses! we shall never enter it so long as they are therein; so, go thou and
thy Lord and fight ye twain; verily, we will sit down here.'

\noindent[\textbf{5:25}]
Said he, 'My Lord, verily, I can control only myself and my brother; therefore part us
from these sinful people.'

\noindent[\textbf{5:26}]
He said, 'Then, verily, it is forbidden them; for forty years shall they wander about in the
earth; so vex not thyself for the sinful people.'

\noindent[\textbf{5:27}]
Recite to them the story of the two sons of Adam; truly when they offered an offering and
it was accepted from one of them, and was not accepted from the other, that one said, 'I will
surely kill thee;' he said, 'God only accepts from those who fear.

\noindent[\textbf{5:28}]
If thou dost stretch forth to me thine hand to kill me, I will not stretch forth mine hand to
kill thee; verily, I fear God the Lord of the worlds;

\noindent[\textbf{5:29}]
verily, I wish that thou mayest draw upon thee my sin and thy sin, and be of the fellows
of the Fire, for that is the reward of the unjust.'

\noindent[\textbf{5:30}]
But his soul allowed him to slay his brother, and he slew him, and in the morning he was
of those who lose.

\noindent[\textbf{5:31}]
And God sent a crow to scratch in the earth and show him how he might hide his
brother's shame, he said, ' Alas, for me! Am I too helpless to become like this crow and hide my
brother's shame?' and in the morning he was of those who did repent.

\noindent[\textbf{5:32}]
For this cause have we prescribed to the children of Israel that whoso kills a soul, unless
it be for another soul or for violence in the land, it is as though he had killed men altogether; but
whoso saves one, it is as though he saved men altogether. Our apostles came to them with
manifest signs; then, verily, many of them did after that commit excesses in the earth.

\noindent[\textbf{5:33}]
The reward of those who make war against God and His Apostle, and strive after
violence in the earth, is only that they shall be slaughtered or crucified, or their hands cut off and
their feet on alternate sides, or that they shall be banished from the land; --- that is a disgrace for
them in this world, and for them in the next is mighty woe;

\noindent[\textbf{5:34}]
save for those who repent before ye have them in your power, for know ye that God is
forgiving, merciful.

\noindent[\textbf{5:35}]
O ye who believe! fear God and crave the means to approach Him, and be strenuous in
His way, haply ye will prosper then.

\noindent[\textbf{5:36}]
Verily, those who disbelieve, even though they had what is in the earth, all of it, and the
like thereof with it, to offer as a ransom from the punishment of the resurrection day, it would
not be accepted from them; but for them is grievous woe.

\noindent[\textbf{5:37}]
They may wish to go forth from the Fire, but they shall not go forth therefrom, for them
is lasting woe.

\noindent[\textbf{5:38}]
The man thief and the woman thief, cut off the hands of both as a punishment, for that
they have erred; --- an example from God, for God is mighty, wise.

\noindent[\textbf{5:39}]
But whoso turns again after his injustice and acts aright, verily, God will turn to him, for,
verily, God is forgiving, merciful.

\noindent[\textbf{5:40}]
Do ye not know that God, His is the kingdom of the heavens and the earth; He punishes
whom He pleases, and forgives whom He pleases, for God is mighty overall?

\noindent[\textbf{5:41}]
O thou Apostle! let not those grieve thee who vie in misbelief; or those who say with
their mouths 'We believe,' but their hearts do not believe; or of those who are Jews, listeners to a
lie, --- listeners to other people, but who come not to thee. They pervert the words from their
places and say, ' If this is what ye are given, take it; but if ye are not given it, then beware!' but
he whom God wishes to mislead, thou canst do nothing with God for him; these are those whose
hearts God wishes not to purify, for them in this world is disgrace, and for them in the next is
mighty woe, ---

\noindent[\textbf{5:42}]
listeners to a lie, eaters of unlawful things! But if they come to thee, then judge between
them or turn aside from them; but if thou turnest aside from them they shall not harm thee at all,
but if thou judgest, then judge between them with justice, verily, God loves the just.

\noindent[\textbf{5:43}]
But how should they make thee their judge, when they have the law wherein is God's
judgment? Yet they turn back after that, for they do not believe.

\noindent[\textbf{5:44}]
Verily, we have revealed the law in which is guidance and light; the prophets who we're
resigned did judge thereby those who were Jews, as did the masters and doctors by what they
remembered of the Book of God and by what they were witnesses of. Fear not men, but fear me,
and sell not my signs for a little price; for whoso will not judge by what God has revealed, these
be the misbelievers.

\noindent[\textbf{5:45}]
We have prescribed for thee therein 'a life for a life, and an eye for an eye, and a nose for
a nose, and an ear for an ear, and a tooth for a tooth, and for wounds retaliation;' but whoso
remits it, it is an expiation for him, but he whoso will not judge by what God has revealed, these
be the unjust.

\noindent[\textbf{5:46}]
And we followed up the footsteps of these (prophets) with Jesus the son of Mary,
confirming that which was before him and the law, and we brought him the gospel, wherein is
guidance and light, verifying what was before it of the law, and a guidance and an admonition
unto those who fear.

\noindent[\textbf{5:47}]
Then let the people of the gospel judge by that which is revealed therein, for whoso will
not judge by what God has revealed, these be the evildoers.

\noindent[\textbf{5:48}]
We have revealed to thee the Book in truth verifying what was before it, and preserving
it; judge then between them by what God has revealed, and follow not their lusts, turning away
from what is given to thee of the truth. For each one of you have we made a law and a pathway;
and had God pleased He would have made you one nation, but He will surely try you concerning
that which He has brought you. Be ye therefore emulous in good deeds; to God is your return
altogether, and He will let you know concerning that wherein ye do dispute.

\noindent[\textbf{5:49}]
Wherefore judge thou between them by what God has revealed, and follow not their
lusts; but beware lest they mislead thee from part of what God has revealed to thee; yet if they
turn back, then know that God wishes to fall on them for some sins of theirs, --- verily, many
men are evildoers.

\noindent[\textbf{5:50}]
Is it the judgment of the Ignorance they crave? but who is better than God to judge for
people who are sure?

\noindent[\textbf{5:51}]
O ye who believe! take not the Jews and Christians for your patrons : they are patrons of
each other; but whoso amongst you takes them for patrons, verily, he is of them, and, verily, God
guides not an unjust people.

\noindent[\textbf{5:52}]
Thou wilt see those in whose hearts is a sickness vieing with them; they say, 'We fear lest
there befall us a reverse.' It may be God will give the victory, or an order from Himself, and they
may awake repenting of what they thought in secret to themselves.

\noindent[\textbf{5:53}]
Those who believe say, 'Are these they who swore by God with their most strenuous oath
that they were surely with you?' --- their works are in vain and they shall wake the losers.

\noindent[\textbf{5:54}]
O ye who believe! whoso is turned away from his religion --- God will bring (instead) a
people whom He loves and who love Him, lowly to believers, lofty to unbelievers, strenuous in
the way of God, fearing not the blame of him who blames. That is God's grace! He gives it unto
whom He pleases, for God both comprehends and knows.

\noindent[\textbf{5:55}]
God only is your patron, and His Apostle and those who believe, who are steadfast in
prayer and give alms, bowing down.

\noindent[\textbf{5:56}]
Whoso taketh as patrons God and His apostles and those who believe; --- verily, God's
crew, they are victorious!

\noindent[\textbf{5:57}]
O ye who believe! take not for patrons those who take your religion for a jest or a sport,
from amongst those who have been given the Book before and the misbelievers; but fear God if
ye be believers.

\noindent[\textbf{5:58}]
Nor those who, when ye call to prayer, take it for a jest and a sport; that is because they
are a people who do not understand.

\noindent[\textbf{5:59}]
Say, 'O people of the Book! do ye disavow us, for aught but that we believe in God, and
what was revealed to us before, and for that most of you are evildoers?'

\noindent[\textbf{5:60}]
Say, 'Can I declare unto you something worse than retribution from God?' Whomsoever
God has cursed and been wroth with --- and he has made of them apes and swine --- and who
worship Taghut, they are in a worse plight and are more erring from the level path.

\noindent[\textbf{5:61}]
When they come to you they say, 'We believe;' but they entered in with unbelief, and
they went out therewith, and God knows best what they did hide.

\noindent[\textbf{5:62}]
Thou wilt see many of them vieing in sin and enmity, and in eating unlawful things, ---
evil is it that they have done.

\noindent[\textbf{5:63}]
The masters and their doctors prohibit them from speaking sin and eating unlawful
things, --- evil is what they have performed.

\noindent[\textbf{5:64}]
The Jews say, --- 'God's hand is fettered;' their hands are fettered and they are cursed for
what they said; nay! His hands are outspread, He expends how He pleases! and that which has
been sent down to thee from thy Lord will surely increase many of them in their rebellion and
misbelief, for we have cast amongst them enmity and hatred till the resurrection day. Whenever
they light a fire for war, God puts it out; they strive for corruption in the earth, but God loves not
the corrupt.

\noindent[\textbf{5:65}]
But did the people of the Book believe and fear, we would cover their offences, and we
would make them enter into gardens of pleasure;

\noindent[\textbf{5:66}]
and were they steadfast in the law and the gospel, and what has been sent down to them
from their Lord, they should eat from above them and below them. Amongst them are a nation
who are moderate, but many of them --- bad is what they do.

\noindent[\textbf{5:67}]
O thou Apostle! preach what has been revealed to thee from thy Lord; if thou do it not
thou hast not preached His message, and God will not hold thee free from men; for God guides
not people who misbelieve.

\noindent[\textbf{5:68}]
Say, 'O people of the Book! ye rest on naught until ye stand fast by the law and the
gospel, and what is revealed to you from your Lord.' But what has been revealed to thee from
thy Lord will of a surety increase many of them in rebellion and misbelief, vex not thyself then
for a people who misbelieve.

\noindent[\textbf{5:69}]
Verily, those who believe and those who are Jews, and the Sabaeans, and the Christians,
whosoever believes in God and the last day, and does what is right, there is no fear for them, nor
shall they grieve.

\noindent[\textbf{5:70}]
We took a compact of the children of Israel, and we sent to them apostles; every time
there came to them an apostle with what their souls loved not, a part of them they did call liars
and a part of them they slew.

\noindent[\textbf{5:71}]
And they reckoned that there would be no disturbance; but they were blind and deaf! and
then God turned again towards them: and then many amongst them were blind and deaf! but God
saw what they did.

\noindent[\textbf{5:72}]
They misbelieve who say, 'Verily, God is the Messiah the son of Mary;' but the Messiah
said, 'O children of Israel! worship God, my Lord and your Lord;' verily, he who associates
aught with God, God hath forbidden him Paradise, and his resort is the Fire, and the unjust shall
have none to help them.

\noindent[\textbf{5:73}]
They misbelieve who say, 'Verily, God is the third of three;' for there is no God but one,
and if they do not desist from what they say, there shall touch those who misbelieve amongst
them grievous woe.

\noindent[\textbf{5:74}]
Will they not turn again towards God and ask pardon of Him? for God is forgiving and
merciful.

\noindent[\textbf{5:75}]
The Messiah the son of Mary is only a prophet: prophets before him have passed away;
and his mother was a confessor; they used both to eat food. --- See how we explain to them the
signs, yet see how they turn aside!

\noindent[\textbf{5:76}]
Say, 'Will ye serve, other than God, what can neither hurt you nor profit you?' but God,
He both hears and knows.

\noindent[\textbf{5:77}]
Say, 'O people of the Book! exceed not the truth in your religion, and follow not the lusts
of a people who have erred before, and who lead many astray, and who go away from the level
path.'

\noindent[\textbf{5:78}]
Those of the children of Israel who disbelieved were cursed by the tongue of David and
Jesus the son of Mary; that is because they rebelled and did transgress;

\noindent[\textbf{5:79}]
they would not desist from the wrong they did; evil is that which they did.

\noindent[\textbf{5:80}]
Thou wilt see many of them taking those who disbelieve for their patrons; evil is that
which their souls have sent before them, for God's wrath is on them, and in the torment shall
they dwell for aye.

\noindent[\textbf{5:81}]
But had they believed in God and the prophet, and what was revealed to him, they had
not taken these for their patrons; but many of them are evildoers.

\noindent[\textbf{5:82}]
Thou wilt surely find that the strongest in enmity against those who believe are the Jews
and the idolaters; and thou wilt find the nearest in love to those who believe to be those who say,
'We are Christians;' that is because there are amongst them priests and monks, and because they
are not proud.

\noindent[\textbf{5:83}]
And when they hear what has been revealed to the prophet, you will see their eyes gush
with tears at what they recognise as truth therein; and they will say, 'O our Lord! we believe, so
write us down amongst the witnesses.

\noindent[\textbf{5:84}]
Why should we not believe in God and the truth that is given to us, nor desire that our
Lord should make us enter with the upright people?'

\section{Surah 16: The Chapter of the Bee (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{16:98}]
When thou dost read the Qur'an, ask refuge with God from Satan the pelted one.

\noindent[\textbf{16:99}]
Verily, he has no power over those who believe and who upon their Lord rely.

\noindent[\textbf{16:100}]
His power is only over those who take him for a patron, and over the idolaters.

\noindent[\textbf{16:101}]
And whenever we change one verse for another,--- God knows best what He sends down.
They say, 'Thou art but a forger!' --- Nay, most of them do not know.

\noindent[\textbf{16:102}]
Say, 'The Holy Spirit brought it down from thy Lord in truth, to stablish those who
believe, and for a guidance and glad tidings to those who are resigned.'

\noindent[\textbf{16:103}]
We knew that they said, 'It is only some mortal who teaches him.' --- The tongue of him
they lean towards is barbarous, and this is plain Arabic.

\noindent[\textbf{16:104}]
Verily, those who believe not in God's signs, God will not guide them, and for them is
grievous woe.

\noindent[\textbf{16:105}]
Only they are the forgers of a lie who believe not in God's signs; and these, they are the
liars.

\section{Surah 6: The Chapter of Cattle (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)

\noindent[\textbf{6:4}]
There came not to them any sign of the signs of their Lord, but they turned away;

\noindent[\textbf{6:5}]
and they have called the truth a lie now that it has come to them, but there shall come to
them the message of that at which they mocked.

\noindent[\textbf{6:6}]
Do not they see how many a generation we have destroyed before them, whom we had
settled in the earth as we have not settled for you, and sent the rain of heaven upon them in
copious showers, and made the waters flow beneath them? Then we destroyed them in their sins,
and raised up other generations after them.

\noindent[\textbf{6:7}]
Had we sent down to thee a book on paper, and they had touched it with their hands, still
those who misbelieve would have said, 'This is naught but obvious magic.'

\noindent[\textbf{6:8}]
They say, 'Why has not an angel been sent down to him?' but if we had sent down an
angel, the affair would have been decided, and then they would have had no respite.

\noindent[\textbf{6:9}]
And had we made him an angel, we should have made him as a man too; and we would
have made perplexing for them that which they deem perplexing now.

\noindent[\textbf{6:10}]
There have been prophets before thee mocked at, but that encompassed them which the
scoffers among them mocked at.

\section{Surah 17: The Chapter of the Night Journey (Mecca).}

 (selections)

\noindent[\textbf{17:58}]
There is no city but we will destroy it before the day of judgment, or torment it with keen
torment;--- that is in the Book inscribed.

\noindent[\textbf{17:59}]
Naught hindered us from sending thee with signs, save that those of yore said they were
lies; so we gave Thamud the visible she-camel, but they treated her unjustly! for we do not send
(any one) with signs save to make men fear.

\noindent[\textbf{17:60}]
And when we said to thee, 'Verily, thy Lord encompasses men!' and we made the vision
which we showed thee only a cause of sedition unto men, and the cursed tree as well; for we will
frighten them, but it will only increase them in great rebellion.

\noindent[\textbf{17:61}]
And when we said to the angels, ' Adore Adam;' and they adored, save Iblis, who said, '
Am I to adore one whom Thou hast created out of clay?'

\noindent[\textbf{17:62}]
Said he, 'Dost thou see now? this one whom Thou hast honoured above me, verily, if
Thou shouldst respite me until the resurrection day, I will of a surety utterly destroy his seed
except a few.'

\noindent[\textbf{17:63}]
Said He, 'Begone! and whoso of them follows thee --- verily, hell is your recompense, an
ample recompense.

\noindent[\textbf{17:64}]
Entice away whomsoever of them thou canst with thy voice; and bear down upon them
with thy horse and with thy foot; and share with them in their wealth and their children; and
promise them,--- but Satan promises them naught but deceit.

\noindent[\textbf{17:65}]
Verily, my servants, thou hast no authority over them; thy Lord is guardian enough over
them!'

\noindent[\textbf{17:66}]
It is your Lord who drives the ships for you in the sea that ye may seek after plenty from
Him; verily, He is ever merciful to you.

\noindent[\textbf{17:67}]
And when distress touches you in the sea, those whom ye call on, except Him, stray away
from you; but when He has brought you safe to shore, ye turn away; for man is ever ungrateful.

\noindent[\textbf{17:68}]
Are ye sure that He will not cleave with you the side of the shore, or send against you a
heavy sand-storm? then ye will find no guardian for yourselves.

\noindent[\textbf{17:69}]
Or are ye sure that He will not send you back therein another time, and send against you a
violent wind, and drown you for your misbelief? then ye will find for yourselves no protector
against us.

\noindent[\textbf{17:70}]
But, we have been gracious to the children of Adam, and we have borne them by land
and sea, and have provided them with good things, and have preferred them over many that we
have created.

\noindent[\textbf{17:71}]
The day when we will call all men by their high priest; and he whose book is given in his
right hand --- these shall read their book, nor shall they be wronged a straw.

\noindent[\textbf{17:72}]
But he who in this life is blind shall be blind in the next too, and err farther from the way.

\begin{center}
  {\Huge

    \hspace{2em}
    *
    \hspace{2em}
    *
    \hspace{2em}
    *
  }
\end{center}
%TODO use arabic unicode symbols instead?

\noindent[\textbf{17:1}]
Celebrated be the praises of Him who took His servant a journey by night from the
Sacred Mosque, to the Remote Mosque, the precinct of which we have blessed, to show him of
our signs! verily, He both hears and looks.

\section{Surah 15: The Chapter of El 'Hagr (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{15:85}]
We did not create the heavens and the earth and all that is between them both, save in
truth. And, verily, the Hour is surely coming; then do thou pardon with a fair pardon.

\noindent[\textbf{15:86}]
Verily, thy Lord He is the creator, the knowing!

\noindent[\textbf{15:87}]
We have already brought thee Seven of the Repetition, and the mighty Qur'an.

\noindent[\textbf{15:88}]
Let not thine eyes strain after what we have allowed a few pairs of them to enjoy, nor
grieve for them; but lower thy wing to the believers,

\noindent[\textbf{15:89}]
and say, 'Verily, I am an obvious warner.'

\noindent[\textbf{15:90}]
As we sent down (punishment) on the separatists

\noindent[\textbf{15:91}]
who dismember the Qur'an.

\noindent[\textbf{15:92}]
But, by thy Lord! we will question them, one and all,

\noindent[\textbf{15:93}]
about what they have done.

\noindent[\textbf{15:94}]
Therefore, publish what thou art bidden, and turn aside from the idolaters.

\noindent[\textbf{15:95}]
Verily, we are enough for thee against the scoffers.

\noindent[\textbf{15:96}]
Who place with God other gods; but they at length shall know!

\noindent[\textbf{15:97}]
And we knew that thy breast was straitened at what they say.

\noindent[\textbf{15:98}]
Then celebrate the praises of thy Lord, and be thou of those who adore.

\noindent[\textbf{15:99}]
And serve thy Lord until the certainty shall come to thee.

\section{Surah 2: The Chapter of the Heifer (Medina).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{2:275}]
Those who devour usury shall not rise again, save as he riseth whom Satan hath paralysed
with a touch; and that is because they say 'selling is only like usury,' but God has made selling
lawful and usury unlawful; and he to whom the admonition from his Lord has come, if he
desists, what has gone before is his: his matter is in God's hands. But whosoever returns (to
usury) these are the fellows of the Fire, and they shall dwell therein for aye.

\noindent[\textbf{2:276}]
God shall blot out usury, but shall make almsgiving profitable, for God loves not any
sinful misbeliever.

\noindent[\textbf{2:277}]
Verily, those who believe, and act righteously, and are steadfast in prayer, and give alms,
theirs is their hire with their Lord; there is no fear on them, nor shall they grieve.

\noindent[\textbf{2:278}]
O ye who believe! fear God, and remit the balance of usury, if ye be believers;

\noindent[\textbf{2:279}]
and if ye will not do it, then hearken to the proclamation of war from God and His
Apostle; but if ye repent, your capital is yours. Ye shall not wrong, nor shall ye be wronged.

\noindent[\textbf{2:280}]
And if it be one in difficulties, then wait for easy circumstances; but that ye remit it as
alms is better for you, if ye did but know.

\noindent[\textbf{2:281}]
Fear the day wherein ye shall return to God; then shall each soul be paid what it has
earned, and they shall not be wronged.

\section{Surah 38: The Chapter of SAD (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{38:1}]
SAD. By the Qur'an with its reminder!

\noindent[\textbf{38:2}]
nay, but those who misbelieve are in pride, schism!

\noindent[\textbf{38:3}]
How many a generation have we destroyed before them, and they cried out, but it was no
time to escape!

\noindent[\textbf{38:4}]
And they wonder that a warner has come from amongst themselves, and the misbelievers
say, 'This is a magician, a liar!'

\noindent[\textbf{38:5}]
What! does he make the gods to be one God? verily, this is a wondrous thing.

\noindent[\textbf{38:6}]
And the chiefs of them went away: 'Go on and persevere in your gods; this is a thing
designed;

\noindent[\textbf{38:7}]
we never heard this in any other sect; this is nothing but a fiction!

\noindent[\textbf{38:8}]
Has a reminder come down upon him from amongst us?' nay, they are in doubt
concerning my reminder; nay, they have not yet tasted of my torment!

\noindent[\textbf{38:9}]
Have they the treasures of the mercy of thy mighty Lord, the giver?

\noindent[\textbf{38:10}]
or have they the kingdom of the heavens and of the earth, and what is between the
two?--- then let them climb tip the ropes thereof.

\noindent[\textbf{38:11}]
Any host whatever of the confederates shall there be routed.

\section{Surah 46: The Chapter of El A'hqaf (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{46:28}]
Why did not those help them, whom beside God they took for gods that could draw nigh
to Him? Nay! they strayed away from them; for that was their lie and what they had forced.

\noindent[\textbf{46:29}]
And when we turned towards thee some of the ginn listening to the Qur'an, and when
they were present at (the reading of) it, they said, 'Be silent!' and when it was over they turned
back to their people, warning them.

\noindent[\textbf{46:30}]
Said they, 'O our people! verily, we have heard a book sent down after Moses, verifying
what came before it, guiding to the truth, and unto the right way.

\clearpage
\chapter{Session 7}


\section{Surah 60: The Chapter of the Tried (Medinah).}

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{60:1}]
O ye who believe! take not my enemy and your enemy for patrons, encountering them
with love for they misbelieve in the truth that is to come to you; they drive out the Apostle and
you for that ye believe in God your Lord! If ye go forth fighting strenuously in my cause and
craving my good pleasure, and secretly show love for them, yet do I know best what ye conceal
and what ye display! and he of you who does so has erred from the level path.

\noindent[\textbf{60:2}]
If they find you they will be enemies to you, and they will stretch forth against you their
hands and their tongues for evil, and would fain that ye should disbelieve;

\noindent[\textbf{60:3}]
neither your kindred nor your children shall profit you upon the resurrection day; it will
separate you! but God on what ye do doth look!

\noindent[\textbf{60:4}]
Ye had a good example in Abraham and those with him, when they said to their people,
'Verily, we are clear of you and of what ye serve beside God. We disbelieve in you: and between
us and you is enmity and hatred begun for ever, until ye believe in God alone!' But not the
speech of Abraham to his father, 'Verily, I will ask forgiveness for thee, though I cannot control
aught from God!' O our Lord! on thee do we rely! and unto thee we turn! and unto thee the
journey is!

\noindent[\textbf{60:5}]
Our Lord! make us not a trial for those who misbelieve; but forgive us! Our Lord! verily,
thou art mighty, wise!

\noindent[\textbf{60:6}]
Ye had in them a good example for him who would hope in God and the last day. But
whoso turns his back, verily, God, He is rich and to be praised.

\noindent[\textbf{60:7}]
Mayhap that God will place love between you and between those of them ye are hostile
towards: for God is powerful, and God is forgiving, compassionate.

\noindent[\textbf{60:8}]
God forbids you not respecting those who have not fought against you for religion's sake,
and who have not driven you forth from your homes, that ye should act righteously and justly
towards them; verily, God loves the just!

\noindent[\textbf{60:9}]
He only forbids you to make patrons of those who have fought against you for religion's
sake, and driven you forth from your homes, or have aided in your expulsion; and whoever
makes patrons of them, they are the unjust!

\noindent[\textbf{60:10}]
O ye who believe! when there come believing women who have fled, then try them: God
knows their faith. If ye know them to be believers do not send them back to the misbelievers;---
they are not lawful for them, nor are the men lawful for these; --- but give them what they have
expended, and it shall be no crime against you that ye marry them, when ye have given them
their hire. And do not ye retain a right over misbelieving women; but ask for what ye have spent,
and let them ask for what they have spent. That is God's judgment: He judges between you, for
God is knowing, wise!

\noindent[\textbf{60:11}]
And if any of your wives escape from you to the misbelievers, and your turn comes, then
give to those whose wives have gone away the like of what they have spent; and fear God, in
whom it is that ye believe.

\noindent[\textbf{60:12}]
O thou prophet! when believing women come to thee and engage with thee that they will
not associate aught with God, and will not steal, and will not fornicate, and will not kill their
children, and will not bring a calumny which they have forged between their hands and feet, and
that they will not rebel against thee in what is reasonable, then engage with them and ask
forgiveness for them of God;--- verily, God is forgiving, compassionate.

\noindent[\textbf{60:13}]
O ye who believe! take not for patrons a people whom God is wroth against; they despair
of the hereafter, as the misbelievers despair of the fellows of the tombs!

\section{Surah 22: The Chapter of the Pilgrimage (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{22:38}]
Verily, God will defend those who believe; verily, God loves not any misbelieving
traitor.

\noindent[\textbf{22:39}]
Permission is given to those who fight because they have been wronged, --- and, verily,
God to help them has the might,---

\noindent[\textbf{22:40}]
who have been driven forth from their homes undeservedly, only for that they said, 'Our
Lord is God;' and were it not for God's repelling some men with others, cloisters and churches
and synagogues and mosques, wherein God's name is mentioned much, would be destroyed. But
God will surely help him who helps Him; verily, God is powerful, mighty.

\noindent[\textbf{22:41}]
Who, if we establish them in the earth, are steadfast in prayer, and give alms, and bid
what is right, and forbid what is wrong; and God's is the future of affairs.

\noindent[\textbf{22:42}]
But if they call thee liar, the people of Noah called him liar before them, as did 'Ad and
Thamud,

\noindent[\textbf{22:43}]
and the people of Abraham, and the people of Lot,

\noindent[\textbf{22:44}]
and the fellows of Midian; and Moses was called a liar too: but I let the misbelievers
range at large, and then I seized on them, and how great was the change!

\noindent[\textbf{22:45}]
And how many a city have we destroyed while it yet did wrong, and it was turned over
on its roofs, and (how many) a deserted well and lofty palace!

\noindent[\textbf{22:46}]
Have they not travelled on through the land? and have they not hearts to understand with,
or ears to hear with? for it is not their eyes which are blind, but blind are the hearts which are
within their breasts.

\noindent[\textbf{22:47}]
They will bid thee hasten on the torment, but God will never fail in his promise; for,
verily, a day with thy Lord is as a thousand years of what ye number.

\noindent[\textbf{22:48}]
And to how many a city have I given full range while it yet did wrong! then I seized on it,
and unto me was the return.

\noindent[\textbf{22:49}]
Say, 'O ye folk! I am naught but a plain warner to you,

\noindent[\textbf{22:50}]
but those who believe and do right, for them is forgiveness and a generous provision;

\noindent[\textbf{22:51}]
but those who strive to discredit our signs, they are the fellows of hell!'

\section{Surah 2: The Chapter of the Heifer (Medina).}

 (selections)

\noindent[\textbf{2:190}]
Fight in God's way with those who fight with you, but transgress not; verily, God loves
not those who do transgress.

\noindent[\textbf{2:191}]
Kill them wherever ye find them, and drive them out from whence they drive you out; for
sedition is worse than slaughter; but fight them not by the Sacred Mosque until they fight you
there; then kill them, for such is the recompense of those that misbelieve.

\noindent[\textbf{2:192}]
But if they desist, then, verily, God is forgiving and merciful.

\noindent[\textbf{2:193}]
But fight them that there be no sedition and that the religion may be God's; but, if they
desist, then let there be no hostility save against the unjust.

\noindent[\textbf{2:194}]
The sacred month for the sacred month; for all sacred things demand retaliation; and
whoso transgresses against you, transgress against him like as he transgressed against you; but
fear ye God, and know that God is with those who fear.

\noindent[\textbf{2:195}]
Expend in alms in God's way and be not cast by your own hands into perdition; but do
good, for God loves those who do good.

\begin{center}
  {\Huge
    *
    \hspace{2em}
    *
    \hspace{2em}
    *
    \hspace{2em}
    *
  }
\end{center}
%TODO use arabic unicode symbols instead?

\noindent[\textbf{2:216}]
Prescribed for you is fighting, but it is hateful to you. Yet peradventure that ye hate a
thing while it is good for you, and peradventure that ye love a thing while it is bad for you; God
knows, and ye,--- ye do not know!

\noindent[\textbf{2:217}]
They will ask thee of the sacred month,--- of fighting therein. Say, 'Fighting therein is a
great sin; but turning folks off God's way, and misbelief in Him and in the Sacred Mosque, and
turning His people out therefrom, is a greater in God's sight; and sedition is a greater sin than
slaughter.' They will not cease from fighting you until they turn you from your religion if they
can; but whosoever of you is turned from his religion and dies while still a misbeliever; these are
those whose works are vain in this world and the next; they are the fellows of the Fire, and they
shall dwell therein for aye.

\noindent[\textbf{2:218}]
Verily, those who believe, and those who flee, and those who wage war in God's way;
these may hope for God's mercy, for God is forgiving and merciful.

\section{Surah 8: The Chapter of the Spoils (Medinah).}

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{8:1}]
They will ask thee about the spoils. Say, 'The spoils are God's and the Apostle's; fear
God and settle it amongst yourselves; obey God and the Apostle if ye do believe.'

\noindent[\textbf{8:2}]
Verily, the believers are those who, when God's name is mentioned, their hearts sink
with fear; and when His signs are rehearsed to them they increase them in faith; and on their
Lord do they rely;

\noindent[\textbf{8:3}]
who are steadfast in prayer, and of what we have bestowed upon them give in alms;

\noindent[\textbf{8:4}]
these are in truth believers; to them are degrees with their Lord, and forgiveness, and a
generous provision.

\noindent[\textbf{8:5}]
As thy Lord caused thee to go forth from thy house with the truth, although a sect of the
believers were averse therefrom.

\noindent[\textbf{8:6}]
They wrangled with thee about the truth after it was made plain, as though they were
being driven on to death and looked thereon;

\noindent[\textbf{8:7}]
and when God promised you that one of the two troops should be yours, and ye would
fain have had those who had no arms. God wished to prove the truth true by His words, and to
cut off the hindermost parts of those who misbelieve ---

\noindent[\textbf{8:8}]
to prove the truth true, and to make vain the vain, although the sinners are averse.

\noindent[\textbf{8:9}]
When ye asked for succour from your Lord, and He answered you, 'I will assist you with
a thousand angels, with others in reserve.'

\noindent[\textbf{8:10}]
God made it only glad tidings to quiet your hearts therewith; for victory is only from
God! verily, God is mighty and wise.

\noindent[\textbf{8:11}]
When drowsiness covered you as a security from Him, and He sent down upon you from
the heavens water to purify you withal, and to take away from you the plague of Satan, and to tie
up your hearts and to make firm your footsteps.

\noindent[\textbf{8:12}]
When your Lord inspired the angels --- 'Verily, I am with you; make ye firm then those
who believe; I will cast dread into the hearts of those who misbelieve,--- strike off their necks
then, and strike off from them every finger tip.'

\noindent[\textbf{8:13}]
That is, because they went into opposition against God and His Apostle; for he who goes
into opposition against God and His Apostle --- verily, God is keen to punish.

\noindent[\textbf{8:14}]
There, taste it! since for the misbelievers is the torment of the Fire.

\noindent[\textbf{8:15}]
O ye who believe! when ye meet those who misbelieve in swarms, turn not to them your
hinder parts;

\noindent[\textbf{8:16}]
for he who turns to them that day his hinder parts, save turning to fight or rallying to a
troop, brings down upon himself wrath from God, and his resort is hell, and an ill journey shall it
be!

\noindent[\textbf{8:17}]
Ye did not slay them, but it was God who slew them; nor didst thou shoot when thou
didst shoot, but God did shoot, to try the believers from Himself with a goodly trial; verily, God
both hears and knows.

\noindent[\textbf{8:18}]
There! verily, God weakens the stratagem of the misbelievers.

\noindent[\textbf{8:19}]
If ye wish the matter to be decided, a decision has now come to you; but if ye desist, it is
better for you; and if ye turn back we will turn too, and your troop shall avail nothing, great in
number though it be, since God is with the believers!

\noindent[\textbf{8:20}]
O ye who believe! obey God and His Apostle, and turn not from Him while ye hear,

\noindent[\textbf{8:21}]
and be not like those who say, 'We hear,' and yet they hear not.

\noindent[\textbf{8:22}]
Verily, the worst of beasts in God's sight are the deaf, the dumb who do not understand.

\noindent[\textbf{8:23}]
Had God known any good in them, He would have made them hear; but had He made
them hear, they would have turned back and have swerved aside.

\noindent[\textbf{8:24}]
O ye who believe! answer God and His Apostle when He calls you to that which
quickens you; and know that God steps in between man and his heart; and that to Him ye shall be
gathered.

\noindent[\textbf{8:25}]
And fear temptation, which will not light especially on those of you who have done
wrong; but know that God is keen to punish.

\noindent[\textbf{8:26}]
Remember when ye were few in number and weak in the land, fearing lest people should
snatch you away; then He sheltered you and aided you with victory, and provided you with good
things; haply ye may give thanks.

\noindent[\textbf{8:27}]
O ye who believe! be not treacherous to God and His Apostle; nor be treacherous to your
engagement while ye know!

\noindent[\textbf{8:28}]
Know that your wealth and your children are but a temptation, and that God --- with Him
is mighty hire!

\noindent[\textbf{8:29}]
O ye who believe! if ye fear God He will make for you a discrimination, and will cover
for you your offences, and will forgive you; for God is Lord of mighty grace.

\noindent[\textbf{8:30}]
And when those who misbelieve were crafty with thee to detain thee a prisoner, or kill
thee, or drive thee forth; they were crafty, but God was crafty too, for God is best of crafty ones!

\noindent[\textbf{8:31}]
But when our verses were rehearsed to them they said, 'We have already heard.--- If we
pleased we could speak like this; verily, this is nothing but tales of those of yore.'

\noindent[\textbf{8:32}]
When they said, 'O God! if this be truth, and from Thee, then rain upon us stones from
heaven or bring us grievous woe!'

\noindent[\textbf{8:33}]
But God would not torment them while thou art amongst them; nor was God going to
torment them while they asked Him to forgive.

\noindent[\textbf{8:34}]
But what ails them that God should not torment them while they turn folk away from the
Holy Mosque, though they are not the guardians thereof --- its guardians are only the pious?---
but most of them know not.

\noindent[\textbf{8:35}]
Their prayer at the House was naught but whistling and clapping hands!--- taste then the
torment for that ye misbelieved!

\noindent[\textbf{8:36}]
Verily, those who misbelieve expend their wealth to turn folk from the path of God; but
they shall spend it, and then it shall be for them sighing, and then they shall be overcome! Those
who misbelieve, into hell shall they be gathered!---

\noindent[\textbf{8:37}]
that God may distinguish the vile from the good, and may put the vile, some on the top of
the other, and heap all up together, and put it into hell!--- These are those who lose!

\noindent[\textbf{8:38}]
Say to those who misbelieve, if they desist they will be forgiven what is past; but if they
return,--- the course of those of former days has passed away.

\noindent[\textbf{8:39}]
Fight them then that there should be no sedition, and that the religion may be wholly
God's; but if they desist, then God on what they do doth look.

\noindent[\textbf{8:40}]
But if they turn their backs, then know that God is your Lord; a good Lord is He, and a
good help;

\noindent[\textbf{8:41}]
and know that whenever ye seize anything as a spoil, to God belongs a fifth thereof, and
to His Apostle, and to kindred and orphans, and the poor and the wayfarer; if ye believe in God
and what we have revealed unto our servants on the day of the discrimination,--- the day when
the two parties met; and God is mighty over all.

\noindent[\textbf{8:42}]
When ye were on the near side of the valley, and they were on the far side, and the
camels were below you; had ye made an appointment then ye would have failed to keep your
appointment --- but it was that God might accomplish a thing that was as good as done! that he
who was to perish might perish with a manifest sign; and that he who was to live might live with
a manifest sign; for, verily, God hears and knows!

\noindent[\textbf{8:43}]
When God showed thee them in thy dream as though they were but few; but had He
shown thee them as though they were many, ye would have been timid, and ye would have
quarrelled about the matter;--- but God preserved you; verily, He knows the nature of men's
breasts!

\noindent[\textbf{8:44}]
And when He showed them to you, as ye encountered them, as few in your eyes; and
made you seem few in their eyes; that God might accomplish a thing that was as good as done;
for unto God do things return!

\noindent[\textbf{8:45}]
O ye who believe! when ye encounter a troop, then stand firm and remember God; and
haply ye may prosper!

\noindent[\textbf{8:46}]
and fear God and His Apostle, and do not quarrel or be timid, so that your turn of luck go
from you; but be ye patient, verily, God is with the patient.

\noindent[\textbf{8:47}]
And be not like those who went forth from their homes with insolence, and for
appearance sake before men, and to turn folks off God's way; for all they do God comprehends.

\noindent[\textbf{8:48}]
And when Satan made their works appear seemly to them, and said, 'There is none
amongst mankind to conquer you today, for, verily, I am your neighbour!' and when the two
troops came in sight of each other, he turned upon his heels and said, 'Verily, I am clear of you!
verily, I see what you see not! verily, I fear God, for God is keen to punish!'

\noindent[\textbf{8:49}]
And when the hypocrites and those in whose hearts was sickness said, 'Their religion
hath beguiled these men, but he who relies upon God, verily, God is mighty and wise.'

\noindent[\textbf{8:50}]
Couldst thou see when the angels take away the souls of those who misbelieve; they
smite them on their faces and hinder parts.--- 'Taste ye the torment of burning!

\noindent[\textbf{8:51}]
that is for what your hands have sent on before; and for that God is no unjust one towards
his servants.'

\noindent[\textbf{8:52}]
As was the wont of Pharaoh's people and those before them! they disbelieved in the signs
of God, and God overtook them in their sins; verily, God is strong and keen to punish.

\noindent[\textbf{8:53}]
That is because God is not one to change a favour He has favoured a people with, until
they change what they have in themselves, and for that God both hears and knows.

\noindent[\textbf{8:54}]
As was the wont of Pharaoh's people and those before them! they said our signs were
lies, and we destroyed them in their sins, and drowned Pharaoh's people; and all of them were
evildoers.

\noindent[\textbf{8:55}]
Verily, the worst of beasts in God's eyes are those who misbelieve and will not believe;

\noindent[\textbf{8:56}]
with whom if thou dost make a league, they break their league each time, for they fear
not God;

\noindent[\textbf{8:57}]
but shouldst thou ever catch them in war, then make those who come after them run by
their example, haply they may remember then.

\noindent[\textbf{8:58}]
And shouldst thou ever fear from any people treachery, then throw it back to them in like
manner; verily, God loves not the treacherous.

\noindent[\textbf{8:59}]
Deem not that those who misbelieve can win; verily, they cannot make (God) powerless!

\noindent[\textbf{8:60}]
Prepare ye against them what force and companies of horse ye can, to make the enemies
of God, and your enemies, and others beside them, in dread thereof. Ye do not know them, but
God knows them! and whatever ye expend in God's way He will repay you; and ye shall not be
wronged.

\noindent[\textbf{8:61}]
But if they incline to peace, incline thou to it too, and rely upon God; verily, He both
hears and knows.

\noindent[\textbf{8:62}]
But if they wish to betray thee, then God is enough for thee! He it is who supports thee
with His help and with the believers;

\noindent[\textbf{8:63}]
and reconciles their hearts! Didst thou expend all that is in the earth thou couldst not
reconcile their hearts, but God reconciled them, verily, He is mighty and wise!

\noindent[\textbf{8:64}]
O thou prophet ! God is sufficient for thee, with those of the believers who follow thee!

\noindent[\textbf{8:65}]
O thou prophet! urge on the believers to fight. If there be of you twenty patient men, they
shall conquer two hundred; if there be of you a hundred, they shall conquer a thousand of those
who misbelieve, because they are a people who did not discern.---

\noindent[\textbf{8:66}]
Now has God made it light for you; He knows that there is a weakness amongst you: but
if there be amongst you but a patient hundred, they will conquer two hundred; and if there be of
you a thousand, they will conquer two thousand, by the permission of God,--- for God is with the
patient!

\noindent[\textbf{8:67}]
It has not been for any prophet to take captives until he hath slaughtered in the land! Ye
wish to have the goods of this world, but God wishes for the next, for God is mighty, wise!

\noindent[\textbf{8:68}]
Were it not for a book from God that had gone before, there would have touched you, for
that which ye took, a mighty punishment.

\noindent[\textbf{8:69}]
Eat of what spoils ye have taken, what is lawful and good; and fear God, verily, God is
forgiving and merciful.

\noindent[\textbf{8:70}]
O thou prophet! say to such of the captives as are in your hands, 'If God knows of any
good in your hearts, he will give you better than that which is taken from you, and will forgive
you; for God is forgiving and merciful.'

\noindent[\textbf{8:71}]
But if they desire to betray thee,--- they have betrayed God before! but He hath given you
power over them; for God is knowing, wise!

\noindent[\textbf{8:72}]
Verily, those who believe and have fled and fought strenuously with their wealth and
persons in God's way, and those who have given refuge and help, these shall be next of kin to
each other. But those who believe, but have not fled, ye have naught to do with their claims of
kindred, until they flee as well. But if they ask you for aid for religion's sake, then help is due
from you, except against a people between whom and you there is an alliance; for God on what
ye do doth look.

\noindent[\textbf{8:73}]
And those who misbelieve, some of them are next of kin to others --- unless ye act the
same there will be sedition in the land, and great corruption.

\noindent[\textbf{8:74}]
Those who believe and have fled and fought strenuously in God's cause, and those who
have given a refuge and a help, those it is who believe; to them is forgiveness and generous
provision due.

\noindent[\textbf{8:75}]
And those who have believed afterwards and have fled and fought strenuously with you;
these too are of you, but blood relations are nearer in kin by the Book of God. Verily, God all
things doth know.

\section{Surah 39: The Chapter of the Troops (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{39:41}]
Verily, we have sent down to thee the Book for men in truth; and whosoever is guided it
is for his own soul: but whoso goes astray it is against them, and thou art not a guardian for them.

\noindent[\textbf{39:42}]
God takes to Himself souls at the time of their death; and those which do not die (He
takes) in their sleep; and He holds back those on whom He has decreed death, and sends others
back till their appointed time;--- verily, in that are signs unto a people who reflect.

\noindent[\textbf{39:43}]
Do they take besides God intercessors? Say, 'What! though they have no control over
anything and have no sense.'

\noindent[\textbf{39:44}]
Say, 'God's is the intercession, all of it; His is the kingdom of the heavens and the earth;
then unto Him shall ye be sent back.'

\noindent[\textbf{39:45}]
And when God alone is mentioned the hearts of those who believe not in the hereafter
quake, and when those beside Him are mentioned, lo, they are joyful!

\noindent[\textbf{39:46}]
Say, 'O God! originator of the heavens and the earth, who knowest the unseen and the
visible, thou wilt judge between thy servants concerning that whereon they do dispute!'

\noindent[\textbf{39:47}]
And had those who do wrong all that is in the earth, and the like thereof with it, they
would ransom themselves therewith from the evil of the torment on the resurrection day! but
there shall appear to them from God that which they had not reckoned on;

\noindent[\textbf{39:48}]
and the evils of what they have earned shall appear to them; but that shall close in on
them at which they mocked!

\noindent[\textbf{39:49}]
And when harm touches man he calls on us; then, when we grant him favour from us, he
says, 'Verily, I am given it through knowledge!' nay, it is a trial,--- but most of them do not
know!

\noindent[\textbf{39:50}]
Those before them said it too, but that availed them not which they had earned,

\noindent[\textbf{39:51}]
and there befell them the evil deeds of what they had earned: and those who do wrong of
these (Meccans), there shall befall them too the evil deeds of what they had earned, nor shall
they frustrate Him.

\noindent[\textbf{39:52}]
Have they not known that God extends His provision to whom He pleases, or doles it
out? verily, in that are signs unto a people who believe.

\noindent[\textbf{39:53}]
Say, 'O my servants! who have been extravagant against their own souls!' be not in
despair of the mercy of God; verily, God forgives sins, all of them; verily, He is forgiving,
merciful.

\noindent[\textbf{39:54}]
But turn repentant unto your Lord, and resign yourselves to Him, before there comes on
you torment! then ye shall not be helped:

\noindent[\textbf{39:55}]
and follow the best of what has been sent down to you from your Lord, before there come
on you the torment suddenly, ere ye can perceive!

\noindent[\textbf{39:56}]
Lest a soul should say, 'O my sighing! for what I have neglected towards God! for,
verily, I was amongst those who did jest!'

\noindent[\textbf{39:57}]
or lest it should say, 'If God had but guided me, I should surely have been of those who
fear!'

\noindent[\textbf{39:58}]
or lest it should say, when it sees the torment, 'Had I another turn I should be of those
who do well!'

\noindent[\textbf{39:59}]
'Yea! there came to thee my signs and thou didst call them lies, and wert too big with
pride, and wert of those who misbelieved!'

\noindent[\textbf{39:60}]
And on the resurrection day thou shalt see those who lied against God, with their faces
blackened. Is there not in hell a resort for those who are too big with pride?

\noindent[\textbf{39:61}]
And God shall rescue those who fear Him, into their safe place; no evil shall touch them,
nor shall they be grieved.

\noindent[\textbf{39:62}]
God is the creator of everything, and He is guardian over everything;

\noindent[\textbf{39:63}]
His are the keys of the heavens and the earth; and those who misbelieve in the signs of
God, they it is who lose!

\noindent[\textbf{39:64}]
Say, 'What! other than God would you bid me serve, O ye ignorant ones?

\noindent[\textbf{39:65}]
When He has inspired thee and those before thee that, "If thou dost associate aught with
Him, thy work will surely be in vain, and thou shalt surely be of those who lose!"

\noindent[\textbf{39:66}]
Nay, but God do thou serve, and be of those who do give thanks!'

\noindent[\textbf{39:67}]
And they do not value God at His true value; while the earth all of it is but a handful for
Him on the resurrection day, and the heavens shall be rolled up in His right hand! Celebrated be
His praise! and exalted be He above what they associate with Him!

\noindent[\textbf{39:68}]
And the trumpet shall be blown, and those who are in the heavens and in the earth shall
swoon, save whom God pleases. Then it shall be blown again, and, lo! they shall stand up and
look on.

\noindent[\textbf{39:69}]
And the earth shall beam with the light of its Lord, and the Book shall be set forth, and
the prophets and martyrs shall be brought; and it shall be decreed between them in truth, and they
shall not be wronged!

\noindent[\textbf{39:70}]
And every soul shall be paid for what it has done, and He knows best that which they do;

\noindent[\textbf{39:71}]
and those who misbelieve shall be driven to hell in troops; and when they come there, its
doors shall be opened, and its keepers shall say to them, 'Did not apostles from amongst
yourselves come to you to recite to you the signs of your Lord, and to warn you of the meeting of
this day of yours?' They shall say, 'Yea, but the sentence of torment was due against the
misbelievers!'

\noindent[\textbf{39:72}]
It shall be said, 'Enter ye the gates of hell, to dwell therein for aye! Hell is the resort of
those who are too big with pride!'

\noindent[\textbf{39:73}]
But those who fear their Lord shall be driven to Paradise in troops; until they come there,
its doors shall be opened, and its keepers shall say to them, 'Peace be upon you, ye have done
well! so enter in to dwell for aye!'

\noindent[\textbf{39:74}]
and they shall say, ' Praise be to God, who hath made good His promise to us, and hath
given us the earth to inherit! We establish ourselves in Paradise wherever we please; and goodly
is the reward of those who work!'

\noindent[\textbf{39:75}]
And thou shalt see the angels circling round about the throne, celebrating the praise of
their Lord; and it shall be decided between them in truth; and it shall be said, 'Praise be to God,
the Lord of the worlds!'

\section{Surah 36: The Chapter of YA SIN (Mecca).}

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{36:1}]
YA SIN.

\noindent[\textbf{36:2}]
By the wise Qur'an,

\noindent[\textbf{36:3}]
verily, thou art of the apostles

\noindent[\textbf{36:4}]
upon a right way.

\noindent[\textbf{36:5}]
The revelation of the mighty, the merciful!

\noindent[\textbf{36:6}]
That thou mayest warn a people whose fathers were not warned, and who themselves are
heedless.

\noindent[\textbf{36:7}]
Now is the sentence due against most of them, for they will not believe.

\noindent[\textbf{36:8}]
Verily, we will place upon their necks fetters, and they shall reach up to their chins, and
they shall have their heads forced back;

\noindent[\textbf{36:9}]
and we will place before them a barrier, and behind them a barrier; and we will cover
them and they shall not see;

\noindent[\textbf{36:10}]
and it is all, the same to them if thou dost warn them or dost warn them not, they will not
believe.

\noindent[\textbf{36:11}]
Thou canst only warn him who follows the reminder, and fears the Merciful in the
unseen; but give him glad tidings of forgiveness and a noble hire.

\noindent[\textbf{36:12}]
Verily, we quicken the dead, and write down what they have done before, and what
vestiges they leave behind; and everything have we counted in a plain model.

\noindent[\textbf{36:13}]
Strike out for them a parable: the fellows of the city when there came to it the apostles;

\noindent[\textbf{36:14}]
when we sent those two and they called them both liars, and we strengthened them with a
third; and they said, 'Verily, we are sent to you.'

\noindent[\textbf{36:15}]
They said, 'Ye are only mortals like ourselves, nor has the Merciful sent down aught; ye
are naught but liars.'

\noindent[\textbf{36:16}]
They said, 'Our Lord knows that we are sent to you,

\noindent[\textbf{36:17}]
and we have only our plain message to preach.'

\noindent[\textbf{36:18}]
They said, 'Verily, we have augured concerning you, and if ye do not desist we will
surely stone you, and there shall touch you from us a grievous woe.'

\noindent[\textbf{36:19}]
Said they, 'Your augury is with you; what! if ye are reminded ---? Nay, ye are an
extravagant people!'

\noindent[\textbf{36:20}]
And there came from the remote part of the city a man hastening up. Said he, 'O my
people! follow the apostles;

\noindent[\textbf{36:21}]
follow those who do not ask you a hire, and who are guided.

\noindent[\textbf{36:22}]
What ails me that I should not worship Him who originated me, and unto whom I must
return?

\noindent[\textbf{36:23}]
Shall I take gods beside Him? If the Merciful One desires harm for me, their intercession
cannot avail me at all, nor can they rescue me.'

\noindent[\textbf{36:24}]
Verily, I should then be in obvious error;

\noindent[\textbf{36:25}]
verily, I believe in your Lord, then listen ye to me!'

\noindent[\textbf{36:26}]
It was said, 'Enter thou into Paradise!' said he, 'O, would that my people did but know!

\noindent[\textbf{36:27}]
for that my Lord has forgiven me, and has made me of the honoured.'

\noindent[\textbf{36:28}]
And we did send down upon his people no hosts from heaven, nor yet what we were wont
to send down;

\noindent[\textbf{36:29}]
it was but a single noise, and lo! they were extinct.

\noindent[\textbf{36:30}]
Alas for the servants! there comes to them no apostle but they mock at him!

\noindent[\textbf{36:31}]
Have they not seen how many generations we have, destroyed before them?

\noindent[\textbf{36:32}]
verily, they shall not return to them; but all of them shall surely altogether be arraigned.

\noindent[\textbf{36:33}]
And a sign for them is the dead earth which we have quickened and brought forth
therefrom seed, and from it do they eat;

\noindent[\textbf{36:34}]
and we made therein gardens and palms and grapes, and we have caused fountains to
gush forth therein,

\noindent[\textbf{36:35}]
that they may eat from the fruit thereof, and of what their hands have made; will they not
then give thanks?

\noindent[\textbf{36:36}]
Celebrated be the praises of Him who created all kinds, of what the earth brings forth,
and of themselves, and what they know not of!

\noindent[\textbf{36:37}]
And a sign to them is the night, from which we strip off the day, and lo! they are in the
dark;

\noindent[\textbf{36:38}]
and the sun runs on to a place of rest for it; that is the ordinance of the mighty, the wise.

\noindent[\textbf{36:39}]
And the moon, we have ordered for it stations, until it comes again to be like an old dry
palm branch.

\noindent[\textbf{36:40}]
