Neither is it proper for it to catch up the moon, nor for the night to outstrip the day, but
each one floats on in its sky.

\noindent[\textbf{36:41}]
And a sign for them is that we bear their seed in a laden ship,

\noindent[\textbf{36:42}]
and we have created for them the like thereof whereon to ride;

\noindent[\textbf{36:43}]
and if we please, we drown them, and there is none for them to appeal to; nor are they
rescued,

\noindent[\textbf{36:44}]
save by mercy from us, as a provision for a season.

\noindent[\textbf{36:45}]
And when it is said to them, 'Fear what is before you and what is behind you, haply ye
may obtain mercy;'

\noindent[\textbf{36:46}]
and thou bringest them not any one of the signs of their Lord, but they turn away
therefrom,

\noindent[\textbf{36:47}]
and when it is said to them, 'Expend in alms of what God has bestowed upon you,' those
who misbelieve say to those who believe, 'Shall we feed him whom, if God pleased, He would
feed? ye are only in an obvious error.'

\noindent[\textbf{36:48}]
They say, 'When shall this promise come to pass, if ye do tell the truth?'

\noindent[\textbf{36:49}]
They await but a single noise, that shall seize them as they are contending.

\noindent[\textbf{36:50}]
And they shall not be able to make a bequest; nor to their people shall they return;

\noindent[\textbf{36:51}]
but the trumpet shall be blown, and, behold, from their graves unto their Lord shall they
slip out!

\noindent[\textbf{36:52}]
They shall say, 'O, woe is us! who has raised us up from our sleeping-place? this is what
the Merciful promised, and the apostles told the truth!'

\noindent[\textbf{36:53}]
It shall be but a single noise, and lo! they are all arraigned before us.

\noindent[\textbf{36:54}]
And on that day no soul shall be wronged at all, nor shall ye be rewarded for aught but
that which ye have done.

\noindent[\textbf{36:55}]
Verily, the fellows of Paradise upon that day shall be employed in enjoyment;

\noindent[\textbf{36:56}]
they and their wives, in shade upon thrones, reclining;

\noindent[\textbf{36:57}]
therein shall they have fruits, and they shall have what they may call for.

\noindent[\textbf{36:58}]
'Peace!' --- a speech from the merciful Lord!

\noindent[\textbf{36:59}]
'Separate yourselves today, O ye sinners!

\noindent[\textbf{36:60}]
Did I not covenant with you, O children of Adam! that ye should not serve Satan? verily,
he is to you an open foe;

\noindent[\textbf{36:61}]
but serve ye me, this is the right way.

\noindent[\textbf{36:62}]
But he led astray a numerous race of you; what! had ye then no sense?

\noindent[\textbf{36:63}]
this is hell, which ye were threatened;

\noindent[\textbf{36:64}]
broil therein today, for that ye misbelieved!'

\noindent[\textbf{36:65}]
On that day we will seal their mouths, and their hands shall speak to us, and their feet
shall bear witness of what they earned.

\noindent[\textbf{36:66}]
And if we please we could put out their eyes, and they would race along the road; and
then how could they see?

\noindent[\textbf{36:67}]
And if we pleased we would transform them in their places, and they should not be able
to go on, nor yet to return.

\noindent[\textbf{36:68}]
And him to whom we grant old age, we bow him down in his form; have they then no
sense?

\noindent[\textbf{36:69}]
We have not taught him poetry, nor was it proper for him; it is but a reminder and a plain
Qur'an,

\noindent[\textbf{36:70}]
to warn him who is living; but the sentence is due against the misbelievers.

\noindent[\textbf{36:71}]
Have they not seen that we have created for them of what our hands have made for them,
cattle, and they are owners thereof?

\noindent[\textbf{36:72}]
and we have tamed them for them, and of them are some to ride, and of them are what
they eat,

\noindent[\textbf{36:73}]
and therein have they advantages and beverages; will they not then give thanks?

\noindent[\textbf{36:74}]
But they take, beside God, gods that haply they may be helped.

\noindent[\textbf{36:75}]
They cannot help them; yet are they a host ready for them.

\noindent[\textbf{36:76}]
But let not their speech grieve thee: verily, we know what they conceal and what they
display.

\noindent[\textbf{36:77}]
Has not man seen that we have created him from a clot? and lo! he is an open opponent;

\noindent[\textbf{36:78}]
and he strikes out for us a likeness; and forgets his creation; and says, 'Who shall quicken
bones when they are rotten?'

\noindent[\textbf{36:79}]
Say, 'He shall quicken them who produced them at first, for every creation does He
know;

\noindent[\textbf{36:80}]
who has made for you fire out of a green tree, and lo! ye kindle therewith.'

\noindent[\textbf{36:81}]
Is not He who created the heavens and the earth able to create the like thereof? yea! He is
the knowing Creator;

\noindent[\textbf{36:82}]
His bidding is only, when He desires anything to say to it, 'BE,' and it is.

\noindent[\textbf{36:83}]
Then celebrated be the praises of Him in whose hands is the kingdom of everything! and
unto Him shall ye return.

\section{Surah 52: The Chapter of the Mount (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{52:29}]
Wherefore do thou remind them: for thou art, by the favour of thy Lord, neither a
soothsayer nor mad!

\noindent[\textbf{52:30}]
Will they say, 'A poet; we wait for him the sad accidents of fate?'

\noindent[\textbf{52:31}]
Say, 'Wait ye then; for I too am of those who wait!'

\noindent[\textbf{52:32}]
Do their dreams bid them this? or are they an outrageous people?

\noindent[\textbf{52:33}]
Or will they say, 'He has invented it?' --- nay, but they do not believe!

\noindent[\textbf{52:34}]
But let them bring a discourse like it, if they tell the truth!

\noindent[\textbf{52:35}]
Or were they created of nothing, or were they the creators?

\noindent[\textbf{52:36}]
Or did they create the heavens and the earth? --- nay, but they are not sure!

\noindent[\textbf{52:37}]
Or have they the treasures of thy Lord? or are they the governors supreme?

\noindent[\textbf{52:38}]
Or have they a ladder whereon they can listen?--- then let their listener bring obvious
authority.

\noindent[\textbf{52:39}]
Has He daughters, while ye have sons?

\noindent[\textbf{52:40}]
Or dost thou ask them a hire, while they are borne down by debt?

\noindent[\textbf{52:41}]
Or have they the unseen, so that they write it down?

\noindent[\textbf{52:42}]
Or do they desire a plot? --- but those who misbelieve it is who are plotted against!

\noindent[\textbf{52:43}]
Or have they a god beside God? celebrated be God's praises above what they join with
Him!

\noindent[\textbf{52:44}]
But if they should see a fragment of the sky falling down, they would say, 'Clouds in
masses!'

\noindent[\textbf{52:45}]
But leave them till they meet that day of theirs whereon they shall swoon;

\noindent[\textbf{52:46}]
the day when their plotting shall avail them naught, and they shall not be helped!

\noindent[\textbf{52:47}]
And, verily, there is a torment beside that for those who do wrong; but most of them do
not know!

\noindent[\textbf{52:48}]
But wait thou patiently for the judgment of thy Lord, for thou art in our eyes. And
celebrate the praises of thy Lord what time thou risest,

\noindent[\textbf{52:49}]
and in the night, and at the fading of the stars!

\section{Surah 34: The Chapter of Seba (Mecca).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)


\noindent[\textbf{34:22}]
Say, 'Call on those whom ye pretend beside God;' they cannot control the weight of an
atom in the heavens or in the earth; nor have they any partnership in either; nor has He amongst
them any supporter;

\noindent[\textbf{34:23}]
nor is intercession of any avail with Him, except for him whom He permits; so that when
fright is removed from their hearts they say, 'What is it that your Lord says?' they say, 'The
truth; for He is the high, the great.'

\noindent[\textbf{34:24}]
Say, 'Who provides from the heavens and the earth?' Say, 'God.' And, verily, we or ye
are surely in guidance or in an obvious error.

\noindent[\textbf{34:25}]
Say, 'Ye shall not be asked about what we have sent, nor shall we be asked about what ye
do.

\noindent[\textbf{34:26}]
'Our Lord shall assemble us together; then He shall open between us in truth, for He is
the opener who knows.'

\noindent[\textbf{34:27}]
Say, 'Show me those whom ye have added to Him as partners; not so! nay, but He is
God, the mighty, the wise!'

\noindent[\textbf{34:28}]
We have only sent thee to men generally as a herald of glad tidings and a warner; but
most men do not know.

\noindent[\textbf{34:29}]
And they say, 'When shall this promise be, if ye do speak the truth?'

\noindent[\textbf{34:30}]
say, 'For you is the appointment of a day of which ye shall not keep back an hour nor
shall ye bring it on!'

\noindent[\textbf{34:31}]
And those who misbelieve say, 'We will never believe in this Qur'an or in what is before
it;' but couldst thou see when the unjust are set before their Lord, they shall rebut each other in
speech. Those who were thought weak shall say to those who were big with pride, 'Had it not
been for you we should have been believers.'

\noindent[\textbf{34:32}]
Those who were big with pride shall say to those who were thought weak, 'Was it we
who turned you away from the guidance after it came to you? nay, ye were sinners.'

\noindent[\textbf{34:33}]
And those who were thought weak shall say to those who were big with pride, 'Nay, but
it was the plotting by night and day, when ye did bid us to disbelieve in God, and to make peers
for Him!' and they shall display repentance when they see the torment; and we will put fetters on
the necks of those who misbelieved. Shall they be rewarded except for that which they have
done?

\noindent[\textbf{34:34}]
We have not sent to any city a warner but the opulent thereof said, 'We, in what ye are
sent with, disbelieve.'

\noindent[\textbf{34:35}]
And they say, 'We have more wealth and children, and we shall not be tormented.'

\noindent[\textbf{34:36}]
Say, 'Verily, my Lord extends provision to whom He pleases or doles it out, but most
men do not know;

\noindent[\textbf{34:37}]
but neither your wealth nor your children is that which will bring you to a near approach
to us, save him who believes and does right; these, for them is a double reward for what they
have done, and they in upper rooms shall be secure.'

\noindent[\textbf{34:38}]
And those who strive concerning our signs to frustrate them, these in the torment shall be
arraigned.

\noindent[\textbf{34:39}]
Verily, my Lord extends provision to whomsoever He will of His servants, or doles it out
to him. And what ye expend in alms at all, He will repay it; for He is the best of providers.

\noindent[\textbf{34:40}]
And on the day He will gather them all together, then He will say to the angels, 'Are
these those who used to worship you?'

\noindent[\textbf{34:41}]
They shall say, 'Celebrated be thy praises! thou art our patron instead of them. Nay, they
used to worship the ginns, most of them believe in them.

\noindent[\textbf{34:42}]
But today they cannot control for each other, either profit or harm;' and we will say to
those who have done wrong, 'Taste ye the torment of the fire wherein ye did disbelieve!'

\clearpage
\chapter{Session 8}


\section{Surah 63: The Chapter of the Hypocrites (Medinah).}

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{63:1}]
When the hypocrites come to thee, they say, 'We bear witness that thou art surely the
Apostle of God;' but God knows that thou art His Apostle: and God bears witness that the
hypocrites are liars!

\noindent[\textbf{63:2}]
They take their faith for a cloak, and then they turn folks from God's way: --- evil is that
which they have done!

\noindent[\textbf{63:3}]
That is because they believed and then disbelieved, wherefore is a stamp set on their
hearts so that they do not understand!

\noindent[\textbf{63:4}]
And when thou seest them, their persons please thee; but if they speak, thou listenest to
their speech: they are like timber propped up: they reckon every noise against them! They are the
foe, so beware of them!--- God fight against them, how they lie!

\noindent[\textbf{63:5}]
And when it is said to them, 'Come, and the Apostle of God will ask forgiveness for
you!' they turn away their heads, and thou mayest see them turning away since they are so big
with pride!

\noindent[\textbf{63:6}]
It is the same to them whether thou dost ask forgiveness for them, or whether thou dost
not ask forgiveness for them,--- God will not forgive them; verily, God guides not a people who
work abomination!

\noindent[\textbf{63:7}]
They it is who say, 'Expend not in alms upon those who are with the Apostle of God, in
order that they may desert him!' --- but God's are the treasures of the heavens and the earth; but
the hypocrites have no sense!

\noindent[\textbf{63:8}]
They say, 'If we return to el Medinah, the mightier will surely drive out the meaner
therefrom;' but to God belongs the might, and to His Apostle and to the believers; but the
hypocrites do not know!

\noindent[\textbf{63:9}]
O ye who believe! let not your property nor your children divert you from the
remembrance of God, --- for whosoever does that, they are those who lose!

\noindent[\textbf{63:10}]
But expend in alms of what we have bestowed upon you before death come on any one of
you, and he says, 'My Lord! wouldst thou but have respited me till an appointed time nigh at
hand, then would I surely give in charity and be among the righteous!'

\noindent[\textbf{63:11}]
But God will never respite a soul when its appointed time has come: and God of what ye
do is well aware!

\section{Surah 2: The Chapter of the Heifer (Medina).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{2:1}]
ALIF LAM MIM.

\noindent[\textbf{2:2}]
That is the book! there is no doubt therein; a guide to the pious,

\noindent[\textbf{2:3}]
who believe in the unseen, and are steadfast in prayer, and of what we have given them
expend in alms;

\noindent[\textbf{2:4}]
who believe in what is revealed to thee, and what was revealed before thee, and of the
hereafter they are sure.

\noindent[\textbf{2:5}]
These are in guidance from their Lord, and these are the prosperous.

\noindent[\textbf{2:6}]
Verily, those who misbelieve, it is the same to them if ye warn them or if ye warn them
not, they will not believe.

\noindent[\textbf{2:7}]
God has set a seal upon their hearts and on their hearing; and on their eyes is dimness,
and for them is grievous woe.

\noindent[\textbf{2:8}]
And there are those among men who say, 'We believe in God and in the last day;' but
they do not believe.

\noindent[\textbf{2:9}]
They would deceive God and those who do believe; but they deceive only themselves and
they do not perceive.

\noindent[\textbf{2:10}]
In their hearts is a sickness, and God has made them still more sick, and for them is
grievous woe because they lied.

\noindent[\textbf{2:11}]
And when it is said to them, 'Do not evil in the earth,' they say, 'We do but what is
right.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:12}]
Are not they the evildoers? and yet they do not perceive.

\noindent[\textbf{2:13}]
And when it is said to them, 'Believe as other men believe,' they say, 'Shall we believe
as fools believe?' Are not they themselves the fools? and yet they do not know.

\noindent[\textbf{2:14}]
And when they meet those who believe, they say, 'We do believe;' but when they go
aside with their devils, they say, 'We are with you; we were but mocking!'

\noindent[\textbf{2:15}]
God shall mock at them and let them go on in their rebellion, blindly wandering on.

\noindent[\textbf{2:16}]
Those who buy error for guidance, their traffic profits not, and they are not guided.

\noindent[\textbf{2:17}]
Their likeness is as the likeness of one who kindles a fire; and when it lights up all
around, God goes off with their light, and leaves them in darkness that they cannot see.

\noindent[\textbf{2:18}]
Deafness, dumbness, blindness, and they shall not return!

\noindent[\textbf{2:19}]
Or like a storm-cloud from the sky, wherein is darkness and thunder and lightning; they
put their fingers in their ears at the thunder-clap, for fear of death, for God encompasses the
misbelievers.

\noindent[\textbf{2:20}]
The lightning well-nigh snatches off their sight, whenever it shines for them they walk
therein; but when it is dark for them they halt; and if God willed He would go off with their
hearing and their sight; verily, God is mighty over all.

\noindent[\textbf{2:21}]
O ye folk! serve your Lord who created you and those before you; haply ye may fear!

\noindent[\textbf{2:22}]
who made the earth for you a bed and the heaven a dome; and sent down from heaven
water, and brought forth therewith fruits as a sustenance for you; so make no peers for God, the
while ye know!

\noindent[\textbf{2:23}]
And if ye are in doubt of what we have revealed unto our servant, then bring a chapter
like it, and call your witnesses other than God if ye tell truth.

\noindent[\textbf{2:24}]
But if ye do it not, and ye shall surely do it not, then fear the fire whose fuel is men and
stones, prepared for misbelievers.

\noindent[\textbf{2:25}]
But bear the glad tidings to those who believe and work righteousness, that for them are
gardens beneath which rivers flow; whenever they are provided with fruit therefrom they say,
'This is what we were provided with before,' and they shall be provided with the like; and there
are pure wives for them therein, and they shall dwell therein for aye.

\noindent[\textbf{2:26}]
Why, God is not ashamed to set forth a parable of a gnat, or anything beyond; and as for
those who believe, they know that it is truth from the Lord; but as for those who disbelieve, they
say, 'What is it that God means by this as a parable? He leads astray many and He guides many;'
--- but He leads astray only the evildoers;

\noindent[\textbf{2:27}]
who break God's covenant after the fixing thereof, and cut asunder what God has ordered
to be joined, and do evil in the earth;--- these it is who lose.

\noindent[\textbf{2:28}]
How can ye disbelieve in God, when ye were dead and He made you alive, and then He
will kill you and then make you alive again, and then to Him will ye return?

\noindent[\textbf{2:29}]
It is He who created for you all that is in the earth, then he made for the heavens and
fashioned them seven heavens; and He knows all things.

\noindent[\textbf{2:30}]
And when thy Lord said unto the angels, 'I am about to place a vicegerent in the earth,'
they said, 'Wilt Thou place therein one who will do evil therein and shed blood? we celebrate
Thy praise and hallow Thee.' Said (the Lord), 'I know what ye know not.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:31}]
And He taught Adam the names, all of them; then He propounded them to the angels and
said, 'Declare to me the names of these, if ye are truthful.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:32}]
They said, 'Glory be to Thee! no knowledge is ours but what Thou thyself hast taught us,
verily, Thou art the knowing, the wise.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:33}]
Said the Lord, 'O Adam declare to them their names;' and when he had declared to them
their names He said, Did I not say to you, I know the secrets of the heavens and of the earth, and
I know what ye show and what ye were hiding?'

\noindent[\textbf{2:34}]
And when we said to the angels, 'Adore Adam,' they adored him save only Iblis, who
refused and was too proud and became one of the misbelievers.

\noindent[\textbf{2:35}]
And we said, 'O Adam dwell, thou and thy wife, in Paradise, and eat therefrom amply as
you wish; but do not draw near this tree or ye will be of the transgressors.

\noindent[\textbf{2:36}]
And Satan made them backslide therefrom and drove them out from what they were in,
and we said, 'Go down, one of you the enemy of the other, and in the earth there is an abode and
a provision for a time.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:37}]
And Adam caught certain words from his Lord, and He turned towards him, for He is the
compassionate one easily turned.

\noindent[\textbf{2:38}]
We said, 'Go down therefrom altogether and haply there may come from me a guidance,
and whoso follows my guidance, no fear is theirs, nor shall they grieve.

\noindent[\textbf{2:39}]
But those who misbelieve, and call our signs lies, they are the fellows of the Fire, they
shall dwell therein for aye.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:40}]
O ye children of Israel! remember my favours which I have favoured you with; fulfil my
covenant and I will fulfil your covenant; me therefore dread.

\noindent[\textbf{2:41}]
Believe in what I have revealed, verifying what ye have got, and be not the first to
disbelieve in it, and do not barter my signs for a little price, and me do ye fear.

\noindent[\textbf{2:42}]
Clothe not truth with vanity, nor hide the truth the while ye know.

\noindent[\textbf{2:43}]
Be steadfast in prayer, give the alms, and bow down with those who bow.

\noindent[\textbf{2:44}]
Will ye order men to do piety and forget yourselves? ye read the Book, do ye not then
understand?

\noindent[\textbf{2:45}]
Seek aid with patience and prayer, though it is a hard thing save for the humble,

\noindent[\textbf{2:46}]
who think that they will meet their Lord, and that to Him will they return.

\noindent[\textbf{2:47}]
O ye children of Israel! remember my favours which I have favoured you with, and that I
have preferred you above the worlds.

\noindent[\textbf{2:48}]
Fear the day wherein no soul shall pay any recompense for another soul, nor shall
intercession be accepted for it, nor shall compensation be taken from it, nor shall they be helped.

\noindent[\textbf{2:49}]
When we saved you from Pharaohs people who sought to wreak you evil and woe,
slaughtering your sons and letting your women live; in that was a great trial for you from your
Lord.

\noindent[\textbf{2:50}]
When we divided for you the sea and saved you and drowned Pharaohs people while ye
looked on.

\noindent[\textbf{2:51}]
When we treated with Moses forty nights, then ye took the calf after he had gone and ye
did wrong.

\noindent[\textbf{2:52}]
Yet then we forgave you after that; perhaps ye may be grateful.

\noindent[\textbf{2:53}]
And when we gave Moses the Scriptures and the Discrimination; perhaps ye will be
guided.

\noindent[\textbf{2:54}]
When Moses said to his people, 'O my people! Ye have wronged yourselves in taking
this calf; repent unto your Creator and kill each other; that will be better for you in your Creators
eyes; and He turned unto you, for He is the compassionate one easily turned.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:55}]
And when ye said to Moses, 'O Moses! we will not believe in thee until we see God
manifestly,' and the thunderbolt caught you while ye yet looked on.

\noindent[\textbf{2:56}]
Then we raised you up after your death; perhaps ye may be grateful.

\noindent[\textbf{2:57}]
And we overshadowed you with the cloud, and sent down the manna and the quails; 'Eat
of the good things we have given you.' They did not wrong us, but it was themselves they were
wronging.

\noindent[\textbf{2:58}]
And when we said, 'Enter this city and eat therefrom as plentifully as ye wish; and enter
the gate worshipping and say 'hittatun. So will we pardon you your sins and give increase unto
those who do well.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:59}]
But those who did wrong changed it for another word than that which was said to them:
and we sent down upon those who did wrong, wrath from heaven for that they had so sinned.

\noindent[\textbf{2:60}]
When Moses, too, asked drink for his people and we said, 'Strike with thy staff the rock,'
and from it burst forth twelve springs; each man among them knew his drinking place. 'Eat and
drink of what God has provided, and transgress not on the earth as evildoers.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:61}]
And when they said, 'O Moses, we cannot always bear one kind of food; pray then thy
Lord to bring forth for us of what the earth grows, its green herbs, its cucumbers, its garlic, its
lentils, and its onions.' Said he, 'Do ye ask what is meaner instead of what is best? Go down to
Egypt,--- there is what ye ask.' Then were they smitten with abasement and poverty, and met
with wrath from God. That was because they had misbelieved in God's signs and killed the
prophets undeservedly; that was for that they were rebellious and had transgressed.

\noindent[\textbf{2:62}]
Verily, whether it be of those who believe, or those who are Jews or Christians or
Sabaeans, whosoever believe in God and the last day and act aright, they have their reward at
their Lords hand, and there is no fear for them, nor shall they grieve.

\noindent[\textbf{2:63}]
And when we took a covenant with you and held the mountain over you; ' Accept what
we have brought you with strong will, and bear in mind what is therein, haply ye yet may fear.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:64}]
Then did ye turn aside after this, and were it not for God's grace towards you and His
mercy, ye would have been of those who lose.

\noindent[\textbf{2:65}]
Ye know too of those among you who transgressed upon the Sabbath, and we said,
'Become ye apes, despised and spurned.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:66}]
Thus we made them an example unto those who stood before them, and those who should
come after them, and a warning unto those who fear.

\noindent[\textbf{2:67}]
And when Moses said to his people, 'God bids you slaughter a cow,' they said, 'Art thou
making a jest of us?' Said he, 'I seek refuge with God from being one of the unwise.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:68}]
They said, 'Then pray thy Lord for us to show us what she is to be.' He answered, 'He
saith it is a cow, nor old, nor young, of middle age between the two; so do as ye are bid.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:69}]
They said, 'Pray now thy Lord to show us what her colour is to be.' He answered, 'He
saith it is a dun cow, intensely dun, her colour delighting those who look upon her.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:70}]
Again they said, 'Pray thy Lord to show us what she is to be; for cows appear the same to
us; then we, if God will, shall be guided.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:71}]
He answered, 'He saith, it is a cow, not broken in to plough the earth or irrigate the tilth,
a sound one with no blemish on her.' They said, 'Now hast thou brought the truth.' And they
slaughtered her, though they came near leaving it undone.

\noindent[\textbf{2:72}]
When too ye slew a soul and disputed thereupon, and God brought forth that which ye
had hidden,

\noindent[\textbf{2:73}]
then we said, 'Strike him with part of her.' Thus God brings the dead to life and shows
you His signs, that haply ye may understand.

\noindent[\textbf{2:74}]
Yet were your hearts hardened even after that, till they were as stones or harder still, for
verily of stones are some from which streams burst forth, and of them there are some that burst
asunder and the water issues out, and of them there are some that fall down for fear of God; but
God is never careless of what ye do.

\noindent[\textbf{2:75}]
Do ye crave that they should believe you when already a sect of them have heard the
word of God and then perverted it after they had understood it, though they knew?

\noindent[\textbf{2:76}]
And when they meet those who believe they say, 'We believe,' but when one goes aside
with another they say, 'Will ye talk to them of what God has opened up to you, that they may
argue with you upon it before your Lord? Do ye not therefore understand?'

\noindent[\textbf{2:77}]
Do they not then know that God knoweth what they keep secret and what they make
known abroad?

\noindent[\textbf{2:78}]
And some of them there are, illiterate folk, that know not the Book, but only idle tales;
for they do but fancy.

\noindent[\textbf{2:79}]
But woe to those who write out the Book with their hands and say 'this is from God;' to
buy therewith a little price! and woe to them for what their hands have written, and woe to them
for what they gain!

\noindent[\textbf{2:80}]
And then they say, 'Hell fire shall not touch us save for a number of days.' Say, 'Have ye
taken a covenant with God?' but God breaks not His covenant. Or do ye say of God that which
ye do not know?

\noindent[\textbf{2:81}]
Yea! whoso gains an evil gain, and is encompassed by his sins, those are the fellows of
the Fire, and they shall dwell therein for aye!

\noindent[\textbf{2:82}]
But such as act aright, those are the fellows of Paradise, and they shall dwell therein for
aye!

\noindent[\textbf{2:83}]
And when we took from the children of Israel a covenant, saying, 'Serve ye none but
God, and to your two parents show kindness, and to your kindred and the orphans and the poor,
and speak to men kindly, and be steadfast in prayer, and give alms;' and then ye turned back,
save a few of you, and swerved aside.

\noindent[\textbf{2:84}]
And when we took covenant from you, 'shed ye not your kinsmans blood, nor turn your
kinsmen out of their homes:' then did ye confirm it and were witnesses thereto.

\noindent[\textbf{2:85}]
Yet ye were those who slay your kinsmen and turn a party out of their homes, and back
each other up against them with sin and enmity. But if they come to you as captives ye ransom
them!--- and yet it is as unlawful for you to turn them out. Do ye then believe in part of the Book
and disbelieve in part? But the reward of such among you as do that shall be nought else but
disgrace in this worldly life, and on the day of the resurrection shall they be driven to the most
grievous torment, for God is not unmindful of what ye do.

\noindent[\textbf{2:86}]
Those who have bought this worldly life with the Future, the torment shall not be
lightened from them nor shall they be helped.

\noindent[\textbf{2:87}]
We gave Moses the Book and we followed him up with other apostles, and we gave Jesus
the son of Mary manifest signs and aided him with the Holy Spirit. Do ye then, every time an
apostle comes to you with what your souls love not, proudly scorn him, and charge a part with
lying and slay a part?

\noindent[\textbf{2:88}]
They say, 'Our hearts are uncircumcised;' nay, God has cursed them in their unbelief,
and few it is who do believe.

\noindent[\textbf{2:89}]
And when a book came down from God confirming what they had with them, though
they had before prayed for victory over those who misbelieve, yet when that came to them which
they knew, then they disbelieved it,--- God's curse be on the misbelievers.

\noindent[\textbf{2:90}]
For a bad bargain have they sold their souls, not to believe in what God has revealed,
grudging because God sends down of His grace on whomsoever of His servants He will; and
they have brought on themselves wrath after wrath and for the misbelievers is there shameful
woe.

\noindent[\textbf{2:91}]
And when they are told to believe in what God has revealed, they say, 'We believe in
what has been revealed to us;' but they disbelieve in all beside, although it is the truth confirming
what they have. Say, 'Wherefore did ye kill God's prophets of yore if ye were true believers?'

\noindent[\textbf{2:92}]
Moses came to you with manifest signs, then ye took up with the calf when he had gone
and did so wrong.

\noindent[\textbf{2:93}]
And when we took a covenant with you and raised the mountain over you, 'Take what we
have given you with resolution and hear;' they said, 'We hear but disobey;' and they were made
to drink the calf down into their hearts for their unbelief. Say, 'An evil thing is it which your
belief bids you do, if ye be true believers.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:94}]
Say, 'If the abode of the future with God is yours alone and not mankinds: long for death
then if ye speak the truth.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:95}]
But they will never long for it because of what their hands have sent on before; but God
is knowing as to the wrong doers.

\noindent[\textbf{2:96}]
Why, thou wilt find them the greediest of men for life; and of those who associate others
with God one would fain live for a thousand years,--- but he will not be reprieved from
punishment by being let live, for God seeth what they do.

\noindent[\textbf{2:97}]
Say, 'Who is an enemy to Gabriel?' for he hath revealed to thy heart, with God's
permission, confirmation of what had been before, and a guidance and glad tidings to believers.

\noindent[\textbf{2:98}]
Who is an enemy to God and His angels and His apostles and Gabriel and Michael?---
Verily, God is an enemy to the unbelievers.

\noindent[\textbf{2:99}]
We have sent down to thee conspicuous signs, and none will disbelieve therein except the
evildoers.

\noindent[\textbf{2:100}]
Or every time they make a covenant, will a part of them repudiate it? Nay, most of them
do not believe.

\noindent[\textbf{2:101}]
And when there comes to them an apostle confirming what they have, a part of those who
have received the Book repudiate God's book, casting it behind their backs as though they did
not know.

\noindent[\textbf{2:102}]
And they follow that which the devils recited against Solomons kingdom;--- it was not
Solomon who misbelieved, but the devils who misbelieved, teaching men sorcery,--- and what
has been revealed to the two angels at Babylon, Harut and Marut; yet these taught no one until
they said, 'We are but a temptation, so do not misbelieve.' Men learn from them only that by
which they may part man and wife; but they can harm no one therewith, unless with the
permission of God, and they learn what hurts them and profits them not. And yet they knew that
he who purchased it would have no portion in the future; but sad is the price at which they have
sold their souls, had they but known.

\noindent[\textbf{2:103}]
But had they believed and feared, a reward from God were better, had they but known.

\noindent[\textbf{2:104}]
O ye who believe! say not 'ra'hina,' but say 'unthurna,' and hearken; for unto
misbelievers shall be grievous woe.

\noindent[\textbf{2:105}]
They who misbelieve, whether of those who have the Book or of the idolaters, would fain
that no good were sent down to you from your Lord; but God specially favours with His mercy
whom He will, for God is Lord of mighty grace.

\noindent[\textbf{2:106}]
Whatever verse we may annul or cause thee to forget, we will bring a better one than it,
or one like it; dost thou not know that God is mighty over all?

\noindent[\textbf{2:107}]
Dost thou not know that God's is the kingdom of the heavens and the earth? nor have ye
besides God a patron or a help.

\noindent[\textbf{2:108}]
Do ye wish to question your apostle as Moses was questioned aforetime? but whoso takes
misbelief in exchange for faith has erred from the level road.

\noindent[\textbf{2:109}]
Many of those who have the Book would fain turn you back into misbelievers after ye
have once believed, through envy from themselves, after the truth has been made manifest to
them; but pardon and shun them till God brings His command; verily, God is mighty over all.

\noindent[\textbf{2:110}]
Be ye steadfast in prayer, and give alms; and whatsoever good ye send before for your
own souls, ye shall find it with God, for God in all ye do doth see.

\noindent[\textbf{2:111}]
They say, 'None shall enter Paradise save such as be Jews or Christians;' that is their
faith. Say thou, 'Bring your proofs, if ye be speaking truth.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:112}]
Aye, he who resigns his face to God, and who is kind, he shall have his reward from his
Lord, and no fear shall be on them, and they shall not grieve.

\noindent[\textbf{2:113}]
The Jews say, 'The Christians rest on nought;' and the Christians say, 'The Jews rest on
nought;' and yet they read the Book. So, too, say those who know not, like to what these say; but
God shall judge between them on the resurrection day concerning that whereon they do dispute.

\noindent[\textbf{2:114}]
But who is more unjust than he who prohibits God's mosques, that His name should not
be mentioned there, and who strives to ruin them? 'Tis not for such to enter into them except in
fear, for them is disgrace in this world, and in the future mighty woe.

\noindent[\textbf{2:115}]
God's is the east and the west, and wherever ye turn there is God's face; verily, God
comprehends and knows.

\noindent[\textbf{2:116}]
They say, 'God takes unto Himself a son.' Celebrated be His praise! Nay, His is what is
in the heavens and the earth, and Him all things obey.

\noindent[\textbf{2:117}]
The Originator of the heavens and the earth, when He decrees a matter He doth but say
unto it, 'BE,' and it is.

\noindent[\textbf{2:118}]
And those who do not know (the Scriptures) say, 'Unless God speak to us, or there comes
a sign.' So spake those before them like unto their speech. Their hearts are all alike. We have
made manifest the signs unto a people that are sure.

\noindent[\textbf{2:119}]
We have sent thee with the truth, a bearer of good tidings and of warning, and thou shalt
not be questioned as to the fellows of hell.

\noindent[\textbf{2:120}]
The Jews will not be satisfied with thee, nor yet the Christians, until thou followest their
creed. Say, 'God's guidance is the guidance;' and if thou followest their lusts after the
knowledge that has come to thee, thou hast not then from God a patron or a help.

\noindent[\textbf{2:121}]
They to whom we have brought the Book and who read it as it should be read, believe
therein; and whoso disbelieve therein, 'tis they who lose thereby.

\noindent[\textbf{2:122}]
O children of Israel! remember my favours with which I favoured you, and that I have
preferred you over the worlds.

\noindent[\textbf{2:123}]
And fear the day when no soul shall pay a recompense for a soul, nor shall an equivalent
be received therefrom, nor any intercession avail; and they shall not be helped.

\noindent[\textbf{2:124}]
And when his Lord tried Abraham with words, and he fulfilled them, He said, 'Verily, I
will set thee as a high priest for men.' Said he, ' And of my seed?' God said, 'My covenant
touches not the evildoers.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:125}]
And when we made the House a place of resort unto men, and a sanctuary, and (said)
take the station of Abraham for a place of prayer; and covenanted with Abraham and Ishmael,
saying, 'Do ye two cleanse my house for those who make the circuit, for those who pay
devotions there, for those who bow down, and for those too who adore.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:126}]
When Abraham said, 'Lord, make this a town of safety, and provide the dwellers there
with fruits, such as believe in God and the last day!' (God) said, ' And he who misbelieves, I will
give him but little to enjoy, then will drive him to the torment of the fire, an evil journey will it
be.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:127}]
And when Abraham raised up the foundations of the House with Ishmael, 'Lord! receive
it from us, verily, thou art hearing and dost know.

\noindent[\textbf{2:128}]
Lord! and make us too resigned unto Thee, and of our seed also a nation resigned unto
Thee, and show us our rites, and turn towards us, verily, Thou art easy to be turned and merciful.

\noindent[\textbf{2:129}]
Lord! and send them an apostle from amongst themselves, to read to them Thy signs and
teach them the Book and wisdom, and to purify them; verily, Thou art the mighty and the wise.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:130}]
Who is averse from the faith of Abraham save one who is foolish of soul? for we have
chosen him in this world, and in the future he is surely of the righteous.

\noindent[\textbf{2:131}]
When his Lord said to him, 'Be resigned,' he said, 'I am resigned unto the Lord of the
worlds.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:132}]
And Abraham instructed his sons therein, and Jacob (saying), 'O my sons! verily, God
has chosen for you a religion, do not therefore die unless ye be resigned.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:133}]
Were ye then witnesses when Jacob was facing death, when he said to his sons, 'What
will ye serve when I am gone?' They said, 'We will serve thy God, the God of thy fathers
Abraham, and Ishmael, and Isaac, one God; and we are unto Him resigned.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:134}]
That is a nation that has passed away, theirs is what they gained; and yours shall be what
ye have gained; ye shall not be questioned as to that which they have done.

\noindent[\textbf{2:135}]
They say, 'Be ye Jews or Christians so shall ye of Abraham be guided.' Say, 'Not so! but
the faith of Abraham the 'Hanif, be was not of the idolaters.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:136}]
Say ye, 'We believe in God, and what has been revealed to us, and what has been
revealed to Abraham, and Ishmael, and Isaac, and Jacob, and the Tribes, and what was brought
to Moses and Jesus, and what was brought unto the Prophets from their Lord; we will not
distinguish between any one of them, and unto Him are we resigned.

\noindent[\textbf{2:137}]
If they believe in that in which ye believe, then are they guided; but if they turn back,
then are they only in a schism, and God will suffice thee against them, for He both hears and
knows.

\noindent[\textbf{2:138}]
The dye of God! and who is better than God at dyeing? and we are worshippers of Him.

\noindent[\textbf{2:139}]
Say, 'Do ye dispute with us concerning God, and He is our Lord and your Lord? Ye have
your works and we have ours, and unto Him are we sincere.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:140}]
Do ye say that Abraham, and Ishmael, and Isaac, and Jacob, and the Tribes were Jews or
Christians? Say, 'Are ye more knowing than God? Who is more unjust than one who conceals a
testimony that he has from God?' But God is not careless of what ye do.

\noindent[\textbf{2:141}]
That is a nation that has passed away; theirs is what they gained, and yours shall be what
ye have gained; ye shall not be questioned as to that which they have done.

\noindent[\textbf{2:142}]
The fools among men will say, 'What has turned them from their qiblah, on which they
were agreed?' Say, 'God's is the east and the west, He guides whom He will unto the right path.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:143}]
Thus have we made you a middle nation, to be witnesses against men, and that the
Apostle may be a witness against you. We have not appointed the qiblah on which thou wert
agreed, save that we might know who follows the Apostle from him who turns upon his heels
although it is a great thing save to those whom God doth guide. But God will not waste your
faith, for verily, God with men is kind and merciful.

\noindent[\textbf{2:144}]
We see thee often turn about thy face in the heavens, but we will surely turn thee to a
qiblah thou shalt like. Turn then thy face towards the Sacred Mosque; wherever ye be, turn your
faces towards it; for verily, those who have the Book know that it is the truth from their Lord;---
God is not careless of that which ye do.

\noindent[\textbf{2:145}]
And if thou shouldst bring to those who have been given the Book every sign, they would
not follow your qiblah; and thou art not to follow their qiblah; nor do some of them follow the
qiblah of the others: and if thou followest their lusts after the knowledge that has come to thee
then art thou of the evildoers.

\noindent[\textbf{2:146}]
Those whom we have given the Book know him as they know their sons, although a sect
of them do surely hide the truth, the while they know.

\noindent[\textbf{2:147}]
The truth (is) from thy Lord; be not therefore one of those who doubt thereof.

\noindent[\textbf{2:148}]
Every sect has some one side to which they turn (in prayer); but do ye hasten onwards to
good works; wherever ye are God will bring you all together; verily, God is mighty over all.

\noindent[\textbf{2:149}]
From whencesoever thou comest forth, there turn thy face towards the Sacred Mosque,
for it is surely truth from thy Lord; God is not careless about what ye do.

\noindent[\textbf{2:150}]
And from whencesoever thou comest forth, there turn thy face towards the Sacred
Mosque, and wheresoever ye are, turn your faces towards it, that men may have no argument
against you, save only those of them who are unjust; and fear them not, but fear me and I will
fulfil my favours to you, perchance ye may be guided yet.

\noindent[\textbf{2:151}]
Thus have we sent amongst you an apostle of yourselves, to recite to you our signs, to
purify you and teach you the Book and wisdom, and to teach you what ye did not know;

\noindent[\textbf{2:152}]
remember me, then, and I will remember you; thank me, and do not misbelieve.

\noindent[\textbf{2:153}]
O ye who do believe! seek aid from patience and from prayer, verily, God is with the
patient.

\noindent[\textbf{2:154}]
And say not of those who are slain in God's way (that they are) dead, but rather living;
but ye do not perceive.

\noindent[\textbf{2:155}]
We will try you with something of fear, and hunger and loss of wealth, and souls and
fruit; but give good tidings to the patient,

\noindent[\textbf{2:156}]
who when there falls on them a calamity say, 'Verily, we are God's and, verily, to Him
do we return.'

\noindent[\textbf{2:157}]
These, on them are blessings from their Lord and mercy, and they it is who are guided.

\noindent[\textbf{2:158}]
Verily, Zafa and Merwah are of the beacons of God, and he who makes the pilgrimage
unto the House, or visits it, it is no crime for him to compass them both about; and he who obeys
his own impulse to a good work,--- God is grateful and doth know.

\noindent[\textbf{2:159}]
Verily, those who hide what we have revealed of manifest signs and of guidance after we
have manifested it to men in the Book, them God shall curse, and those who curse shall curse
them too.

\noindent[\textbf{2:160}]
Save those who turn and do right and make (the signs) manifest; these will I turn to again,
for I am easy to be turned and merciful.

\noindent[\textbf{2:161}]
Verily, those who misbelieve and die while still in misbelief, on them is the curse of God,
and of the angels, and of mankind altogether;

\noindent[\textbf{2:162}]
to dwell therein for aye; the torment shall not be lightened for them, nor shall they be
looked upon.

\noindent[\textbf{2:163}]
Your God is one God; there is no God but He, the merciful, the compassionate.

\noindent[\textbf{2:164}]
Verily, in the creation of the heavens and the earth, and the alternation of night and day,
and in the ship that runneth in the sea with that which profits man, and in what water God sends
down from heaven and quickens therewith the earth after its death, and spreads abroad therein all
kinds of cattle, and in the shifting of the winds, and in the clouds that are pressed into service
betwixt heaven and earth, are signs to people who can understand.

\noindent[\textbf{2:165}]
Yet are there some amongst mankind who take to themselves peers other than God; they
love them as they should love God while those who believe love God more. O that those who are
unjust could only see, when they see the torment, that power is altogether God's! Verily, God is
keen to torment.

\noindent[\textbf{2:166}]
When those who are followed clear themselves of those who followed them, and see the
torment, and the cords are cut asunder,

\noindent[\textbf{2:167}]
those who followed shall say, 'Had we but another turn, then would we clear ourselves of
them as they have cleared themselves of us.' So will God show them their works; for them are
sighs, and they shall not come forth from out the fire.

\noindent[\textbf{2:168}]
O ye folk! eat of what is in the earth, things lawful and things good, and follow not the
footsteps of Satan, verily, to you he is an open foe.

\noindent[\textbf{2:169}]
He does but bid you evil and sin, and that ye should speak against God what ye do not
know.

\noindent[\textbf{2:170}]
When it is said to them, 'Follow what God has revealed,' they say, 'Nay, we will follow
what we found our fathers agreed upon.' What! and though their fathers had no sense at all or
guidance ---?

\noindent[\textbf{2:171}]
The likeness of those who misbelieve is as the likeness of him who shouts to that which
hears him not, save only a call and a cry; deafness, dumbness, blindness, and they shall not
understand.

\section{Surah 3: The Chapter of Imran's Family (Medina).}
\vspace{-1em}(selection)

IN the name of the merciful and compassionate God.

\noindent[\textbf{3:1}]
ALIF LAM MIM.

\noindent[\textbf{3:2}]
God, there is no god but He, the living, the self-subsistent.

\noindent[\textbf{3:3}]
He has sent down to thee the Book, in truth, confirming what was before it, and has
revealed the law, and the gospel

\noindent[\textbf{3:4}]
before for the guidance of men, and has revealed the Discrimination. Verily, those who
disbelieve in the signs of God, for them is severe torment, for God is mighty and avenging.

\noindent[\textbf{3:5}]
Verily, God, there is nothing hidden from Him in the earth, nor in the heaven;

\noindent[\textbf{3:6}]
He it is who fashions you in the womb as He pleases. There is no God but He, the
mighty, the wise.

\noindent[\textbf{3:7}]
He it is who has revealed to thee the Book, of which there are some verses that are
decisive, they are the mother of the Book; and others ambiguous; but as for those in whose hearts
is perversity, they follow what is ambiguous, and do crave for sedition, craving for (their own)
interpretation of it; but none know the interpretation of it except God. But those who are well
grounded in knowledge say, 'We believe in it; it is all from our Lord; but none will remember
save those who possess minds.

\noindent[\textbf{3:8}]
'O Lord! pervert not our hearts again when Thou hast guided them, and grant us mercy
from Thee, for Thou art He who grants.

\noindent[\textbf{3:9}]
O Lord! Thou shalt gather together men unto the day wherein is no doubt. Verily, God
will not depart from His promise.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:10}]
Verily, those who misbelieve, their wealth shall not help them, nor their children, against
God at all; and they it is who are the fuel of the fire.

\noindent[\textbf{3:11}]
As was the wont of Pharaoh's people, and those before them, they said our signs were
lies, and God caught them up in their sins, for God is severe to punish.

\noindent[\textbf{3:12}]
Say to those who misbelieve, 'Ye shall be overcome and driven together to hell, an ill
couch will it be.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:13}]
'Ye have had a sign in the two parties who met; one party fighting in the way of God, the
other misbelieving; these saw twice the same number as themselves to the eye-sight, for God
aids with His help those whom He pleases.' Verily, in that is a lesson for those who have
perception.

\noindent[\textbf{3:14}]
Seemly unto men is a life of lusts, of women, and children, and hoarded talents of gold
and silver, and of horses well-bred, and cattle, and tilth;--- that is the provision for the life of this
world; but God, with Him is the best resort.

\noindent[\textbf{3:15}]
Say, 'But shall we tell you of a better thing than this?' For those who fear are gardens
with their Lord, beneath which rivers flow; they shall dwell therein for aye, and pure wives and
grace from God; the Lord looks on His servants,

\noindent[\textbf{3:16}]
who say, 'Lord, we believe, pardon Thou our sins and keep us from the torment of the
fire,'

\noindent[\textbf{3:17}]
the patient, the truthful, the devout, and those who ask for pardon at the dawn.

\noindent[\textbf{3:18}]
God bears witness that there is no god but He, and the angels, and those possessed of
knowledge standing up for justice. There is no God but He, the mighty, the wise.

\noindent[\textbf{3:19}]
Verily, (the true) religion in God's sight is Islam, and those to whom the Book was given
disagreed not until after that there was given to them knowledge, through mutual envy. But
whoso disbelieves in God's signs, truly God is quick at reckoning up.

\noindent[\textbf{3:20}]
And if they would dispute with thee, then say, 'I turn my face with resignation unto God,
and whoso follows me.' And say to those who have been given the Book, unto the Gentiles, 'Are
ye, too, resigned' and if they are resigned, then are they guided. But if they turn their backs, then
thou hast only to preach, and God looks on his servants.

\noindent[\textbf{3:21}]
Verily, those who disbelieve in God's signs, and kill the prophets without right, and kill
those from among men, who bid what is just,--- to them give the glad tidings of grievous woe!

\noindent[\textbf{3:22}]
These are they whose works are void in this world and the next, and helpers have they
none.

\noindent[\textbf{3:23}]
Did ye not see those who have been given a portion of the Book? they were called unto
the Book of God to decide between them; and then a sect of them turned their backs and turned
away;---

\noindent[\textbf{3:24}]
that is because they say the fire shall not touch us save for a certain number of days. But
that deceived them in their religion which they had invented.

\noindent[\textbf{3:25}]
How will it be when we have gathered them together for a day whereof there is no doubt,
when each soul shall be paid what it has earned, and they shall not be wronged?

\noindent[\textbf{3:26}]
Say, 'O God, Lord of the kingdom! Thou givest the kingdom to whomsoever Thou
pleasest, and strippest the kingdom from whomsoever Thou pleasest; Thou honourest whom
Thou pleasest, and abasest whom Thou pleasest; in Thy hand is good. Verily, Thou art mighty
over all.

\noindent[\textbf{3:27}]
Thou dost turn night to day, and dost turn day to night, and dost bring forth the living
from the dead, and dost provide for whom Thou pleasest without taking count.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:28}]
Those who believe shall not take misbelievers for their patrons, rather than believers, and
he who does this has no part with God at all, unless, indeed, ye fear some danger from them. But
God bids you beware of Himself, for unto Him your journey is.

\noindent[\textbf{3:29}]
Say, 'If ye hide that which is in your breasts, or if ye show it, God knows it: He knows
what is in the heavens and what is in the earth, for God is mighty over all.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:30}]
The day that every soul shall find what it has done of good present before it; and what it
has done of evil, it would fain that there were between itself and that a wide interval. 'God bids
you beware of Himself, but God is gentle with His servants.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:31}]
Say, 'If ye would love God then follow me, and God will love you and forgive you your
sins, for God is forgiving and merciful.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:32}]
Say, 'Obey God and the Apostle; but if ye turn your backs God loves not misbelievers.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:33}]
Verily, God has chosen Adam, and Noah, and Abraham's people, and Imran's people
above the world,---

\noindent[\textbf{3:34}]
a seed, of which one succeeds the other, but God both hears and knows.

\noindent[\textbf{3:35}]
When Imran's wife said, 'Lord! I have vowed to Thee what is within my womb, to be
dedicated unto Thee, receive it then from me. Verily, Thou dost hear and know.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:36}]
And when she brought it forth she said, 'Verily, I have brought it forth a female' --- but
God knew best what she brought forth; and a male is not like a female --- 'I have called her
Mary, and I seek a refuge in Thee for her and for her seed from Satan the pelted.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:37}]
And her Lord received her with a good reception, and made her grow up with a good
growth, and Zachariah took care of her. Whenever Zachariah entered the chamber to her he
found beside her a provision, and said, 'O Mary, how hast thou this?' She said, 'It is from God,
for God provides for whom He pleases without count.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:38}]
Therefore prayed Zachariah to his Lord, and said, 'Lord, grant me from Thee a good
seed. Verily, Thou hearest prayer.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:39}]
And an angel cried out to him as he was standing praying in the chamber (and said) that
'God gives thee the glad tidings of John, to confirm the Word from God,--- of a chief and a
chaste one, and a prophet from amongst the righteous.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:40}]
He said, 'My Lord, how can there be to me a boy when old age has reached me, and my
wife is barren?' Said he, 'Thus God does what He pleaseth.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:41}]
He said, 'My Lord, make for me a sign.' He said, 'Thy sign is that thou shalt not speak to
men for three days, save by gesture; but remember thy Lord much, and celebrate His praises in
the evening and the morning.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:42}]
And when the angels said, 'O Mary! verily, God has chosen thee, and has purified thee,
and has chosen thee above the women of the world.

\noindent[\textbf{3:43}]
O Mary! be devout unto thy Lord, and adore and bow down with those who bow.

\noindent[\textbf{3:44}]
That is (one) of the declarations of the unseen world which we reveal to thee, though thou
wert not by them when they threw their lots which of them should take care of Mary, nor were ye
by them when they did dispute.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:45}]
When the angel said, 'O Mary! verily, God gives thee the glad tidings of a Word from
Him; his name shall be the Messiah Jesus the son of Mary, regarded in this world and the next
and of those whose place is nigh to God.

\noindent[\textbf{3:46}]
And he shall speak to people in his cradle, and when grown up, and shall be among the
righteous.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:47}]
She said, 'Lord! how can I have a son, when man has not yet touched me?' He said,
'Thus God creates what He pleaseth. When He decrees a matter He only says BE and it is;

\noindent[\textbf{3:48}]
and He will teach him the Book, and wisdom, and the law, and the gospel,

\noindent[\textbf{3:49}]
and he shall be a prophet to the people of Israel (saying), that I have come to you, with a
sign from God, namely, that I will create for you out of clay as though it were the form of a bird,
and I will blow thereon and it shall become a bird by God's permission; and I will heal the blind
from birth, and lepers; and I will bring the dead to life by God's permission; and I will tell you
what you eat and what ye store up in your houses. Verily, in that is a sign for you if ye be
believers.

\noindent[\textbf{3:50}]
And I will confirm what is before you of the law, and will surely make lawful for you
some of that which was prohibited from you. I have come to you with a sign from your Lord, so
fear God and follow me,

\noindent[\textbf{3:51}]
for God is my Lord, and your Lord, so worship Him:--- this is the right path.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:52}]
And when Jesus perceived their unbelief, He said, 'Who are my helpers for God?' Said
the apostles, 'We are God's helpers. We believe in God, so bear witness that we are resigned.

\noindent[\textbf{3:53}]
Lord, we have believed in what Thou hast revealed, and we have followed the Apostle, so
write us down with those which bear witness.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:54}]
But they (the Jews) were crafty, and God was crafty, for God is the best of crafty ones!

\noindent[\textbf{3:55}]
When God said, 'O Jesus! I will make Thee die and take Thee up again to me and will
clear thee of those who misbelieve, and will make those who follow thee above those who
misbelieve, at the day of judgment, then to me is your return. I will decide between you
concerning that wherein ye disagree.

\noindent[\textbf{3:56}]
And as for those who misbelieve, I will punish them with grievous punishment in this
world and the next, and they shall have none to help them.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:57}]
But as for those who believe and do what is right, He will pay them their reward, for God
loves not the unjust.

\noindent[\textbf{3:58}]
That is what we recite to thee of the signs and of the wise reminder.

\noindent[\textbf{3:59}]
Verily the likeness of Jesus with God is as the likeness of Adam. He created him from
earth, then He said to him BE, and he was;---

\noindent[\textbf{3:60}]
the truth from thy Lord, so be thou not of those who are in doubt.

\noindent[\textbf{3:61}]
And whoso disputeth with thee after what has come to thee of knowledge, say, 'Come, let
us call our sons and your sons, and our women and your women, and ourselves and yourselves:
then we will imprecate and put God's curse on those who lie.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:62}]
Verily, those are the true stories, and there is no god but God, and, verily, God He is the
mighty, the wise;

\noindent[\textbf{3:63}]
but if they turn back, God knows the evildoers.

\noindent[\textbf{3:64}]
Say, 'O ye people of the Book, come to a word laid down plainly between us and you,
that we will not serve other than God, nor associate aught with him, nor take each other for lords
rather than God.' But if they turn back then say, 'Bear witness that we are resigned.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:65}]
O people of the Book, why do ye dispute about Abraham, when the law and the gospel
were not revealed until after him? What! do ye not understand?

\noindent[\textbf{3:66}]
Here ye are, disputing about what ye have some knowledge of; why then do ye dispute
about what ye have no knowledge of? God knows and ye know not.

\noindent[\textbf{3:67}]
Abraham was not a Jew, nor yet a Christian, but he was a 'Hanif resigned, and not of the
idolaters.

\noindent[\textbf{3:68}]
Verily, the people most worthy of Abraham are those who follow him and his prophets,
and those who believe;--- God is the patron of the believers.

\noindent[\textbf{3:69}]
A sect of the people of the Book would fain they could lead you astray, but they only lead
themselves astray, and they do not perceive.

\noindent[\textbf{3:70}]
O people of the Book! why do ye disbelieve in the signs of God, the while ye witness
them?

\noindent[\textbf{3:71}]
O people of the Book! why do ye clothe the truth with falsehood and hide the truth the
while ye know?

\noindent[\textbf{3:72}]
A sect of the people of the Book say, 'Believe in what was revealed to those who
believed at the first appearance of the day, and disbelieve it at the end thereof,' --- that (others)
may perchance go back (from their faith)---

\noindent[\textbf{3:73}]
'do not believe save one who followeth your religion.' Say, 'Verily, the (true) guidance is
the guidance of God, that one should be given like what ye are given.' Or would they dispute
with you before your Lord, say, 'Grace is in the hand of God, He gives it to whom he pleases, for
God both comprehends and knows.

\noindent[\textbf{3:74}]
He specially favours with his mercy whom he pleases, for God is Lord of mighty grace.

\noindent[\textbf{3:75}]
And of the people of the Book, there are some of them who, if thou entrust them with a
talent give it back to you; and some of them, if thou entrust them with a dinar, he will not give it
back to thee except so long as thou dost stand over him. That is because they say, We owe no
duty to the Gentiles;' but they tell a lie against God, the while they know.

\noindent[\textbf{3:76}]
Yea, whoso fulfils his covenant and fears,--- verily, God loves those who fear.

\noindent[\textbf{3:77}]
Those who sell God's covenant and their oaths for a little price, these have no portion in
the future life. God will not speak to them, and will not look upon them on the resurrection day,
and will not purify them; but for them is grievous woe.

\noindent[\textbf{3:78}]
And, verily, amongst them is a sect who twist their tongues concerning the Book, that ye
may reckon it to be from the Book, but it is not from the Book. They say, 'It is from God,' but it
is not from God, and they tell a lie against God, the while they know.

\noindent[\textbf{3:79}]
It is not right for a man that God should give him a Book, and judgment, and prophecy,
and that then he should say to men, 'Be ye servants of mine rather than of God;' but be ye rather
masters of teaching the Book and of what ye learn.

\noindent[\textbf{3:80}]
He does not bid you take the angels and the prophets for your lords; shall He bid you
misbelieve again when you are once resigned?

\noindent[\textbf{3:81}]
And when God took the compact from the prophets '(this is) surely what we have given
you of the Book and wisdom. Then shall come to you the Apostle confirming what is with you.
Ye must believe in him and help him.' He said, moreover, 'Are ye resolved and have ye taken
my compact on that (condition).' They say, 'We are resolved.' He said, 'Then bear witness, for I
am witness with you;

\noindent[\textbf{3:82}]
but he who turns back after that, these are sinners.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:83}]
What is it other than God's religion that they crave? when to Him is resigned whosoever
is in the heavens and the earth, will he or nill he, and to him shall they return!

\noindent[\textbf{3:84}]
Say, 'We believe in God, and what has been revealed to thee, and what was revealed to
Abraham, and Ishmael, and Isaac, and Jacob, and the tribes, and what was given to Moses, and
Jesus, and the prophets from their Lord,--- we will make no distinction between any of them,---
and we are unto Him resigned.

\noindent[\textbf{3:85}]
Whosoever craves other than Islam for a religion, it shall surely not be accepted from
him, and he shall, in the next world, be of those who lose.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:86}]
How shall God guide people who have disbelieved after believing and bearing witness
that the Apostle is true, and after there come to them manifest signs? God guides the unjust folk.

\noindent[\textbf{3:87}]
These, their reward is, that on them is the curse of God, and of the angels, and of men
together;

\noindent[\textbf{3:88}]
they shall dwell therein for aye --- the torment shall not be alleviated from them, nor shall
they be respited;

\noindent[\textbf{3:89}]
save those who repent after that, and act aright, for verily, God is forgiving and merciful.

\noindent[\textbf{3:90}]
Verily, those who misbelieve after believing, and then increase in misbelief, their
repentance shall not be accepted; these are those who err.

\noindent[\textbf{3:91}]
Verily, those who misbelieve and die in misbelief, there shall not be accepted from any
one of them the earth---full of gold, though he should give it as a ransom. For them is grievous
woe, and helpers have they none.

\noindent[\textbf{3:92}]
Ye cannot attain to righteousness until ye expend in alms of what ye love. But what ye
expend in alms, that God knows.

\noindent[\textbf{3:93}]
All food was lawful to the children of Israel save what Israel made unlawful to himself
before that the law was revealed. Say, 'Bring the law and recite it, if ye speak the truth.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:94}]
But whoso forges against God a lie, after that, they are the unjust.

\noindent[\textbf{3:95}]
Say, 'God speaks the truth, then follow the faith of Abraham, a 'hanif, who was not of the
idolaters.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:96}]
Verily, the first House founded for men was surely that at Bekkah, for a blessing and a
guidance to the worlds.

\noindent[\textbf{3:97}]
Therein are manifest signs,--- Abraham's station, and whosoever enters in is safe. There
is due to God from man a pilgrimage unto the House, for whosoever can find his way there. But
whoso misbelieves --- God is independent of the worlds.

\noindent[\textbf{3:98}]
Say, 'O people of the Book! why do ye misbelieve in God's signs, while God is witness
of what ye do?'

\noindent[\textbf{3:99}]
Say, 'O people of the Book! why do ye turn from the way of God him who believes,
craving to make it crooked, while ye are witnesses? But God is not careless of what ye do.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:100}]
O ye who believe! if ye obey the sect of those to whom the Book was brought, they will
turn you, after your faith, to unbelievers again.

\noindent[\textbf{3:101}]
How can ye misbelieve while unto you are recited the signs of God, and among you is
His Apostle? But whoso takes tight hold on God, he is guided into the right way.

\noindent[\textbf{3:102}]
O ye who believe! fear God with the fear that He deserves, and die not save ye be
resigned.

\noindent[\textbf{3:103}]
Take tight hold of God's rope altogether, and do not part in sects; but remember the
favours of God towards you, when ye were enemies and He made friendship between your
hearts, and on the morrow ye were, by His favour, brothers. Ye were on the edge of a pit of fire,
but he rescued you therefrom. Thus does God show to you His signs, perchance ye may be
guided;

\noindent[\textbf{3:104}]
and that there may be of you a nation who shall invite to good, and bid what is
reasonable, and forbid what is wrong; these are the prosperous.

\noindent[\textbf{3:105}]
Be not like those who parted in sects and disagreed after there came to them manifest
signs; for them is mighty woe,

\noindent[\textbf{3:106}]
on the day when faces shall be whitened and faces shall be blackened. As for those whose
faces are blackened,--- 'Did ye misbelieve after your faith, then taste the torment for your
misbelief!'

\noindent[\textbf{3:107}]
But as for those whose faces are whitened, they are in God's mercy, and they shall dwell
therein for aye.

\noindent[\textbf{3:108}]
These are the signs of God. We recite them to you in truth, for God desires not wrong
unto the worlds.

\noindent[\textbf{3:109}]
God's is what is in the heavens and what is in the earth, and unto God affairs return.

\noindent[\textbf{3:110}]
Ye were the best of nations brought forth unto man. Ye bid what is reasonable, and forbid
what is wrong, believing in God. Had the people of the Book believed, it would have been better
for them. There are believers among them, though most of them are sinners.

\noindent[\textbf{3:111}]
They shall surely not harm you save a hurt; and if they fight you, they shall show you
their backs, then they shall not be helped.

\noindent[\textbf{3:112}]
They are smitten with abasement wherever they be found, save for the rope of God and
the rope of man; and they draw on themselves wrath from God. They are smitten, too, with
poverty; that is because they did disbelieve in God's signs, and kill the prophets undeservedly.
That is because they did rebel and did transgress.

\noindent[\textbf{3:113}]
They are not all alike. Of the people of the Book there is a nation upright, reciting God's
signs throughout the night, as they adore the while.

\noindent[\textbf{3:114}]
They believe in God, and in the last day, and bid what is reasonable, and forbid what is
wrong, and vie in charity; these are among the righteous.

\noindent[\textbf{3:115}]
What ye do of good surely God will not deny, for God knows those who fear.

\noindent[\textbf{3:116}]
Verily, those who misbelieve, their wealth is of no service to them, nor their children
either, against God; they are the fellows of the Fire, and they shall dwell therein for aye.

\noindent[\textbf{3:117}]
The likeness of what they expend in this life of the world, is as the likeness of wind
wherein is a cold blast that falls upon a people's tilth who have wronged themselves and destroys
it. It is not God who wrongs them, but it is themselves they wrong.

\noindent[\textbf{3:118}]
O ye who believe! take not to intimacy with others than yourselves; they will not fail to
spoil you; they would fain ye came to trouble,--- hatred is shown by their mouths; but what their
breasts conceal is greater still. We have made manifest to you our signs, did ye but understand.

\noindent[\textbf{3:119}]
Ye it is who love them, but they love not you; and ye believe in the Book, all of it. But
when they meet you they say, 'We believe;' and when they go aside they bite their finger tips at
you through rage. Say, 'Die in your rage, for God doth know the nature of men's breasts.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:120}]
If good luck touch you it is bad for them, but if bad luck befall you they rejoice therein;
yet if ye are patient and fear, their tricks shall not harm you, for what they do God comprehends.

\noindent[\textbf{3:121}]
When thou didst set forth early from thy people to settle for the believers a camp to
fight;--- but God both hears and knows;---

\noindent[\textbf{3:122}]
when two companies of you were on the point of showing cowardice; but God was their
guardian, for on God surely the believers do rely.

\noindent[\textbf{3:123}]
Why! God gave you victory at Bedr when ye were in a poor way; fear God, then, haply
ye may give thanks.

\noindent[\textbf{3:124}]
When thou didst say unto the believers, 'Is it not enough for you that your Lord assists
you with three thousand of the angels sent down from on high?

\noindent[\textbf{3:125}]
Yea, if ye are patient and fear God, and they come upon you on a sudden, now, your Lord
will assist you with five thousand of His angels, (angels) of mark.

\noindent[\textbf{3:126}]
God only made this as glad tidings for you to comfort your hearts withal,--- for victory is
but from God, the mighty, the wise;---

\noindent[\textbf{3:127}]
to cut off the flank of those who misbelieve, or make them downcast, that they may retire
disappointed.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:128}]
Thou hast nothing to do with the affair at all, whether He turn towards them again or
punish them; for, verily, they are unjust.

\noindent[\textbf{3:129}]
God's is what is in the heavens and in the earth. He forgives whom He pleases, and
punishes whom He pleases; for God is forgiving and merciful.

\noindent[\textbf{3:130}]
O ye who believe! devour not usury doubly doubled, but fear God, perchance ye may be
prosperous;

\noindent[\textbf{3:131}]
fear the fire which is prepared for the unbelievers,

\noindent[\textbf{3:132}]
and obey God and His Apostle, perchance ye may get mercy.

\noindent[\textbf{3:133}]
And vie with one another for pardon from your Lord, and for Paradise, the breadth of
which is as the heaven and the earth, prepared for those who fear;---

\noindent[\textbf{3:134}]
for those who expend in alms, in prosperity and adversity, for those who repress their
rage, and those who pardon men; God loves the kind.

\noindent[\textbf{3:135}]
Those who when they do a crime, or wrong themselves, remember God, and ask
forgiveness for their sins,--- and who forgives sins save God?--- and do not persevere in what
they did, the while they know;---

\noindent[\textbf{3:136}]
these have their reward:--- pardon from their Lord, and gardens beneath which rivers
flow, dwelling therein for aye; for pleasant is the hire of those who act like this.

\noindent[\textbf{3:137}]
Incidents have passed before your time, go on then in the earth, and see what was the end
of those who called (the prophets) liars.

\noindent[\textbf{3:138}]
This is an explanation unto men, and a guidance and a warning unto those who fear.

\noindent[\textbf{3:139}]
Do not give way nor grieve, for ye shall have the upper hand if ye but be believers.

\noindent[\textbf{3:140}]
If a sore touch you, a sore like it has touched people: these are days which we make to
alternate amongst mankind that God may know who it is that believe, and may take from you
witnesses, for God loves not the unjust;

\noindent[\textbf{3:141}]
and that God may assay those who believe, and blot out the misbelievers.

\noindent[\textbf{3:142}]
Do ye think that ye can enter Paradise and God not know those of you who have fought
well, or know the patient?

\noindent[\textbf{3:143}]
Why, ye longed for death before ye met it! Now ye have looked upon it and ye halt!

\noindent[\textbf{3:144}]
Mohammed is but an apostle; apostles have passed away before his time; what if he die
or is killed, will ye retreat upon your heels? He who retreats upon his heels does no harm to God
at all; but God will recompense the thankful.

\noindent[\textbf{3:145}]
It is not for any soul to die, save by God's permission written down for an appointed
time; but he who wishes for the reward of this world we will give him of it, and he who wishes
for the reward of the future we will give him of it, and we will recompense the grateful.

\noindent[\textbf{3:146}]
How many prophets have myriads fought against! yet they did not give way at what befel
them in God's way Nor were they weak, nor did they demean themselves:--- God loves the
patient.

\noindent[\textbf{3:147}]
And their word was only to say, 'Lord, forgive us our sins and our extravagance in our
affairs; and make firm our footing, and help us against the misbelieving folk!'

\noindent[\textbf{3:148}]
and God gave them the reward of this world, and good reward for the future too, for God
doth love the kind.

\noindent[\textbf{3:149}]
O ye who believe! if ye obey those who misbelieve, they will turn you back upon your
heels, and ye will retreat the losers.

\noindent[\textbf{3:150}]
Nay, God is your Lord, He is the best of helpers.

\noindent[\textbf{3:151}]
We will throw dread into the hearts of those who misbelieve, for that they associate that
with God which He has sent down no power for; but their resort is fire, and evil is the resort of
the unjust.

\noindent[\textbf{3:152}]
God has truly kept His promise, when ye knocked them senseless by His permission,
until ye showed cowardice, and wrangled, and rebelled, after he had shown you what ye loved.
Amongst you are those who love this world, and amongst you are those who love the next. Then
He turned you away from them to try you; but He has pardoned you, for God is Lord of grace
unto believers,---

\noindent[\textbf{3:153}]
when ye went up and looked not round upon any one, although the Apostle was calling
you from your rear. Therefore did God reward you with trouble on trouble that ye should not
grieve after what ye had missed, nor for what befel you, for God is well aware of what ye do.

\noindent[\textbf{3:154}]
Then He sent down upon you after trouble safety,--- drowsiness creeping over one
company of you, and one company of you getting anxious about themselves, suspecting about
God other than the truth, with the suspicion of the ignorant, and saying, 'Have we any chance in
the affair?' Say, 'Verily, the affair is God's.' They conceal in themselves what they will not
show to thee, and say, 'If we had any chance in the affair we should not be killed here.' Say, 'If
ye were in your houses, surely those against whom slaughter was written down, would have gone
forth to fight even to where they are lying now; that God may try what is in your breasts and
assay what is in your hearts, for God doth know the nature of men's breasts.'

\noindent[\textbf{3:155}]
Verily, those of you who turned your backs on that day when the two armies met, it was
but Satan who made them slip for something they had earned. But God has now pardoned them;
verily, God is forgiving and clement.

\noindent[\textbf{3:156}]
O ye who believe! be not like those who misbelieve, and say unto their brethren when
they knock about in the earth, or are upon a raid, 'Had they but been at home, they had not died
and had not been killed.' It was that God might make a sighing in their hearts, for God gives life
and death; and God on what ye do doth look.

\noindent[\textbf{3:157}]
And if, indeed, ye be killed in God's way or die, surely forgiveness from God and mercy
is better than what ye gather;

\noindent[\textbf{3:158}]
and if ye die or be killed it is to God ye shall be assembled.

\noindent[\textbf{3:159}]
It was by a sort of mercy from God thou didst deal gently with them, for hadst thou been
rough and rude of heart they had dispersed from around thee. But pardon them, and ask
forgiveness for them, and take counsel with them in the affair. As for what thou hast resolved,
rely upon God; verily, God loves those who do rely.

\noindent[\textbf{3:160}]
If God help you, there is none can overcome you; but if He leave you in the lurch, who is
there can help you after Him? Upon God then let believers rely.

\noindent[\textbf{3:161}]
It is not for the prophet to cheat; and he who cheats shall bring what he has cheated on
the resurrection day. Then shall each soul be paid what it has earned, and they shall not be
wronged.

\noindent[\textbf{3:162}]
Is he who follows the pleasure of God, like him who has drawn on himself anger from
God, whose resort is hell? An evil journey shall it be!

\noindent[\textbf{3:163}]
